### [1] [Spin-glass order in a field requires magnetic-field chaos in XY gauge glasses for $d\leq 4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14109)
*Manaka Okuyama,Masayuki Ohzeki,Hidetoshi Nishimori*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在 $d\leq 4$ 的 XY 规范玻璃模型中，有限温度下沿固定方向、幅度按 Rayleigh 分布逐点涨落的磁场，要么在自旋玻璃相中诱导磁場混沌，要么完全破坏自旋玻璃序（只要零场下存在自旋玻璃相）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XY 规范玻璃模型被认为在 $d\geq 3$ 存在自旋玻璃相，但对该相在磁场下的行为（是否出现磁场混沌、序是否存活）缺乏严格结果；本文旨在给出有限维系统中自旋玻璃相性质的严格约束。

Method: 证明对任意固定非零场强，零场与非零场平衡自旋构型间平方重叠的无序平均在热力学极限下趋于零；经规范变换将重叠统计等价于处于高斯随机场中、具有关联键无序的 XY 规范玻璃的磁化统计；再将 Dario–Harel–Peled（基于 Imry–Ma 与 Aizenman–Wehr）关于连续自旋系统中随机场抑制铁磁序的定量估计推广到键依赖相互作用。

Result: 对 $d\leq 4$ 与任意有限温度，所施加的随机取向固定方向磁场要么使自旋玻璃相出现磁场混沌，要么消除自旋玻璃序；若如 RSB 图像所预言自旋玻璃相在该场下存续，则必然呈现磁场混沌。

Conclusion: 该结果为有限维自旋玻璃相性质提供了罕见的严格约束，但未判定自旋玻璃序在磁场中是否存活，因而无法区分 droplet 与 RSB 图像；严格均匀场的情形仍未解决。

Abstract: The XY gauge-glass model is believed to exhibit a spin-glass phase in dimensions $d\geq 3$. In this paper, we prove that, in dimensions $d\leq 4$ and at any finite temperature, a magnetic field applied in a fixed direction, with amplitudes that fluctuate from site to site according to the Rayleigh distribution, must either induce magnetic-field chaos in the spin-glass phase or destroy spin-glass order altogether, whenever a spin-glass phase exists at zero field. In particular, if a spin-glass phase persists under such a field, as predicted by the replica-symmetry-breaking (RSB) picture, it must exhibit magnetic-field chaos. This conclusion follows from our proof that, for any fixed nonzero field strength, the disorder average of the squared overlap between equilibrium spin configurations at zero field and those at nonzero field vanishes in the thermodynamic limit. By gauge transformations, the overlap statistics are identical to the magnetization statistics of an XY gauge glass with correlated bond disorder in a Gaussian random field. Our proof combines this exact identity with quantitative estimates on the suppression of ferromagnetic order by random fields in continuous-spin systems, due to Dario, Harel, and Peled [Commun. Math. Phys. 405, 212 (2024)] and building on the work of Imry and Ma [Phys. Rev. Lett. 35, 1399 (1975)] and of Aizenman and Wehr [Phys. Rev. Lett. 62, 2503 (1989); Commun. Math. Phys. 130, 489 (1990)]. We extend these estimates to bond-dependent interactions. Although our result provides a rare rigorous constraint on the nature of spin-glass phases in finite-dimensional systems, it does not determine whether spin-glass order survives in a field, and therefore does not distinguish between the droplet and RSB pictures. The case of a strictly uniform field remains open.

</details>


### [2] [Disorder-Induced Logarithmic Spreading and Localization of Quantum Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14200)
*Ang Yang,Jingxuan Zhang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在弱相互作用无序玻色系统中，量子涨落不呈弹道式扩散，而是在中等时间尺度上呈对数式扩散并最终饱和；扩散速率随无序强度按幂律下降，机制源于局域化平均场波函数作为含时配对背景，其对距离指数衰减的涨落产生率导致对数前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序可使量子粒子局域化（即使存在相互作用），但其对超越平均场动力学的量子涨落的影响尚不清楚，本文旨在厘清无序如何调控量子涨落的传播。

Method: 对比Bose–Hubbard模型与Gross–Pitaevskii方程，研究弱相互作用无序玻色系统中量子涨落的传播；同时考察随机无序与准周期无序，改变粒子数与维度（含二维）；并采用含时Bogoliubov–de Gennes框架分析涨落产生机制。

Result: 无无序时为弹道扩散；在随机与准周期无序下，中等时间呈对数扩散随后饱和；扩散速率随无序强度增加按幂律下降，且在粒子数和维度变化下保持稳健；含时BdG分析表明局域化平均场波函数充当含时配对背景，涨落产生率随距离指数抑制，解释了对数前沿。

Conclusion: 揭示了无序调控量子涨落动力学的新区域，确立无序可作为控制量子涨落传播的手段。

Abstract: Disorder can localize quantum particles, even in the presence of interactions, but its influence on quantum fluctuations beyond mean--field dynamics remains poorly understood. Here, we investigate the propagation of quantum fluctuations in weakly interacting disordered bosonic systems by comparing the Bose--Hubbard model with the Gross--Pitaevskii equation. We find that, in contrast to ballistic spreading in the disorder--free system, quantum fluctuations exhibit logarithmic spreading at intermediate times under both random and quasiperiodic disorder, before eventually saturating. The spreading rate decreases algebraically with increasing disorder strength, and this behavior persists across different particle numbers and extends to two dimensions. Using a time--dependent Bogoliubov--de Gennes framework, we identify the localized mean--field wavefunction as a time--dependent pairing background that generates quantum fluctuations. The exponential suppression of fluctuation--generation rates with distance provides a physical mechanism for the logarithmically advancing fluctuation front. Our results reveal a distinct regime of disorder--controlled quantum--fluctuation dynamics and establish disorder as a means of controlling their spreading.

</details>

### [3] [Borrowed Planckian dissipation in magic-angle twisted graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12947)
*Dino Škrgić,Dejan Stojkovic*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 魔角扭转三层石墨烯中，位移场D通过"借用"平带的普朗克自能来调控输运，使电阻率斜率随a(1+D²/D₀²)增长，且场差熵比值被预言为2π³/(21ζ(3))≈2.46，提供以D为步长测熵的判别性实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异金属中线性电阻率系数与对数比热系数之比不含电子-散射体耦合，但现有材料中该耦合由化学固定、无法调制。本文寻找一个可变"旋钮"来检验这一普适性，并区分耗散由平带自身电子还是被"借用"所致。

Method: 以魔角扭转三层石墨烯为平台，用破坏镜面对称的位移场D将轻Dirac锥与平带分离，Dirac载流子以∝(D²+D₀²)的权重借用平带的普朗克自能；在八个场值下测电阻曲线，并结合热平均Kubo公式与数值搜索分析输运与熵。

Result: 八个场的电阻曲线表明场作用于散射率而非载流子数；温度斜率遵循a(1+D²/D₀²)且不含耗尽Dirac口袋带来的四次项；0.20–0.30 V/nm间弹性部分变化小于5%，非弹性部分至0.38 V/nm增长2.5倍；Kubo公式偏好关于费米能对称分布的平带权重；场差熵比在共形平带下为2π³/(21ζ(3))≈2.46，在无高能速率下限的单调浴谱中数值搜索上界为2.73。

Conclusion: 借用耗散保持平带浴指数与输运前因子，但当浴自身有动力学时每单位借用耦合携带恰好两倍熵，从而给出可判别的熵比值；关键验证是以位移场D为步长测量熵。

Abstract: In strange metals, the ratio of the coefficients producing signature linear-in-$T$ resistivity and the logarithmic specific heat is free of the coupling between the electrons and whatever scatters them. In the materials studied so far that coupling is fixed by chemistry. Magic-angle twisted trilayer graphene provides a dial with a displacement field $D$ that breaks the mirror symmetry separating a light Dirac cone from the flat bands, and the Dirac carriers borrow the Planckian self-energy of the flat band with a weight proportional to $D^2+D_0^2$. Resistance curves taken at eight fields are consistent with the field acting on the scattering rate rather than on the number of carriers. The slope in temperature follows $a(1+D^2/D_0^2)$ without the quartic term that a depleting Dirac pocket adds, and the elastic part varies by less than 5\% between 0.20 and 0.30~V/nm while the inelastic part grows 2.5-fold up to 0.38~V/nm. The thermally averaged Kubo formula favors flat-band weight placed symmetrically about the Fermi level over a single level. Borrowed dissipation keeps the bath exponent and transport prefactor of the flat band's own electrons but carries exactly twice their entropy per unit of borrowed coupling whenever the bath has dynamics of its own, so the field-differential ratio is $2\pi^3/21\zeta(3)\approx2.46$ for a conformal flat band and, in a numerical search, at most 2.73 for monotonic bath spectra without a high-energy rate floor. The discriminating test is the entropy measured in steps of $D$.

</details>


### [4] [Hidden Spin-Supercurrent Counterflow in Conventional Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13076)
*Johanne Bratland Tjernshaugen,Jacob Linder*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在自旋劈裂场下的常规超导体中，磁场诱导的自旋混合界面可激活自旋超流分量，使电阻性自旋流在空间变化时总自旋流仍守恒，形成总自旋流与电荷流均为零的"隐藏自旋超流逆流"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，电阻性自旋流的空间衰减归因于自旋弛豫或自旋转移力矩（通过去除角动量）。作者希望寻找一种定性不同的机制：不依赖角动量耗散也能让电阻性自旋流空间变化，从而揭示凝聚体介导的自旋流补偿这一被忽视的替代途径。

Method: 理论分析：考虑处于自旋劈裂场中的常规超导体，并在自旋混合界面处进行自旋注入。将总自旋流分解为电阻性（准粒子）分量与凝聚体（自旋超流）分量，通过守恒方程分析二者的空间分布及其对参数（失配程度、参数区间）的依赖；并设计通过自旋-电荷转换（自旋霍尔注入体的电学响应、局域准粒子逆自旋霍尔信号）探测该效应的方案。

Result: 自旋注入激活了自旋超流贡献，使电阻性自旋流可空间变化而总自旋流守恒。即使超导体上没有净自旋偏压，也出现有限且方向相反的电阻性自旋流与凝聚体自旋流，形成总自旋流与总电荷流均为零的隐藏自旋超流逆流。该机制不要求精确的补偿条件，在较宽参数范围内仍保持可观幅度。

Conclusion: 凝聚体介导的自旋流补偿可作为自旋弛豫之外的一种此前被忽视的机制，用以解释（并可实验探测）超导体中无耗散的自旋流空间调制；可通过自旋-电荷转换在自旋霍尔注入体电学响应或局域准粒子逆自旋霍尔信号中加以验证。

Abstract: Resistive spin currents conventionally vary in space through spin relaxation or torque, which removes angular momentum from the transported spin. We here demonstrate a qualitatively different mechanism in a conventional superconductor exposed to a spin-splitting field. Spin injection across a spin-mixing interface activates a spin-supercurrent contribution, allowing the resistive spin current to vary spatially while the total spin current remains conserved. Even without a net spin-bias across the superconductor, finite and oppositely directed resistive and condensate spin currents arise. This produces a hidden spin-supercurrent counterflow with vanishing total spin and charge currents. The mechanism persists away from exact compensation and remains sizable over broad parameter ranges. The counterflow may be accessible through spin-to-charge conversion, either as a modification of the electrical response of spin-Hall injectors or through a local quasiparticle inverse spin-Hall signal. Our results establish condensate-mediated spin-current compensation as a previously overlooked alternative to spin relaxation.

</details>


### [5] [Neutron Scattering studies of the density dependence of One-dimensional Quantum Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13235)
*Sutirtha Paul,Rahul Soni,Garfield Warren,Adrian Del Maestro,Paul E. Sokol*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过中子散射研究模板多孔材料中受限⁴He一维核在不同填充率下的行为，发现一维液体的密度始终固定不随填充率改变，归因于一维液体长度增长而密度不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已表明，预镀惰性气体以增强维度还原的模板多孔材料中受限⁴He呈现一维行为，其激发谱与二维、三维超流氦在性质上不同，并与量子蒙特卡罗计算一致（后者预言形成三层固体、孔中心为一维液体）。本文旨在进一步考察一维核填充率变化时该体系的激发行为。

Method: 对填充率系统变化的一维核受限⁴He体系开展中子散射测量，比较不同孔填充度下的激发谱特征。

Result: 即便增加孔内填充量，一维液体也总是以固定密度形成，其密度不随填充率变化。

Conclusion: 一维液体密度恒定的原因在于：随着填充率提高，液体的一维延伸长度增长，而密度保持不变。

Abstract: In previous work, we demonstrated that $^4$He confined within a templated porous material preplated with a noble gas to enhance dimensional reduction exhibits one-dimensional behavior. Neutron scattering revealed that the excitations in confined $^4$He are qualitatively distinct from those in two- and three-dimensional superfluid helium, aligning with quantum Monte Carlo calculations. These simulations suggest that three solid layers form, with a one-dimensional liquid located at the center of the pore. Here, we present neutron scattering results as the filling fraction of the one-dimensional core is varied. We observe that the one-dimensional liquid always forms with a fixed density, even as the pore filling increases. We posit that this is due to the growth of the one dimensional extent of the liquid as the filling is increased, but its density remains unchanged.

</details>


### [6] [Optically modulated field-dependent electrical response in a non-collinear antiferromagnet/ferromagnet heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13351)
*Adrielson Dias,André José,José Lucas,Rodrigo Cristian,José Holanda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究MgO/IrMn₃/Py/Au异质结构在射频激励、白光照射与直流偏置联合作用下的磁场依赖电学响应，实现光-电-磁多场调制，电压响应可由数十纳伏提升至约1.8 μV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光、电、磁多场协同调控有望拓展自旋电子异质结的功能响应，但基于非共线反铁磁体与铁磁层复合结构的多场耦合电学行为尚不清晰，需要实验探究其可控调制机制。

Method: 制备MgO/IrMn₃/Py/Au异质结，在20 MHz、标称2 W射频激励下测量磁场依赖电压响应，分别与同时施加约1 W白光照射和1 mA直流偏置；辅以变温磁化测量（交换偏置、阻塞温度）及磁场取向依赖的电阻测量。

Result: 暗态下射频激励电压仅数十纳伏；白光照射后在315–320 Oe附近出现显著磁场依赖特征，达0.9–1.0 μV，光照引起的差分电压约0.95 μV；1 mA直流单独产生约1 μV响应；射频、直流与光同时激励时电压升至约1.8 μV。磁表征显示阻塞温度约380 K的交换偏置，电阻对磁场取向呈显著各向异性依赖。

Conclusion: 磁场依赖电学响应可通过光学与电学激励协同调制，涉及磁光相互作用与自旋依赖输运，可能包含自旋霍尔及磁自旋霍尔效应贡献，为非共线反铁磁/铁磁异质结的多场耦合调控提供了实验依据。

Abstract: The interplay among optical, electrical, and magnetic stimuli offers opportunities to control the functional response of spintronic heterostructures. Here, we investigate the field-dependent electrical response of MgO/IrMn$_3$/Py/Au under radio-frequency (RF) excitation, white-light illumination, and direct-current (DC) bias. Under a 20 MHz RF signal with a nominal power of 2 W, the measured voltage in the dark remains at several tens of nanovolts. Upon illumination with approximately 1 W of white light, a pronounced field-dependent feature emerges around 315--320 Oe, reaching 0.9--1.0 $\mu$V. The illumination-associated differential voltage is approximately 0.95 $\mu$V. A DC current of 1 mA produces a response of approximately 1 $\mu$V, while the simultaneous application of RF, DC, and optical excitation increases the voltage to approximately 1.8 $\mu$V. Magnetic characterization reveals exchange bias with a blocking temperature near 380 K, while electrical resistance measurements show a pronounced dependence on magnetic-field orientation, consistent with the anisotropic response of the heterostructure. These results demonstrate that the field-dependent electrical response can be modulated through combined optical and electrical excitation, involving magneto-optical interactions and spin-dependent transport phenomena, potentially including spin Hall-related contributions and the magnetic spin Hall effect. This work provides insights into coupled optical, electrical, and magnetic-field effects in heterostructures based on non-collinear antiferromagnets and ferromagnetic layers.

</details>


### [7] [Geometry-Selective Higher-order topological insulators induced by $d_{xy}$ altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13438)
*Siyuan Cheng,Yuee Xie,Xiaosen Yang,Yuanping Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过近邻耦合$d_{xy}$波交错磁体，可实现边界几何依赖的高阶拓扑相：轴对齐边界无能隙边缘态，对角边界获取符号交替的有效质量并产生四个角模；$\pi$磁通缺陷给出零能束缚态佐证；Zeeman场与交错磁序协同驱动边缘能隙闭合-重开而体隙保持，实现拓扑相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种便捷的途径来工程化高阶拓扑相，并厘清边界几何在高阶拓扑中的决定性作用——既有方案多忽视边界取向对边缘态质量的调控能力。

Method: 将体系与$d_{xy}$波交错磁体近邻耦合；按不同边界几何投影到边缘态子空间，推导有效边缘哈密顿量；在周期边界下引入$\pi$磁通缺陷，检验通量芯处的零能束缚态；分析Zeeman场与交错磁序竞争下的边缘能隙演化。

Result: 轴对齐边界边缘态保持无能隙；对角边界获得符号交替的有效质量，形成质量畴壁并局域出四个角模；$\pi$通量芯出现零能束缚态，在无物理边界条件下独立证实非平凡拓扑；Zeeman场与交错磁序驱动边缘能隙闭合再重开（体隙始终打开），对应拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 边界几何是高阶拓扑相的关键调控自由度；近邻诱导的$d_{xy}$波交错磁体可作为工程化高阶拓扑相的有效平台。

Abstract: We show that proximity to a $d_{xy}$-wave altermagnet provides an effective route to engineering boundary-geometry-dependent higher-order topological phases. The boundary geometry plays a crucial role in determining the topological properties: axis-aligned edge states remain gapless, whereas diagonal edges acquire effective masses with alternating signs, giving rise to four corner modes localized at the mass domain walls. By projecting onto the edge-state subspace, we derive the effective edge Hamiltonians for different boundary geometries. Furthermore, $\pi$-flux defects introduced under periodic boundary conditions yield zero-energy bound states localized at the flux cores, providing complementary evidence for nontrivial topology without physical boundaries. The interplay between the Zeeman field and the altermagnetic order drives a topological transition through the closing and reopening of the edge gap, while the bulk gap remains open. These findings highlight the crucial role of boundary geometry in higher-order topology and establish proximity-induced $d_{xy}$-wave altermagnetism as a route to engineering higher-order topological phases.

</details>

Abstract: The quantum anomalous Hall (QAH) effect is usually realized through ferromagnetism or magnetic dop ing, which limits material choices and often results in low mobility, disorder sensitivity, and low operating temperatures. The altermagnetic order selectively gaps the Dirac cone at the Z point, while hybridization between the top and bottom surfaces of the WTI thin film opens a trivial gap at the point, generating a Chern number C = 1 phase with a single chiral edge channel. We verify the topological transition through both a minimal thin-film model and a slab geometry, and compute the associated Hall response. As altermagnets and layered WTIs already exist experimentally, this mechanism offers a promising and experimentally accessible route to realizing QAH phases without magnetic doping or global magnetization. The results indicate that altermagnetic surface engineering may provide a clean and controllable pathway for realizing dissipationless edge transport in layered quantum materials.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典可积系统中的周期轨道通常只用能量、周期等动力学量描述，缺乏拓扑刻画；而拓扑能带理论主要针对线性晶格模型。作者希望建立二者之间的桥梁，用Lax对/手征对称紧束缚模型的拓扑不变量来分类非线性周期轨道，并解释轨道结构改变时拓扑如何变化。

Method: 采用经典可积系统的Lax表述：在频率基下沿周期轨道展开手征对称Lax算符，得到紧束缚模型。由能量守恒推出两条能带精确平直；谐振子对应完全二聚化的SSH模型，非线性通过高次谐波产生长程跃迁。对对称双势阱，计算周期轨道在阱内与跨阱时的缠绕数变化，解析求得半无限链边缘态及局域长度随可分界面（separatrix）的对数发散。进一步对Lax算符作正则量子化，将手征缠绕数与超对称量子力学联系；并通过行波约化推广到周期非线性波与孤子极限。

Conclusion: Lax表述为经典非线性周期轨道提供了拓扑分类框架：轨道拓扑（缠绕数）由频率空间紧束缚模型的手征结构决定，其改变由可分界面的奇异极限实现；经典结果经量子化后与超对称指标一致，并可推广至非线性波。该工作把可积动力学、拓扑能带论与超对称量子力学统一起来。

Abstract: We use the Lax formulation of classical integrable systems to characterize the topology of nonlinear periodic orbits. For a Hamiltonian system with one degree of freedom, we express the chiral-symmetric Lax operator along a periodic orbit in a frequency basis. Energy conservation makes both bands of the resulting tight-binding model exactly flat. The harmonic oscillator corresponds to a fully dimerized topological Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model, while nonlinearity generates long-range hopping through higher harmonics. In the symmetric double well, the winding number changes as intrawell periodic orbits give way to an orbit spanning both wells, and the zero-energy edge mode of the semi-infinite chain disappears. We derive the edge-mode profile and show that its localization length diverges logarithmically as the separatrix is approached. At the separatrix, the period and the hopping decay length diverge while the band gap remains finite; this singular limit of the periodic motion allows the winding number of the infinite frequency chain to change. Canonical quantization of the Lax operator also connects the chiral winding numbers to supersymmetric quantum mechanics: for a bound system on the real line with simple turning points, their sum over all periodic orbits at a common positive energy equals the Witten index. We also apply the construction to periodic nonlinear waves through traveling-wave reductions. The finite-period winding number becomes undefined in their solitary-wave limits.

</details>

Abstract: The effective interlayer tunneling (EIT) is a key quantity in determining the out-of-phase plasmons of a bilayer structure. Using the tight-binding model and the random phase approximation, we investigate plasmons in bilayer graphene with O-type Kekulé distortion. Both O/O and G/O bilayers exhibit strong electron-hole asymmetry in the EIT. For the O/O bilayer, the gap of the out-of-phase plasmon remains almost constant as the electron concentration varies, whereas it increases rapidly with increasing hole concentration; hole doping can also lead to stronger anomalous dispersion than electron doping. For the G/O bilayer, enhancing the Kekulé distortion $\Delta_0$ can weaken the EIT, as reflected by the reduced interlayer hybridization caused by the lifted layer degeneracy, especially for the electron-doped case. For the electron-doped G/O bilayer, the EIT is almost turned off when $\Delta_0=0.3$ from the perspective of the dispersion. Our results imply that switching the doping type between electron and hole can tune the performance of out-of-phase plasmon-based devices by modulating the EIT.

</details>

*Debashish Mondal,Arnob Kumar Ghosh,Arijit Saha,Tanay Nag*

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了从一阶到三阶拓扑超导体中马约拉纳零模空间维度递减的图景，并论证周期驱动（Floquet工程）可在静态拓扑平庸体系中实现这些相，同时讨论了Floquet高阶拓扑超导相的实现方案、拓扑不变量，以及区分常规马约拉纳零模与反常马约拉纳π模的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算发展依赖马约拉纳费米子及其拓扑保护，拓扑超导体是研究马约拉纳模与拓扑量子计算的理想平台。在维度 d>1 时，高阶拓扑超导体可将马约拉纳模的空间局域化控制推广到超越一阶拓扑超导的传统框架；同时周期驱动有望在静态拓扑平庸体系中"无中生有"地产生这些相，因此需要系统梳理该领域并厘清不同阶拓扑超导、Floquet实现及拓扑不变量刻画的问题。

Method: 综述性方法：系统梳理从一阶（FOTSC）到三阶（TOTSC）拓扑超导相，按马约拉纳零模空间维度逐步降低的线索组织；引入周期驱动协议进行Floquet工程设计；分析不同驱动方案，以及自旋轨道耦合、磁场、磁性杂质与对称破缺质量项之间的相互作用，以构造具有相应拓扑不变量的Floquet高阶拓扑超导（FHOTSC）相；并广泛讨论可区分常规马约拉纳零模与反常马约拉纳π模的拓扑不变量。

Result: 论证周期驱动可使原本静态拓扑平庸的系统实现一阶至三阶各类拓扑超导相；给出实现FHOTSC相的多种驱动方案及自旋轨道耦合、磁场、磁性杂质和对称破缺质量项的协同机制，并用合适的拓扑不变量刻画这些相；系统讨论了区分零能马约拉纳模与出现在两个不同准能处的反常马约拉纳π模的拓扑不变量。

Conclusion: 拓扑超导从一阶到三阶构成一个马约拉纳模局域维度逐级降低的统一图景；Floquet工程可突破静态拓扑约束，成为实现乃至调控高阶拓扑超导相的有力手段；拓扑不变量是区分常规马约拉纳零模与无静态对应的反常π模的关键工具，对拓扑量子计算具有基础意义。

Abstract: The current developments in quantum technologies are deeply rooted in quantum computation, with Majorana fermions and their topological protection expected to play a central role. In this context, topological superconductors (TSCs) provide a promising platform for investigating Majorana modes and their potential applications in topological quantum computation. In dimensions $d > 1$, the spatial localization of topological Majorana modes can be controlled in higher-order topological superconductors (HOTSCs), extending beyond the conventional framework of first-order topological superconductors (FOTSCs). In this review, we systematically survey the diverse landscape of topological superconductivity, ranging from FOTSCs to third-order topological superconductors (TOTSCs), in which the spatial dimensionality of Majorana zero modes (MZMs) is progressively reduced. We demonstrate how periodic driving protocols enable Floquet engineering of all these phases, even when the corresponding undriven systems are topologically trivial. Furthermore, we investigate various driving schemes and explore the interplay among spin-orbit coupling, magnetic fields, magnetic impurities, and symmetry-breaking mass terms in realizing Floquet higher-order topological superconducting (FHOTSC) phases, characterized by appropriate topological invariants. We also provide an extensive discussion of topological invariants capable of distinguishing conventional MZMs from anomalous Majorana $pi$-modes, which emerge at two distinct quasienergies and have no static counterparts.

</details>

Motivation: 以往研究多将拓扑保护视为单一对称性约束下的二元性质，缺乏对多对称性及其约化形式如何分层、互补地保护边缘态的刻画；本文旨在建立统一的层级框架，厘清不同对称性在保护边缘零模中的角色差异。

Abstract: We reveal a hierarchy of topological protection in which multiple symmetries and their reduced forms provide distinct mechanisms for protecting topological edge states. In Hermitian Hamiltonians obtained by doubling chiral non-Hermitian systems, a structural and an inherited chiral symmetry provide complementary channels of topological protection. By selectively breaking either symmetry, we demonstrate their distinct roles in protecting edge zero modes, establishing different levels of protection under symmetry reduction. Remarkably, even when both full chiral symmetries are broken, fixed subsymmetry constraints allow the protected zero-mode configurations to switch between opposite edges and change in number across the topological phase boundaries of the symmetry-unbroken system. Our results establish a hierarchical framework for topological protection, revealing a route to controlling edge-state configurations via reduced symmetry constraints.

</details>

*Hanchen Wang,Tao Yu*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子Kerr效应（色散频率随磁振子布居变化）是非线性与量子磁子学的关键功能，其强度与符号取决于磁各向异性、偏置与模式几何，缺乏灵活的外场调控手段。作者希望借助超导体的磁屏蔽特性提供一种可调、可区分的调控途径。

Abstract: Magnon Kerr interaction showing a dependence of magnon dispersion frequency on its population is a useful functionality for nonlinear and quantum magnonics; its strength and sign depend on magnetic anisotropy, bias, and mode geometry. We here propose to use a superconducting gate to tune the Kerr interaction of magnons in ferromagnetic insulators. Even when the superconductor responds linearly, the frequency shift induced by its reflected dipolar field depends on the longitudinal magnetization. The decrease of this magnetization with increasing precession amplitude therefore produces a Kerr contribution. This superflow-induced pair breaking adds a second Kerr contribution, controlled by the interfacial superflow relative to the nonlinear screening scale. Abstract: An anomalous Hall crystal (AHC) is a quantum state of matter, in which spontaneous electron crystallization leads to a quantized Hall effect without a magnetic field. The state can emerge in materials with strong Coulomb interaction and large Berry curvatures, such as few-layer rhombohedral graphene. Although the idea of AHCs has been invoked to explain some of the observed Chern insulators in moiré rhombohedral graphene (mRG), direct experimental evidence of AHCs has been missing so far. Here, we report experimental evidence of AHCs in hole-doped mRG through combined electrical transport and noise measurements. We identify a series of distinct Chern insulators spanning a broad and continuous range of doping densities incommensurate with the underlying moiré lattice; the states exhibit current-induced broadband noise and memory effects as well as pronounced gate hysteresis. Systematic magnetic field, temperature and gate dependence studies further support the emergence of a spontaneously charge-ordered state that acts as a parental state for the observed Chern insulators. The absence of Chern insulators at nearby integer fillings also distinguishes the observed state from the reported reentrant or extended quantum (anomalous) Hall states. Altogether, our results establish experimental evidence of AHCs in mRG and open the door for further exploration of the collective dynamics in this new state of matter.

Coupling these directional channels to a $d$-wave altermagnet yields a spin-dependent interference contrast. Hybridized end states along a stepped termination provide multiple tunneling resonances. Our results establish end-state-mediated tunneling as a transport mechanism for STM with strongly confined topological tips.

Abstract: The zero-point energy of an $LC$ circuit measures the polarization fluctuation of an insulator coupled to it. In a crystal this fluctuation is the bulk quantum weight $K_{\rm bulk}$, which the Chern number $C$ bounds below by $|C|$. We show that a finite Chern flake adds an edge term that survives as the flake grows. The low-frequency transitions of its chiral edge have squared dipole matrix elements that sum to an edge weight $K_{\rm edge}\ge |C|$ per unit area, a floor that follows from the positive absorption of both circular polarizations and the known cancellation of circular dichroism between edge and bulk. At weak coupling, which a growing flake eventually leaves, a circuit slower than the edge modes reads $K_{\rm bulk}+K_{\rm edge}$, averaged over two field directions, and a faster one approaches $K_{\rm bulk}$, so the reading depends on whether the flake is made large or the circuit slow first. In exact free-fermion calculations on lattice flakes with $|C|=1$ and 2 the summed circular dichroism of the edge sector extrapolates to $|C|$ in magnitude, so that the floor is $|C|$. Above the floor, each edge channel adds a number fixed by the boundary shape and its edge velocities, which square and rectangular lattice flakes with one channel follow to within 0.003, while the circular dichroism counts only the net chirality. The avoided crossing of the circuit with the edge mode gives the edge weight in units of the Hall conductance quantum, up to a geometric factor of the capacitor.

Method: 以Co/Pt基异质结为研究对象，系统梳理BLS与MOKE两种iDMI测量技术的测量流程、数据评估与不确定度分析，给出完整的方法路线图；重点分析用于提取D的磁性参数所起的关键作用及其不确定度传播，并建立测量模型以提高测量可靠性。

Result: 明确了磁性参数及其不确定度传播对D提取结果的主导影响，并表明合理的测量模型可显著提升测量可靠性；形成了一套涵盖测量流程、数据评估与不确定度分析的完整方法框架。

Conclusion: 所提方法学为提升iDMI测量在不同实验室与不同实验技术间的一致性与可重复性提供了实用框架，可作为该领域标准化测量的参考规范。

Abstract: The interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (iDMI) is a key ingredient for stabilizing chiral magnetic states, such as skyrmions and chiral domain walls, which are key building blocks for next-generation spintronic technologies. Despite its importance, the quantitative determination of the iDMI constant, $D$, is often plagued by significant discrepancies. This paper establishes a set of good practices for measuring iDMI using two primary techniques: Brillouin Light Scattering (BLS) and the Magneto-Optical Kerr Effect (MOKE). Through the analysis of Co/Pt-based heterostructures, this work provides a comprehensive roadmap for measurement procedures, data evaluation, and uncertainty analysis. Our analysis highlights the crucial role of magnetic parameters used to extract the iDMI constant and propagation of their uncertainties, and the measurement model for enhancing measurement reliability. The proposed methodology provides a practical framework for improving the consistency and reproducibility of iDMI measurements across different laboratories and experimental techniques.

</details>


### [20] [Tuning and Mapping of Two-Tone Optomechanical Instability up to the Physical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14427)
*Marco Dicosta,Alexandre Delattre,Louise Banniard,Laure Mercier de Lépinay,Andrew Fefferman,Eddy Collin*

TL;DR: 本文建立了描述双色驱动光机械"反作用规避"实验中双色不稳定性的完整理论，涵盖所有失谐、功率失衡与器件非理想因素，并在超高协同度（近100）实验中得到验证，同时揭示了腔频噪声为限制因素并发现高功率下的机械梳状自驱动新态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反作用规避（back-action-evading）实验是实现量子极限测量等的关键方案，但其固有的双色不稳定性此前缺乏完整理论描述，尤其是功率失衡、有限机械品质因子与边带分辨度等实际条件的影响未被系统刻画。

Method: 构建双色驱动光机械系统的完整不稳定性理论，纳入任意失谐、两泵浦功率失衡、有限机械品质因子及边带分辨度；提出并实施精细调谐（fine-tuning）流程，使实验协同度提升至接近100，并将模型与实验数据直接对比。

Result: 理论与实验符合度很高；功率失谐与边带分辨度对定位反作用规避"甜点"尤为关键；实验的限制因素被确认为腔频噪声；在极高驱动功率下观测到具有机械梳状谱特征的新自驱动态，表现为两个驱动响应之间的同步被非线性效应破坏。

Conclusion: 该工作为反作用规避及其他双色方案实验提供了完整且经高协同度验证的理论框架与调谐方法，并指出腔频噪声是当前性能瓶颈，高功率下的梳状自驱动态为后续研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We report on the compete theory describing the two-tone instability proper to optomechanical back-action-evading experiments. We address all possible mistunings (including power imbalance of the two pumps), as well as the device imperfections (finite mechanical quality factor, and sideband resolution). We illustrate the importance of power mistuning and sideband resolution, the latter being particularly relevant in finding the back-action-evading "sweet spot". The model is compared to experiments performed up to an extremely high cooperativity, of almost a 100. This has been made possible thanks to a fine-tuning procedure, which is presented. The agreement theory-experiment is very good, and the limiting factor is unraveled: cavity frequency noise. Besides, we discover at very high drive powers new self-driven states, which all share the characteristic feature of presenting a mechanical comb spectrum; as if the synchronization of the responses to the two drives (which occurs at lower powers) was destroyed by nonlinear effects. We believe that our work is extremely relevant to the implementation of back-action-evading experiments, as well as other related two-tone schemes.

</details>

### [21] [Universal Fold Caustics in Anharmonic Matter-wave Lenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13032)

TL;DR: 本文在理论上研究了短时非谐透镜聚焦的BEC物质波中的焦散现象：三次像差将谐波焦点变为折叠焦散，局部波场由Airy积分描述；GP模拟表明平均场相互作用改变近焦密度与纵向动量分布并产生非相互作用情形下不存在的次级动量特征；用经典Fisher信息与最大似然估计量化该1D分布携带的相互作用强度信息，10^5个探测点可达相对不确定度2.7×10^-3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 焦散提供了经典光线聚焦与结构波图样之间的普适联系，但这一联系在存在相互作用的非线性物质波中是否成立、相互作用如何破坏单粒子焦散普适性尚不清楚；同时希望把物质波透镜发展为探测平均场相互作用的灵敏工具。

Method: 对BEC施加短时非谐透镜；用半经典（程函/射线）描述分析三次像差导致的折叠焦散，并以Airy正则积分给出局部波场；开展三维Gross-Pitaevskii数值模拟比较有/无相互作用情形；用经典Fisher信息与最大似然估计评估由一维（沿z）密度分布反演有效相互作用强度的精度。

Result: 半经典分析将谐波焦点转变为折叠焦散；GP模拟显示平均场相互作用修正近焦密度与纵向动量分布，并产生非相互作用演化中不存在的次级动量空间特征；Fisher信息分析给出10^5个沿z探测点下有效相互作用强度的相对不确定度为2.7×10^-3。

Conclusion: 研究建立了刻画相互作用驱动偏离单粒子焦散普适性的框架，并表明物质波透镜可作为平均场相互作用的灵敏探针，为基于焦散的相互作用测量提供定量精度基准。

Abstract: Caustics provide a universal connection between classical ray focusing and structured wave patterns. We theoretically investigate this connection in non-linear matter waves focused by a short-duration anharmonic lens acting on a Bose-Einstein condensate. A semi-classical description shows how the leading cubic aberration transforms the harmonic focal point into a fold caustic, whose local wave field is described by an Airy canonical integral. Three-dimensional Gross-Pitaevskii simulations show that mean-field interactions modify the near-focus density and longitudinal momentum distribution, producing a secondary momentum-space feature absent from the non-interacting evolution. We quantify the information about the effective interaction strength carried by the $1D$ distribution using classical Fisher information and maximum-likelihood estimation. We obtain a relative uncertainty of $2.7\times10^{-3}$ for a sample of $10^5$ detected points along $z$. These results establish a framework for studying interaction-driven departures from single-particle caustic universality and promote matter-wave lenses as a sensitive probe of mean-field interactions.

</details>


### [22] [Pair localization in one-dimensional quasicrystals with spin-dependent hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13553)
*Carlos Rosas,U. A. Díaz-Reynoso,Francisco Mireles,G. A. Domínguez-Castro*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

  <summary>Details</summary>

The localization threshold nevertheless remains bounded above by the Aubry-André critical point of the slower constituent. The dynamics of an initially on-site pair provides signatures of delocalization induced by unequal hopping amplitudes. In particular, the effective spreading exponent of the root-mean-square width of a pair distribution reveals a progression from localized to subdiffusive and eventually superdiffusive behavior as the hopping ratio increases. Our results establish unequal hopping amplitudes as a means of tuning the transport properties of interacting pairs in quasiperiodic lattices, with direct relevance to ultracold atoms in optical lattices.

Abstract: We investigate non-Hermitian Josephson dynamics in an atomtronic quantum interference device (AQUID), formed by a ring Bose Einstein condensate with balanced atomic gain and loss regions and two moving optical barriers. The balanced gain and loss profile establishes a parity time symmetric potential while breaking spatial inversion symmetry, providing the essential ingredients for nonreciprocal Josephson transport. The dynamics are monitored nondestructively through the phase quadrature spectrum of an optomechanical cavity driven by Laguerre Gaussian beams coupled to the condensate. We demonstrate that increasing the local gain and loss strength softens the parity time symmetric collective mode towards an exceptional point, as verified by Bogoliubov de Gennes analysis. The associated coherent population exchange produces an additional characteristic splitting in the cavity output spectrum. For linearly translating barriers, non-Hermiticity provides an additional control parameter for tuning the critical current. When the barriers undergo simultaneous translation and rotation, the rotation induced phase modifies the interference between counter propagating currents, offering further control over the nonreciprocal response. Under periodic barrier modulation, we find more pronounced Shapiro steps in the current voltage characteristics due to the intrinsic parity time symmetric collective mode. Finally, we propose an alternative Hermitian realization using a tilted optical lattice, providing an experimentally accessible route to engineering biased transport and population transfer. Our results establish parity time symmetric atomtronic SQUIDs as a versatile platform for controlling nonreciprocal Josephson transport and collective excitations in driven quantum fluids.

Method: 构造量子几何框架：在以空间局域性、对称性与守恒律约束的"允许哈密顿量形变流形"上，定义多体本征态对该形变的敏感度（几何敏感度，可理解为态在形变下的量子几何张量/保真度敏感度类量）。该度量具备三重性质：定量（超越二元判据）、内禀（依赖允许形变流形而非探测方式）、统一（直接连接本征态结构与动力学）。随后在量子混沌的典型模型（如自旋链、无序相互作用模型）中扫描哈密顿量控制参数空间，考察该敏感度与其它混沌判据的对应关系。

Result: 在典型混沌模型中，最小几何敏感度、最大本征态随机性、局域算符的宽谱响应，以及非流体力学慢结构（如近似守恒的局域模式）的抑制，均在哈密顿量控制空间的同一区域同时出现，即这些不同的混沌标志高度重合于同一参数区。

Conclusion: 这些看似不同的混沌特征可视为同一底层几何结构的不同表现，为"某系统更混沌"提供了有意义的定量基础。该框架为理解物理约束下弛豫与置乱的极限提供了途径，并可用于在控制受限的可编程量子系统中工程化强混沌区域。

Abstract: Quantum-chaotic systems share the same coarse-grained features characteristic of random matrices, such as Wigner-Dyson level-spacing statistics, yet can exhibit strikingly different eigenstate structure and many-body dynamics, including relaxation, transport, scrambling, and entanglement growth. This raises the fundamental question of whether these finer-grained features probe genuinely distinct aspects of quantum chaos or, instead, reflect different manifestations of a common underlying structure---and whether such structure provides a meaningful basis for regarding one many-body quantum system as "more chaotic" than another. Here, we address this question by introducing a quantum geometric framework that quantifies the susceptibility of many-body eigenstates to physically allowed deformations of the Hamiltonian, as constrained by spatial locality, symmetries, and conservation laws. The resulting geometric susceptibility is quantitative, providing a metric of chaos beyond traditional binary diagnostics; intrinsic, as it depends on the manifold of allowed Hamiltonian deformations rather than on the way the system is probed; and unifying, as it directly connects eigenstate structure with dynamics. For canonical models of quantum chaos, we find that minimal geometric susceptibility, maximal eigenstate randomness, broad spectral response of local operators, and the suppression of nonhydrodynamic slow structure coalesce in the same region of Hamiltonian control space. This suggests that these seemingly distinct signatures of chaos are different manifestations of a common underlying geometric structure. Our framework provides a route toward understanding the limits of relaxation and scrambling under physical constraints; in applications, it offers a means to engineer strongly chaotic regimes in programmable quantum systems with restricted controls.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 论文旨在回答"纠缠动力学如何编码在时间演化算符的本征态中"这一基础问题，借助本征态热化假设（ETH）的视角，为确定性系统中Rényi熵演化建立谱理论基础。

Result: 在热力学极限下，纯度动力学完全由这些统计所涌现的光滑缓变函数决定，与单复制中的广义ETH表述相平行；在大子系统极限下，动力学局域性与体谱退相位共同使这些函数的结构性简化——其泛函依赖缩减为单一基本频率。

Conclusion: 该形式体系提供了类ETH陈述可同时适用于任意小与任意大子系统的框架，并讨论了向更高阶Rényi熵的推广；除厘清纠缠动力学的谱基础外，还为谱滤波方法在确定性系统中计算Rényi演化奠定基础。

Abstract: How is entanglement dynamics encoded in the eigenstates of the time-evolution operator? We address this question by focusing on Rényi entropy dynamics in deterministic chaotic Floquet models in arbitrary dimensions. Inspired by the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH), we employ spectral representations of reduced density matrix moments (purity and its higher-order generalizations) whose decay through spectral dephasing captures nonequilibrium entanglement growth. These representations closely resemble those governing local-observable dynamics, but involve eigenstates in a replicated Hilbert space and observables are replaced by subsystem permutation operators, such as the swap operator for Rényi-2. Focusing on Rényi-2, we combine exact random matrix theory calculations, established ETH results and numerical tests in 1D to formulate ETH-like ansätze for subsystem swap matrix elements in the replicated eigenbasis and their cross-correlations with initial-state overlap statistics. In the thermodynamic limit, purity dynamics is entirely determined by smooth slowly varying functions that emerge from such statistics, paralleling generalized ETH formulations in a single replica. In the limit of large subsystems, these functions exhibit a striking structural simplification: locality of the dynamics together with bulk spectral dephasing reduce their functional dependence to a single fundamental frequency. Our formalism provides a setting where ETH-like statements naturally coexist for both arbitrarily small and large subsystem sizes. We discuss extensions of our results to higher Rényi entropies. Beyond clarifying the spectral foundations of entanglement dynamics, these results lay some of the groundwork for future applications of spectral filter methods to the computation of Rényi evolution in deterministic systems.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典有效扩散问题多针对静态周期力场（Lifson–Jackson等），而力场内部状态随时间随机重置（涨落）情形下缺乏简洁、无需显式求解correctors的 \(D_{\rm eff}\) 表达式，故作者重新审视该问题。

Method: 在均匀化框架下用后向Kolmogorov方程刻画correctors；考虑力场状态按Poisson时刻从固定分布独立重抽的涨落类；通过反转力场 \(F\to-F\) 构造对偶系统，将corrector空间导数方程重释为对偶系统的平稳前向方程，从而消去corrector；引入由平稳电流决定的辅助函数 \(Q(x)\)，并用解析计算、平稳方程数值解及长时间位移直接模拟（含非零平稳电流的驱动系统）验证。

Result: \(D_{\rm eff}\) 完全用物理系统与对偶系统的平稳密度和概率电流表示；平稳电流为零时 \(Q(x)\) 位置无关，得到局部关系 \(D_{\rm eff}=L^2Q\)，将Lifson–Jackson关系推广至涨落周期力场；数值与模拟结果与理论一致。

Conclusion: 均匀化与随机对偶结合可给出涨落周期力场中有效扩散的简洁闭式表达，Lifson–Jackson型局部关系在无平稳流条件下成立，并可由平稳量而非correctors直接计算。

Abstract: We revisit the classical problem of effective diffusion in fluctuating periodic force fields by combining homogenization with stochastic duality. We consider a specific class of fluctuations in which the internal state of the force field is independently redrawn from a fixed distribution at Poisson-distributed times. In the homogenization framework, the effective diffusion coefficient \(D_{\rm eff}\) is expressed in terms of a corrector function satisfying a backward Kolmogorov equation. For the redraw dynamics considered here, the equation governing the spatial derivative of the corrector can be reinterpreted as the stationary forward equation of a dual system obtained by reversing the force field, \(F\to-F\). This relation eliminates the corrector and yields a representation of \(D_{\rm eff}\) entirely in terms of stationary densities and probability currents of the physical and dual systems. We further introduce an auxiliary function \(Q(x)\) whose spatial variation is determined by the stationary current. When this current vanishes, \(Q(x)\) becomes independent of position and the effective diffusion coefficient reduces to the local relation \( D_{\rm eff}=L^2Q. \) This result generalizes the classical Lifson--Jackson relation to fluctuating periodic force fields. We verify the theory using analytical calculations, numerical solutions of the stationary equations, and direct simulations of the long-time particle displacement, including a driven system with nonzero stationary current.

</details>

Abstract: We study the stochastic dynamics of a system of $N$ polymer rings on an infinite cylinder with only excluded volume interaction, using simulations and also analytically. The strings can diffuse in the transverse direction by local Rouse-like moves where an up-kink changes to a down -kink, or vice versa. The motion is biased if the rates of flip are not equal. In the initial state, we start with a step initial condition: all the sites with a positive $y$ coordinate are empty, and the sites with $y \leq 0$ are occupied with a uniform density. We study the scaling form of the density profile in the hydrodynamic limit. The scaling form is a function of $y/\sqrt{t}$ in the unbiased case, and a function of $y/t$ in the biased case. In the unbiased case, in the steady state on a torus, the marginal probability distribution of the positions of the strings on a line with fixed $x$-coordinate is exactly same as that in the ground state of quantum-mechanical spin-less fermions on a line. The biased problem is equivalent to a growing surface in $2+1$ dimension, which is expected to be in the Kardar-Parisi-Zhang universality class.

Abstract: We demonstrate that the size distribution of giant components in percolation and epidemic spreading models on highly inhomogeneous networks often fails to converge to a Gaussian distribution in the thermodynamic limit. Instead, when appropriately scaled, the distribution converges to a non-Gaussian limit distribution (NGLD). This scaling behavior aligns with the generalized central limit theorem (GCLT), which governs sums of random variables following heavy-tailed distributions. For scale-free networks, the scaling exponent is determined solely by the degree exponent of the network, independently of the control parameter. In contrast, on the Farey graph, a deterministic hierarchical small-world network, the scaling exponent varies continuously with the control parameter.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将双连通分量分解与图的SPQR分解相结合，设计可在大型系统中高效识别三连通团簇的算法；对d=2,3,4进行大规模蒙特卡罗模拟测量分形维数；在d=6及完全图（d=∞）上考察最大三连通团簇的尺寸标度；并分析三连通团簇尺寸分布二阶矩对应的类磁化率奇异贡献χ~L^{2d_3-d}。

Result: 测得d=2,3,4时分形维数分别为d_3=1.2308(3)、1.083(2)、0.77(2)，随维度增大单调递减；d=6时最大三连通团簇随系统尺寸对数增长，完全图上则趋于有限尺寸，二者均表明d≥6时d_3=0；类磁化率奇异项按χ~L^{2d_3-d}标度，在d≥3时消失，留下有限的渐近磁化率。

Conclusion: 三连通团簇的分形维数随维度系统性下降并在d≥6时归零；其类磁化率的奇异贡献在d≥3时不再发散，呈现有限渐近值，与标准渗流中发散行为形成鲜明对比，揭示了高阶连通性在临界现象中的独特标度机制。

Abstract: We investigate the critical geometry of triconnected clusters in percolation, which remain connected after the removal of any two sites. By combining biconnected-component decomposition with the SPQR decomposition of graphs, we develop an efficient algorithm for identifying triconnected clusters in large systems. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations yield estimates of the fractal dimension $d_3=1.2308(3)$, $1.083(2)$, and $0.77(2)$ in dimensions $d=2$, $3$, and $4$, respectively, revealing a systematic decrease of $d_3$ with increasing $d$. In dimension $d=6$, the largest triconnected cluster grows logarithmically with system size, whereas on complete graphs, corresponding to $d=\infty$, it approaches a finite size. Both behaviors indicate a vanishing fractal dimension, $d_3=0$, for $d\geq6$. Furthermore, the singular contribution to the susceptibility-like quantity associated with the second moment of the triconnected-cluster size distribution scales as $\chi\sim L^{2d_3-d}$. Consequently, it vanishes for $d\geq3$, leaving a finite asymptotic susceptibility, in contrast to the divergent behavior observed in standard percolation.

</details>

Result: 16个案例的有效系统能量均极低，因而处于RJ凝聚区：总概率的很大比例集中于最低能态，对应于全文中仅出现一两次的大量稀有词。该RJ凝聚与多模光纤中光传播等物理系统的凝聚现象类似；热化理论对真实数据的描述与Zipf/Lorenz/Pareto曲线高度吻合。

Conclusion: 词语热化假说能够统一、定量地刻画文学作品的词频分布，将语言统计规律与物理热化凝聚及经济财富热化纳入同一理论框架，并给出与实测数据"惊人地好"的拟合结果。

Abstract: We introduce the word thermalization hypothesis according to which in a literary work there are effective interactions between words that drive them to thermalization where their word frequency distribution is the same as for the phenomenon of Rayleigh-Jeans (RJ) thermalization and condensation. In this concept, the different words play a role of classical oscillators (particles) and their word frequencies, or related tokens, are analogous to certain filling factors over energy state levels. The thermalization process is characterized by the conservation of total system energy, or total number of words/tokens, and total probability norm, or total number of different words. We apply this approach to analyze 16 literary works, including Bible, complete works of Shakespeare, and data from American National Corpus. We show that for these cases the effective system energy is very low and thus there is the RJ condensation phenomenon with a big fraction of total probability concentrated at the lowest energy states corresponding to a big fraction of rare words appearing only once or twice in the whole text. Such a RJ condensation is observed for light propagation in multimode optical fibers and other physical systems. We relate the thermalization of words to the recently proposed wealth thermalization for households in world countries. The economy concepts of Lorenz and Pareto curves, and also Zipf curves, are applied to the word frequency distributions of the selected 16 cases and it is shown that the thermalization theory gives a strikingly good description of real data.

</details>

Abstract: In quantum dynamics of few-body observables in quadratic systems, information about the initial state is retained in long-time averages, as captured by the generalized Gibbs ensemble. Here, we show that this memory extends beyond the averages to temporal fluctuations around them. By generating a manifold of non-Gaussian initial states and quantifying their non-Gaussianity via the corresponding entropy, we find that the magnitude of temporal fluctuations is a smooth function of the initial-state non-Gaussianity. This relation explains the qualitatively different scaling of fluctuations observed after quantum quenches: exponentially small fluctuations for non-Gaussian initial states and polynomially small fluctuations for Gaussian initial states. Therefore, our results identify non-Gaussianity as a key to uncovering initial-state memory beyond long-time averages.

</details>

Abstract: Scaling and multi-scaling are two guiding principles for understanding nature or even commerce and industrial outcome. In the realm of critical phenomena, scaling laws describe the behavior of physical quantities near a continuous phase transition. This is a complex phenomena where systems exhibit critical fluctuations and long-range correlations, challenging classical formulations such as the fluctuation-dissipation theorem (FDT). In particular, near critical points, the divergence of the correlation length and the emergence of fractal geometry in spin clusters suggest the need for alternative frameworks. In this work, we explore the connection between critical exponents, fractal dimensions, and the thermodynamic formalism known as the Principium Luxuriae. By linking the thermodynamic fractal dimension D to the correlation length and entropy variations, we reinterpret energy dissipation in terms of multiscale processes. This approach allows us to reproduce effective scaling tendencies associated with the critical behavior of the 2d Ising model and provides new insight into how systems redistribute energy macroscopically during phase transitions. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the decrease in D near the critical temperature marks a transition toward macroscopic coherence, where the system begins to dissipate energy across its entire scale. This unified behavior, however, induces a heightened sensitivity to perturbations, where local fluctuations are transmitted globally through the fractal network. This mechanism establishes a direct bridge between geometric structure, thermodynamic entropy, and the onset of dynamical instability (chaos) as the system approaches criticality.

</details>


### [33] [From Distribution of Extremes With Degradation to the Stress Redundancy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14375)

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究退化（杀伤）源如何影响众多独立随机粒子中最快到达窄靶的那一个的首达时间与出射点分布，揭示杀伤场对 $1/\log N_0$ 标度的修正、提出"应力冗余原理"，并用最优控制给出最快粒子集中的最优轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多相同独立随机粒子中最先成功到达窄靶者的统计行为尚不清楚，而降解（杀伤）源对首达时间分布与出射位置分布的影响更是缺乏定量刻画；这类统计对界定细胞与分子时间尺度很重要。

Method: 利用与 Fokker-Planck 方程对应的时间依赖通量研究首达时间与出射点分布；通过求解混合边值问题计算逃逸概率；在大初始种群 $N_0$ 极限下导出最快粒子平均首达时间的渐近公式；对限制在窄子区域的降解场采用最优控制方法求最优轨迹；并解析推导二维规则域窄吸收弧上的首出点分布。

Result: 得到刻画杀伤场如何修正经典 $1/\log(N_0)$ 标度的渐近公式；发现首次出射时刻的种群规模对降解具有特定依赖，即"应力冗余原理"；最优轨迹会依杀伤强度避开降解区；给出二维规则域窄吸收弧上首出点分布的解析表达，并说明与靶边界相切的杀伤子区域的几何参数如何影响出射分布。

Conclusion: 杀伤源能显著改变最快扩散粒子的统计特性，对定义细胞和分子时间尺度具有实际意义。

Abstract: How a degradation source affects the statistics of the first among many identical independent stochastic particles to successfully reach a narrow target remains unclear. Here, we investigate the impact of degradation on the distributions of first-passage times and exit locations by exploiting the time-dependent fluxes associated with the corresponding Fokker-Planck equation. We also compute the escape probability by solving a mixed boundary value problem. In the large initial population $N_0$ limit, we derive an asymptotic formula for the mean first passage time associated with the fastest particle, revealing how the killing field modifies the classical $1/\log(N_0)$ scaling. However, when considering the population size at the time of the first exit, we obtain a specific dependency with respect to degradation, a phenomenon that we call the stress redundancy principle. For a degradation field restricted to a narrow subregion, we use an optimal control approach to derive the optimal trajectory where the fastest escape particles will be concentrated: interestingly, we report here that the optimal trajectories avoid the degradation area depending on killing intensity. Finally, we obtain analytical expressions for the distribution of first exit points along a narrow absorbing arc in two-dimensional regular domains. In addition, for a killing subregion tangent to the target boundary, our computations reveal how geometrical parameters influence the exit distribution. In summary, a killing source can significantly affect the statistics of the fastest diffusing particles, relevant for defining cellular and molecular time scales.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个紧凑、精确可解且易调节的框架，使少数透明微观参数即可控制手性活性输运，并揭示超出平滑角扩散近似的有限步效应。

Abstract: We introduce a minimal and intuitive lattice model for an active chiral particle which exhibits persistence, reversal, and chirality in its dynamics. It is fully defined by a small set of microscopic parameters that provide transparent control over the resulting dynamics. By coarse-graining, we extract continuum equations and closed-form expressions for the velocity autocorrelation, off-diagonal directional correlation, mean-squared displacement, and long-time diffusion and odd diffusion coefficients. The complex parameter $\lambda=q_p+\mathrm{i}q_c$ thereby encodes the microscopic information on persistence ($q_p$) and chirality ($q_c$) of the walker. In a weak-turning limit, microscopically realized by infrequent reorientation events, the continuum description can be mapped onto well-established models of active matter. Outside this limit the discrete model exhibits finite-step dynamics, such as anti-persistent motion, that are not captured by a smooth angular-diffusion description. The model therefore provides a compact, exactly solvable, and readily tunable framework in which a small number of transparent microscopic parameters control chiral active transport.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互线性（在某一转移速率任意强变化下，稳态可观测量之间仍保持仿射关系）此前仅在马尔可夫跳跃过程和化学反应网络中已知。作者希望检验这一结构是否可推广到非线性、且可能出现物种灭绝与混沌吸引子的生态动力学中。

Method: 将互线性概念引入广义Lotka–Volterra（GLV）动力系统；对单一物种方程的任意改变（参数/项扰动）进行分析，证明其对其他物种的作用可归约为一个有效的“压迫”（press）扰动，并考察灭绝导致的区域分段与混沌吸引子上的时间平均。

Result: GLV被识别为一类新的互线性系统；单物种扰动对其他物种表现为单一有效press；所有丰度保持仿射相关，且在存在灭绝时按分段成立，在混沌吸引子上以长时间平均意义成立。

Conclusion: 互线性并非线性或马尔可夫结构所独有，而是可存在于具有灭绝和混沌等强非线性行为的种群动力学中；这为跨上述系统类别的统一稳态响应理论提供了新范例。

Abstract: Mutual Linearity in Nonlinear Population DynamicsMutual linearity, the persistence of affine relations among stationary observables under arbitrarily strong changes of one transition rate, is known for Markov jump processes and chemical reaction networks. We show that generalized Lotka--Volterra dynamics form a new class of mutually linear systems, even though species can go extinct and attractors can be chaotic. Any change to one species' equation acts on the others as one effective press and keeps all abundances affinely related: piecewise across extinctions, and as time averages on chaotic attractors.

</details>

### [36] [Planckian Charge Equilibration in Fast Scramblers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13081)
*Jaewon Kim,Eduardo Fradkin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究具有最大扰乱（scrambling）速率的cSYK模型及其Yukawa推广中电荷失衡的弛豫，发现低温共形区最慢衰减率为Γ=2π(2Δ−d)k_BT/ℏ，从而确立最大扰乱系统中的Planckian电荷平衡，并表明最大扰乱本身并不固定其无量纲前因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Planckian时间τ_Pl=ℏ/(k_BT)被猜想为强关联量子物质热弛豫时间的下界，但"信息扰乱速率"与"达到平衡/流体力学出现的速率"之间的关系仍不清楚，尤其在最大扰乱的模型中两者是否一致缺乏直接检验。

Method: 以complex Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（cSYK）模型及其Yukawa推广为对象，研究两个子系统间电荷失衡的弛豫；在低温共形区采用线性响应分析计算失衡模式的最慢衰减率，参数化为普适形式Γ=2π(2Δ−d)k_BT/ℏ（Δ为费米子标度维度，d=0,1为空间维度）。

Result: 最慢的电荷失衡衰减率线性于温度，满足Planckian标度，前因子为2π(2Δ−d)；该前因子随Δ与d变化，并非由最大扰乱速率唯一确定（最大扰乱仅给出Γ∼k_BT/ℏ的量级）。

Conclusion: 最大扰乱系统同样呈现Planckian电荷平衡，说明弛豫时间的Planckian下界可与最大扰乱共存；但扰乱速率不能单独固定平衡速率的无量纲系数，扰乱与平衡速率之间不存在一一对应关系。

Abstract: The Planckian time, $\tau_{\rm Pl}=\hbar/(k_BT)$, is conjectured to set a lower limit on thermal relaxation times in strongly interacting quantum matter. Rigorous bounds limit both how rapidly quantum information can scramble and how soon hydrodynamics can emerge, yet the relation between the scrambling and equilibration rates remains unclear. We address this question by studying the relaxation of a charge imbalance between two subsystems of the complex Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev model and its Yukawa generalizations, which exhibit maximal scrambling. In the low-temperature conformal regime, we find through a linear response analysis that the slowest decay of this imbalance occurs at rate $\Gamma=2\pi(2\Delta-d)k_BT/\hbar$, where $\Delta$ is the fermion scaling dimension and $d=0,1$ is the spatial dimension. These results establish Planckian charge equilibration in maximally scrambling systems and show that maximal scrambling alone does not fix its dimensionless prefactor.

</details>

Abstract: Charge density waves are among the earliest known ordered states in quantum materials, yet they have re-emerged at the center of modern discussions of topology, symmetry breaking, and collective dynamics. In several material classes, charge-density-wave order reshapes electronic bands, boundary states, defect structures, and electromagnetic responses in ways that invite a topological interpretation. Real-space imaging has shown that incommensurate order can form networks of phase defects rather than uniform modulations. Boundary-resolved spectroscopy has revealed in-gap modes whose existence and spatial structure track the charge-density-wave order, although such observations remain confined to a single material family. In Weyl materials, density-wave gaps have been linked to axion-like magnetoelectric response, placing the charge-density-wave phase within a dynamical-field description of the effective action. In kagome metals, charge order is intertwined with Berry curvature, unconventional symmetry breaking, and superconductivity. These developments have led to the term "topological charge density wave," but the phrase describes several physically distinct phenomena. We distinguish three meanings: (i) topology of the order parameter and its defects, (ii) topology of the charge-density-wave-reconstructed electronic structure and its boundary modes, and (iii) axion-like electromagnetic response generated by density-wave order. The experimental and theoretical support for these three meanings differs substantially. This Perspective traces the development of these ideas, clarifies the evidence behind each class, identifies unresolved questions and points of debate, and outlines experimental criteria for establishing topology in charge-density-wave states.

This new definition is a parallel to the Gibbs entropy formulation, but it can also be used in out-of-equilibrium regimes. Some important properties of this entropy are discussed. In addition we derive some information theory quantities such as relative entropy, conditional entropy, mutual information and Rényi entropy. We then compare the results with Wehrl entropy for the 1D harmonic oscillator, for pure and mixed states. We conclude the the work discussing potential applications of this new formalism, ranging from quantum correlations beyond entanglement to thermalization in closed quantum systems.

We show how the latter may lead to the sign reversal of the 3-wave mixing nonlinearity and stabilization of the parametric amplification. Conclusion: TDEE的标度行为敏感依赖于能隙、带宽、时间步τ与无序；连续时间极限下时间区间的子希尔伯特空间趋于稳定，可理解为Lieb–Robinson因果钻石所对应希尔伯特空间的子空间。

Abstract: While the entanglement entropy of a spatial subregion has been extensively studied, a properly defined entanglement entropy of quantum states in a time direction interval remains underexplored. We investigate the scaling behaviors of the recently defined time direction entanglement entropy (TDEE) for free fermion quantum states, which is computed from the reduced density matrix of a sub-Hilbert space that minimally supports all the time-evolved operators in the time direction interval. Based on the Fisher--Hartwig conjecture for Toeplitz matrices, we develop an analytical framework for understanding the TDEE in a time interval with discrete time step $\tau$. For disorderless one-dimensional (1D) systems (gapless fermions or band insulators), in different regimes of gap, bandwidth and $\tau$, we find the TDEE can exhibit area law (for nonzero gap), logarithmic law or volume law in the length of time. For 1D Anderson insulators, we numerically find the TDEE shows a Page-curve like behavior, growing at short time, and decaying in power-law at long time exceeding the crossover time determined by localization. In the continuum time limit $\tau\rightarrow 0$, the TDEE of disorderless gapless fermions, band insulators and Anderson insulators exhibit logarithmic law, area law and sub-area law, respectively. We also show that the sub-Hilbert space of a time-direction interval stabilizes in the $\tau\rightarrow 0$ limit, which is understandable as a subspace of the Hilbert space of the Lieb-Robinson causal diamond.

</details>

Abstract: Kinematic quantum geometry describes fast degrees of freedom (DOFs) that adiabatically follow states parametrized by both the coordinates and the velocities of slow DOFs. It extends Born-Oppenheimer geometry, which depends on coordinates alone, introduces mixed Berry curvatures, and modifies the effective theory governing the slow DOFs. We test its predictions in two setups: spinful particles moving in magnetic textures, and Dirac electrons in graphene coupled to an LC circuit. In the first, kinematic quantum geometry produces a velocity-dependent effective mass tensor and generates a Lorentz-like force nonlinear in velocity that can arise even in coplanar textures. In the second, it renormalizes the circuit capacitance and generates a Hall current nonlinear in the electric field. For small velocities, it reproduces recent results for electronic dynamics in external electromagnetic fields and in pseudospin textures. However, the framework can remain valid at large velocities beyond the perturbative regime, provided the kinematic states are sufficiently slowly varying.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 制备、加速、鲁棒性与涨落均由同一个精确可解过程——局域约束违背的输运与吸收——所支配，为测量反馈型多体态制备提供了定量、可解析的设计准则。

Abstract: Quantum circuits based on local measurements and feedback can autonomously prepare and stabilize entangled many-body states. In frustration-free control, the target manifold is specified by local constraints, whose violations are detected by measurement and removed by feedback. We develop an exact theory of this mechanism for the preparation of Dicke states of $N_q$ qubits. Dicke states span the permutation-symmetric subspace, one per magnetization sector. Local singlet projectors define the constraints and detect departures from permutation symmetry. The resulting dynamics closes exactly on the span of the $N_q(N_q-1)/2$ singlet-pair projectors and is equivalent to a classical first-passage process: measurements move the pair endpoints, while feedback absorbs pairs on measured bonds. The closure reduces the exponentially large many-body Liouvillian to a generator of polynomial size. In one dimension we solve this process in closed form. Its slowest rate, $2[1-\cos(\pi/N_q)]$ in units of the measurement rate, is an exact Liouvillian eigenvalue. For any scheme of this class, the relaxation time is bounded below by the inverse fraction of pair configurations on which feedback acts; local feedback reaches $O(N_q)$ of $O(N_q^2)$ configurations, requiring a time at least linear in $N_q$. A second exact closure determines the final state reached from any initial condition. Extensions to modified transport, restricted feedback, and physical errors yield an exact transport-to-absorber crossover, geometric preparation laws, and a bound on the stationary leakage. An exact second-moment closure determines the ensemble fluctuations: the measurement noise is conserving, while feedback generates removal shot noise and pair correlations. Preparation, acceleration, robustness, and fluctuations are thus governed by one exactly solvable process: the transport and absorption of local constraint violations.

</details>

As $\theta$ approaches the ballistic SWAP point $\pi/4$, the two length scales diverge at very different rates, leaving a broad intermediate regime $d_{\mathrm{ba}}\ll d\ll d_\mathrm{RG}$ in which these quantities show substantial finite-size drift and approximate plateaus. To establish the microscopic basis of this crossover picture, we derive a leading inter-flavor quartic interaction of order $\beta_R^2$ in the replicated Majorana theory, which provides a route to the class-D nonlinear sigma model. The resulting marginal RG flow motivates the scaling variable $X_4=\beta_R^4\ln d$, in which free-fermion simulations show approximate collapse of decoding measures. These results are consistent with a Pauli-decoding threshold at $\theta_c=\pi/4$ in the thermodynamic limit. For any even $d$, we prove that maximum-likelihood Pauli decoding maximizes entanglement fidelity over all deterministic syndrome-conditioned quantum recoveries, and that its decoding threshold attains the quantum-optimal limit, coinciding with the one defined by coherent information.

Abstract: Randomized measurements offer a way to probe nonlinear properties of quantum states without reconstructing their full density matrices. The accessible information is encoded in algebraic invariants of the quantum state and is reconstructed by analysing the permutation symmetries induced by the distributed measurement protocol. Exploiting these symmetries, the reconstruction problem collapses, regardless of the number of measurement outcomes, to the symbolic inversion of a 3 x 3 matrix. As a result, nonlinear information can be extracted without inverting the exponentially large matrices that typically arise in distributed multipartite quantum systems.

Abstract: The expressivity, loss landscapes, and generalization behavior of quantum machine learning models have been studied extensively, yet mainly from a model-centric point of view. This metric quantifies the information a model can extract from a dataset, enabling a direct, data-centric comparison of different models. We employ this metric to study which data points contribute most significantly to a model's learning process. Abstract: Coarse-grained measurements provide a lower bound on entropy production, but how much microscopic irreversibility can they conceal? For fixed observation duration $\mathcal T$ and detector parameters, we prove that the observed relative entropy remains uniformly bounded as the bath temperature $T_b$ vanishes and is strictly positive at vacuum. The hidden contribution obeys $\Sigma_{\mathrm{hid}}(\mathcal T,T_b)=\mathcal T\dot Q_0/(k_BT_b)+O(1)$, where $\dot Q_0>0$ is the stationary vacuum heat current. Thus, finite and informative measurements of entropy production can coexist with infinite microscopic irreversibility.

TL;DR: FusionQ 提出一种"融合解码"框架：根据实际观测到的 syndrome 动态构造局部解码任务（围绕被触发的 detector 簇建立开边界局部 DEM），再将局部解融合成单一全局纠错结果，从而打破传统"整体 DEM 或固定时间窗"的固定解码范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 QEC 解码以固定解码实例为中心：要么把整个时空 detector error model（DEM）一次性交给后端，要么按预设时间窗切分，必须等足够 syndrome 到达后才开始解码。这种模式既造成硬件采集与解码无法重叠、延迟高，也无法适配实际稀疏触发的错误分布。

Method: 1）围绕被触发的 detector 簇构建开边界局部 DEM；2）仅当局部最优解使用了虚拟边界错误时，才扩展相应 footprint；3）调度器对后端的要求极简——只需返回局部解与"边界使用标志"，因此精确求解器、图状 DEM 上的匹配类解码器、启发式解码器可共用同一运行时；4）将已发布的局部解码结果融合为全局修正。

Result: 理论上证明了组合最优性定理：在精确最小权局部后端且发布结果不使用边界的条件下，融合局部解可得到原 DEM 的全局最大似然错误（MLE）解。实验上，使用精确后端时 FusionQ 在每一个 shot 上都与整体式 MLE 结果一致；跨多种解码后端，FusionQ 借助局部解码与硬件 syndrome 采集的重叠显著降低了解码延迟。

Conclusion: FusionQ 以"按 syndrome 派生局部任务 + 融合"的方式重构了解码流程，在保持全局最优性保证的同时具备后端无关性和良好的流水线并行性，为低延迟、可扩展的 QEC 解码提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) decoding has traditionally been organized around fixed decoding instances: a monolithic space-time detector error model (DEM), or a predetermined temporal window, is handed to a backend decoder after enough syndrome data has arrived. This paper presents FusionQ, a fusion decoding framework that changes this paradigm by deriving local decoding tasks from the observed syndrome and then fusing their results into a single global correction. Instead of forcing every shot through the same global or windowed problem, FusionQ builds open-boundary local DEMs around fired detector clusters and expands the corresponding footprints only when a local optimum uses virtual boundary errors. FusionQ is generic in the decoder backend: the scheduler requires only a local solution and a boundary-use flag, so exact solvers, matching-style decoders on graphlike DEMs, and heuristic decoders can share the same runtime. We prove a compositional optimality theorem: with an exact minimum-weight local backend and boundary-free publication, fusing the published local decoding results yields a global most-likely error (MLE) solution of the original DEM. Our evaluation shows that, with an exact backend, FusionQ matches the monolithic MLE result on every shot. Across decoder backends, FusionQ substantially reduces decoding latency by overlapping local decoding with hardware syndrome acquisition.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a finite-memory repeat-until-success (RUS) framework for implementing rational spectral filters on a quantum computer, extending spectral filtering beyond the polynomial transformations implemented by quantum signal processing (QSP) and quantum singular value transformation (QSVT). Our approach interleaves queries to a block encoding of a Hermitian operator $X$, whose spectrum lies in $[-1,1]$, and mid-circuit measurements that either accept, reject, or continue the computation without resetting the quantum state. Although the protocol may run for arbitrarily many rounds, its terminal statistics are determined by a finite-dimensional Lyapunov equation. Under uniform termination and phase-inversion symmetry, we characterize exactly the spectral filters $R(X)$ realizable upon acceptance: $R$ can be any real rational function with no poles on $[-1,1]$ and values in $[0,1]$. We provide a constructive compiler for any admissible $R$, with bounds on memory and expected query cost, and characterize the accepted quantum operation through a spectral coherence kernel. Applications to regularized resolvents and Lorentzian spectroscopy yield exact low-degree realizations and query-cost advantages over the fixed-depth QSVT polynomial-postselection baseline considered here, with all repetitions and independent restarts after rejection included in the cost.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Electrical solid-state qubits are generally initialized, manipulated and read out through diffusive conductors. Starting from a Keldysh nonlinear sigma model for the disordered conductive region, coupled capacitively to two-level systems, we obtain the noise kernel in closed form. It decays as the inverse square of the qubit separation and changes sign with the orientation of the pair, so that qubit pairs at equal separation may be correlated or anticorrelated, an unambiguous experimental signature. In two dimensions, the correlated noise a single qubit shares with the rest of the array, in the worst-case scenario, grows logarithmically with array size rather than converging. The sufficient condition for fault tolerance under correlated noise is therefore violated, so standard threshold theorems no longer guarantee protection against this channel for large qubit arrays. As this dephasing originates from the soft-mode content of the environment rather than on any specific gate detail, we expect correlated dephasing channels of this kind wherever solid-state qubits or sensors share a gapless conductive medium.

</details>

The memory of the record is therefore quantized, one bit on the edge and at most one more for every site of displacement, a bound that generic gates attain. Abstract: An exceptional point of a memory-coefficient equation need not have the same signature in every quantum-state observable. Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) needs compiler intermediate representations (IRs) that preserve error-correcting-code choices, syndrome information, and non-Clifford resource requirements through analysis, simulation, and hardware lowering. We report practical experience building FTQC, an open-source Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR) dialect that makes logical qubits, syndromes, magic states, and Pauli frames typed static single assignment (SSA) values. Its error-correction-aware type system, two-level lowering design, and IR-resident resource analysis provide one inspectable pipeline to Open Quantum Assembly Language~3 (OpenQASM~3), the Quantum Intermediate Representation (QIR), Stim, and IQM hardware. Case studies across three code families show that typed metadata makes code-specific decisions verifiable and retargetable: on the 20-qubit Pathfinder at Oak Ridge National Laboratory, decoding reduced logical readout error by 3.3 times. They also expose hidden architectural costs: for a three-qubit Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT-3), a magic-state factory requires five times the peak qubits of the encoded circuit. These experiences demonstrate a practical foundation for developing and evaluating fault-tolerant compiler transformations across software and hardware targets.

Abstract: We extend the efficient classical simulation of Ising quantum computers from braidings to measurements. In case the circuit generator can see the entire input, we give a detailed proof that efficient simulation of classical computation would imply the separation of complexity classes $\mathbf{P}$ and $\mathbf{PSPACE}$. If the circuit generator sees only the input length, we prove that Clifford quantum computers decide what we call affine languages.

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes can reduce the number of data qubits required per logical qubit, but their checks require long-range interactions on a planar device. We develop a singlet fabric for bivariate-bicycle codes on a silicon node network. Spin singlets are generated on shuttling lanes and distributed between nodes, where local operations consume them to measure non-local stabilizers. We construct two syndrome-extraction circuits, based on distributed GHZ states and ancilla teleportation, and evaluate them using hardware-constrained schedules and circuit-level memory simulations. We compare these circuits with CAbLECAR, which transports a mobile ancilla between the data qubits of each check. Teleportation can outperform CAbLECAR on its original layout when transport includes spin flips as well as dephasing. Placing the mobile ancilla on our node lattice, however, shortens the mean scheduled route for the [[144,12,12]] Gross code from 350 to 153 $\mu$m and lowers its logical error rate up to 50-fold in the sampled bit-flip regime. Direct transport then achieves a lower logical error rate than either singlet circuit at every sampled Gross-code point. With frequent transport bit flips, the GHZ circuit overtakes direct transport once two-qubit gate and readout errors fall below about $4$-$5\times10^{-4}$, roughly half the value assumed here. Fixing the ancilla's data-visit order reveals a trade-off between shorter routes and the correlated errors produced by ancilla faults. Source heralding improves both singlet circuits, whereas phase purification helps the GHZ circuit only above a noise-dependent crossover and worsens teleportation throughout. These results show that the physical layout and interaction order can be as consequential as the choice of extraction circuit, and that cleaner entangled resources do not necessarily produce a more reliable logical memory.

</details>

Conclusion: 以极小的先验扰动代价即可显著改善量子 BP 译码的精度与效率，PriorTilt 是一种低开销、易集成的量子 LDPC 译码增强方案。

Abstract: Although belief propagation (BP) is a widely adopted decoder for classical low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes, its accuracy is limited in the quantum setting. We introduce \PriorTilt{}, a variant of BP obtained by selectively perturbing a small fraction of its prior log-likelihood ratios using offsets determined by recent posterior and outgoing-message statistics, while preserving the same message-passing rules. We compare \PriorTilt{} with Relay-BP and beam search decoders for circuit-level decoding of three benchmark codes. \PriorTilt{} achieves a lower logical error rate (LER) than both baselines at every tested data point. For the $[[144,12,12]]$ Gross code BB144 at noise rate $p=10^{-3}$, the LER reductions reach $4.3\times$ relative to Relay-BP and $3.1\times$ relative to the beam search decoder. At $p=10^{-3}$, \PriorTilt{} has lower mean decoding times than Relay-BP on all three codes and achieves mean decoding speedups of approximately $2.6\times$--$3.0\times$ over the beam search decoder. For BB144 at $p=10^{-3}$, \PriorTilt{} achieves an LER approximately $610\times$ lower than the standard min-sum BP decoder with the same iteration budget, while perturbing fewer than $0.5\%$ of its priors per round on average.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 对费米子：构造单拷贝测量方案，使测量噪声在每个占据数尺度上与迹距离度量匹配，证得迹距离ε的上界Θ(m³/ε²)，结合已知下界完成匹配；对k-拷贝分块，采用arXiv:2611.07271的协变最高权测量并以Weyl-位移估计读出，证明其对任意态精确无偏，导出Frobenius范数及远离纯边界类迹距离下的Θ(max{m³/k, m²}/ε²)。对玻色子：有界协方差下设计单拷贝测量，给出含r（满足r·n_r≤2的最大下标）与占据数和倒数和项的上界O((m²+r³+Σ_{i,j>r}1/(n_i+n_j))/ε²)，并证明其在二进区间类上为minimax最优；无能量/压缩限制下采用非高斯测量（含块纠缠仅限首轮、块大小4m+6）获得O(ε⁻²)、O(m²/ε²)、O(m³/ε²)的拷贝数。

Result: (1) 费米子混合（含纯模）高斯态单拷贝迹距离学习为Θ(m³/ε²)拷贝、无对数因子，优于集体测量的Θ(m²/ε²)，解决了猜想10.1（ε≤1/3600的下界）并补齐样本复杂度表多数空项；(2) k-拷贝协变最高权测量+Weyl位移估计在任意态上精确无偏，代价Θ(max{m³/k,m²}/ε²)，在所有态上达Frobenius范数最优，在远离纯边界的类上达迹距离最优，实现了k≤m时1/k的加速（而冷守恒态仅为1/√k）；(3) 玻色子有界协方差态O((m²+r³+Σ1/(n_i+n_j))/ε²)单拷贝测量足够且在二进区间类上minimax最优，无需谱隙与冷模计数假设，位移被动态无需协方差界；(4) 无能量/压缩界时非高斯测量从O(ε⁻²)、O(m²/ε²)、O(m³/ε²)拷贝学习中心单模混合态、中心纯m模态与一般m模态，去除高斯测量的log log E项，纠缠仅需首轮块（4m+6）。

Abstract: With single-copy measurements, every mixed fermionic Gaussian state on $m$ modes, pure modes included, is learned to trace distance $\epsilon$ from $\Theta(m^3/\epsilon^2)$ copies, with no logarithmic factor, against the known $\Theta(m^2/\epsilon^2)$ for collective measurements. We prove the upper bound, resolving Conjecture 10.1 of  arXiv:2609.23189  (lower bound for $\epsilon\le1/3600$), and fill most open entries of the sample-complexity table of fermionic and bosonic Gaussian states, for single-copy, $k$-copy and collective measurements. The single-copy measurement's noise is matched to the metric at every occupation scale. With blocks of $k$ copies, the covariant highest-weight measurement of  arXiv:2611.07271 , read out through the Weyl-shift estimator, is exactly unbiased for every state; it gives $\Theta(\max\{m^3/k,m^2\}/\epsilon^2)$ in Frobenius norm on all states and in trace distance on every class at a fixed distance from the pure boundary. The gain $1/k$ ($k\le m$) away from the pure boundary, against $1/\sqrt k$ on cold number-conserving states, is therefore attained. For bosonic Gaussian states with bounded covariance, any displacement and squeezing, and occupations $n_1\le\dots\le n_m$, $O((m^2+r^3+\sum_{i,j>r}1/(n_i+n_j))/\epsilon^2)$ single-copy measurements suffice, $r$ the largest index with $r\,n_r\le2$; this is the minimax rate on dyadic-interval classes, with no spectral gap or cold-mode count assumed, and holds with no covariance bound for displaced passive states. With no bound on energy or squeezing, non-Gaussian measurements learn centred one-mode states, mixed included, from $O(\epsilon^{-2})$ and centred pure $m$-mode states from $O(m^2/\epsilon^2)$ single copies, free of the $\log\log E$ term of Gaussian measurements, and $m$-mode states from $O(m^3/\epsilon^2)$ copies, entangled only in a first round on blocks of $4m+6$.

Abstract: The negatively charged boron vacancy ($V_{\mathrm{B}}^{-}$) in hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) is a promising spin defect for quantum sensing in two-dimensional material systems.
