Here we implement a double-quantum driving scheme based on simultaneous resonant microwave excitation of both ground-state single-quantum transitions, establishing coherent control of the $\Delta m_s=2$ manifold in a $V_{\mathrm{B}}^{-}$ ensemble. We compare the resulting double-quantum response with the corresponding single-quantum protocol under identical experimental conditions. The double-quantum protocol enhances the optical readout contrast and yields a 2.6-fold signal-to-noise improvement in AC magnetometry. Motivation: 以往对腔量子电动力学中原子自发辐射的研究多关注马尔可夫或单模极限，而原子发射的光在有限尺寸离散腔中返回并再激发原子的非马尔可夫反馈会如何塑造时间依赖的物理光谱（可观测光谱，而非仅布居动力学），尚缺乏定量刻画；同时布居对反馈相位不敏感，需寻找能分辨相位的谱学量。

Method: 考虑置于腔中心的二能级原子与大量离散电磁腔模耦合；在耦合强度于原子线宽内近似平坦的条件下，将发射动力学约化为Bixon–Jortner型延迟微分方程，并以其Laguerre多项式解析解为基础，计算时间依赖物理光谱（含滤波带宽 $\Gamma$），辅以精确对角化数值验证缀饰模谱。

Result: 物理光谱呈现与布居复活同步的复兴，其间形成间距为 $2\pi/t_r$ 的瞬态频率梳；梳齿源于相继发射阶段间的量子干涉，位置对应共振反射原子所构成的两个有效半腔的共振而非腔模；梳的可见度随滤波带宽按 $e^{-\Gamma t_r}$ 标度；长时间极限下梳收敛为缀饰模谱，解析结果与精确对角化定量一致；物理光谱还能分辨布居所不敏感的反馈相位，并给出原子位置的额外频率分辨特征。

Conclusion: 该工作表明时间依赖物理光谱是探测腔反馈非马尔可夫动力学、反馈相位与原子位置的有力工具，并给出了超导transmon与微波腔、表面声波谐振器两种可行实验方案。

Abstract: We study the time-dependent physical spectrum of light for a two-level atom coupled to a large, but discrete, number of modes of an electromagnetic cavity. Radiation emitted by the atom, placed at the center of the cavity, returns after the round-trip time $t_r$, re-excites it, and is re-emitted, producing a sequence of atomic population revivals. For a coupling approximately flat over the atomic linewidth, the emission dynamics reduces to a delay-differential equation of Bixon--Jortner type. Using its analytical solution in terms of Laguerre polynomials, we investigate how successive emission and reabsorption cycles shape the time-dependent spectrum. The physical spectrum exhibits revivals during which a transient frequency comb of spacing $2\pi/t_r$ develops. The comb results from the quantum interference between successive emission stages; its teeth sit not at the cavity modes but at the resonances of the effective two half-cavities created by the resonantly reflecting atom. The visibility scales as $e^{-\Gamma t_r}$ with the filter bandwidth $\Gamma$, and at long times the comb converges to the dressed-mode spectrum, which we also obtain analytically in quantitative agreement with exact diagonalization. We show that the physical spectrum resolves the feedback phase, to which the atomic population is insensitive in the regime of well-separated emission stages, and provides additional frequency-resolved signatures of the atomic position. We discuss two feasible experimental implementations of our results with transmons (artificial atoms) coupled to microwave and surface-acoustic-wave resonators.

</details>

Method: 设计一种新型光纤量子路由器，采用更透明的操作方式，减少对混合包（经典头+量子载荷）的扰动与时延；利用两级级联路由器构建双跳路由实验，将包分发至三个不同接收端，且不进行经典头再生；并以基于时间仓量子比特和双诱骗态BB84协议的QKD包作为实际应用验证。

Abstract: The ability to dynamically route quantum information across multiple nodes is a key requirement for realizing versatile and scalable quantum networks. Quantum routers are devices to achieve packet-level routing beyond static point-to-point connections by handling hybrid packets combining classical headers and quantum payloads. Previously proposed methods have selectively accessed the classical and quantum components of the packets utilizing wavelength splitters and optical switches, and repacked the hybrid packets before transmission through the outgoing link with regenerating classical headers. This work reports a novel optical-fiber-based quantum router that minimizes the disturbance and latency on the hybrid packets through more transparent operation. We experimentally demonstrate double-hop routing via two cascaded routers to three distinct receivers without regeneration of the headers. As a practical application, quantum key distribution packets based on time-bin qubits and a BB84 protocol with a two-decoy-state method are successfully delivered to the three receivers achieving nonzero secret key rates. These results demonstrate the feasibility of extensive quantum networks interconnected by multiple-step router nodes.

</details>

Conclusion: 机器学习解码（PRISM）可为擦除主导的容错量子架构提供高精度、低延迟的推理能力，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation requires decoders that combine accuracy, throughput, low latency, and scalability to large numbers of physical qubits. In photonic fusion-based architectures, erasures from photon loss and probabilistic fusion measurements are a dominant error mechanism, motivating decoders that remain accurate at high erasure rates. We introduce PRISM, a transformer-based machine-learning decoder designed to exploit the known locations of erasures in protocols subject to both Pauli and erasure errors. We benchmark PRISM across a wide range of fault-tolerant protocols based on surface codes, color codes, and quantum low-density parity-check codes, with distances up to 24. Across these benchmarks, PRISM matches the logical error rates of integer-programming-based decoders while being significantly faster, including at error rates up to 50% of threshold. We further optimize the decoder and its real-time hardware implementation to reduce latency while maintaining accuracy. Our optimized implementation on an AMD VP1902 FPGA can decode logical blocks of distance 8 in both space and time, demonstrating a latency of 6.59 microseconds and an amortized decoding time of 1.56 microseconds per logical block is possible. These results show that machine-learning decoding can provide accurate, low-latency inference for erasure-dominated fault-tolerant quantum architectures.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀量子态制备是量子算法的基础原语，但针对任意区间（而非整寄存器或 2 的幂长度）的精确、无辅助比特、门数最优构造尚缺系统性结果，本文旨在填补这一空白并刻画其最优性。

Result: 直接构造在 n≥3 时最坏门数精确为 5n-9，n=1、2 时精确最大值为 1 和 3；下界为 Ω(n)，故在寄存器宽度上渐近最优；逻辑深度上界 O(n)；实例依赖界为 O(log2 M + ε)。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了区间均匀态制备的精确、无辅助比特、渐近最优门数方案，并提供了可经典选择的低门数实例自适应构造，可推广至 XOR 映像与特定等差数列。

Abstract: We consider exact preparation of the uniform quantum state supported on an arbitrary nonempty interval of integer labels in an $n$-qubit register. For every $0\leq a<a+M\leq2^n$, we give an ancilla-free circuit using only one-qubit gates and singly controlled one-qubit gates. In the logical gate model specified in the paper, the direct construction has exact worst-case gate count $5n-9$ for $n\geq3$; its exact maxima for $n=1$ and $n=2$ are $1$ and $3$, respectively. A worst-case lower bound of $\Omega(n)$ establishes asymptotic optimality in the register width. In addition, the gate complexity admits an instance-dependent bound $O(\log_2 M+\epsilon)$, where $\epsilon$ is an offset-dependent parameter defined in terms of binary Hamming weights. We also derive a reflected construction and a classical procedure that selects the lower-count exact circuit. The direct construction admits an $O(n)$ logical-depth upper bound. We also treat bitwise-XOR images of intervals and arithmetic progressions with power-of-two spacing.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前光致偶极-偶极相互作用仅在远场平均场区被观测，偶极共振移动也只在多原子远场情形下报道；作者希望在稠密、近场主导的区域（nλ̄³>1）探索强电偶极-偶极相互作用下的光-原子耦合行为。

Result: 相干偶极-偶极项大于耗散项；单光子发射可传递超过十倍单光子反冲的动量；缀饰偶极矩约 1 Debye；两体共振移动由偶极相互作用的近场部分主导，并被实验观测到。

Conclusion: 结果为光缔合过程提供了新认识，并为研究具有强电偶极-偶极相互作用的驱动量子系统奠定基础。

Abstract: We have prepared pairs of atoms at densities exceeding $ 10^{16}/\rm{cm}^3$ corresponding to $n\lambdabar^3>10$ and studied how they interact with light. In this regime, the coherent part of the electric dipole-dipole interaction is larger than the dissipative part, and a single photon emission can transfer large momenta (larger than ten times the single photon recoil) to the atoms. For off-resonant laser light, the atoms are dressed with an electric dipole moment of $\approx$ 1 Debye and interact coherently. This light-induced dipole-dipole interaction has been previously observed only in the far-field mean-field regime. For two atoms, the resonance is shifted by the two-body Lamb shift which is dominated by the near-field part of the dipolar interaction. Dipolar resonance shifts have been observed before only for many atoms interacting with the far-field part of the dipolar interaction. Our work sheds new light on photoassociation processes and paves the way for studying driven quantum systems with strong electric dipole-dipole interactions.

</details>

Result: 量子先验模型表现出更丰富的内部结构，有效秩更高、潜相似性更好；同时从表征精炼中获益最大。最终检测准确率与精炼后的高斯潜模型相近。

Abstract: Anomaly-based malware detectors learn what benign files look like and flag deviations. Generative approaches to this problem can make use of a "latent" or "prior" data representation, which is often chosen to be a Gaussian distribution. We ask whether the choice of prior distribution matters, and whether one produced by quantum hardware behaves differently from a classical one. Building on SAGEGAN, a style-based detector trained only on benign data, we compare three latent distributions: the original 512-dimensional Gaussian, a 48-dimensional Gaussian, and a 48-dimensional distribution sampled from an ORCA Computing PT-2 photonic quantum processor. We then refine each trained model's frozen representation using greedy and reinforcement-learning methods. Across ten random seeds, the quantum-derived model shows richer internal structure with better effective rank and latent similarity. It also benefits most from refinement. Though final detection accuracy is similar to that of the refined Gaussian latent, these findings show differences in latent geometry and refinement behavior between the trained configurations.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum hardware can provide feedback for training circuit generators beyond the reach of exact classical simulation. At 56-70 qubits the selected circuits use 34-72 times fewer two-qubit gates than the local unitary cluster Jastrow (LUCJ) circuit transpiled for the same device. These results demonstrate that circuit generators can be trained with quantum hardware at 70 qubits.

### [80] [Polynomial Quantum Recurrent Channels for Matrix Inverse Actions: Deferred-Reset Dynamics and Chebyshev Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13778)
*Vahid Tavakkoli,Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出完全正、保迹的多项式递推量子信道用于量子解态制备，并给出定点与时变跟踪界；实验表明递推方法仅有条件性跟踪优势，无通用端到端加速或总成本收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向量子溶液态（solution-state）制备，需要可严格保证完全正性（CP）与保迹性（TP）的递推（recurrent）信道构造，并量化其在定点与随时间变化目标下的跟踪性能与成本，从而判断递推方案是否真的优于每次都重新构造的"fresh"方案。

Method: 基于Chebyshev递推的多项式信道，区分"resident（常驻）"与"fresh（每次新构造）"两种递推方式；用精确约化密度矩阵仿真，在2,880个合成配置上比较精度通过的快照计数，并在360个场景上结合假设的成本模型做成本对比，同时分析逻辑噪声率10^-4下的表现。

Result: 在2,880个配置中，resident Chebyshev递推在608例提升精度通过快照数，2,271例持平，仅1例变差。360场景成本研究中，在全部184个联合可行场景里fresh递推更便宜（resident/fresh成本比中位数3.12）；但resident递推在74例中唯一满足精度要求。逻辑噪声率10^-4时两种递推方法均不达标。

Conclusion: 在精确约化密度仿真与假设成本模型下，递推信道表现出条件性的跟踪优势，但不存在普遍的端到端加速或总成本优势；高逻辑噪声下两类递推方法均失效，其收益依赖于具体场景与成本假设。

Abstract: We develop completely positive, trace-preserving polynomial recurrent channels for quantum solution-state preparation and derive fixed-point and time-varying tracking bounds. Across 2,880 synthetic configurations, resident Chebyshev recurrence improves accuracy-passing snapshot counts in 608 cases, ties in 2,271, and loses in one. A separate 360-scenario cost study shows that fresh recurrence is cheaper in all 184 jointly feasible scenarios (median resident-to-fresh cost ratio: 3.12), while resident recurrence uniquely satisfies accuracy requirements in 74 cases. At a logical noise rate of $10^-4$, neither recurrent method qualifies. Based on exact reduced-density simulations and assumed cost models, our results demonstrate conditional tracking advantages but no general end-to-end speedup or total-cost benefit.

</details>


### [81] [Eigenmode and field imaging of a compact superconducting resonator array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13791)
*Benedek Gaál,Vincent Jouanny,Simone Frasca,Christophe Galland,Pasquale Scarlino,Valentin Goblot*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将激光扫描显微成像（LSM）与NV色心磁强计联用于33个集总元件谐振器构成的蜂窝晶格超导谐振腔阵列，实现本征模与局域微波磁场振幅的空间表征，验证了晶格与设计一致、成像出拓扑边缘态并测得其子晶格极化，并给出相对在位频率无序度约0.3%的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导谐振腔阵列可定制光量子环境，但传统基于端口的微波谱学无法解析其模式丰富的空间结构，缺乏对拓扑边缘态等空间特征的原位表征手段。

Method: 对含33个集总元件谐振器、蜂窝晶格排布并承载拓扑边缘态的二维超导谐振腔阵列，同时采用激光扫描显微成像（LSM）成像本征模，用NV色心磁强计测绘局域微波磁场振幅，并结合两者空间信息进行实验验证与定量分析。

Result: 实验证实所实现晶格与设计一致；成功成像拓扑边缘态并测得子晶格极化；确定相对在位频率无序度上界约0.3%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了适用于紧凑超导谐振腔阵列的联合空间表征方法，可服务于混合自旋—超导量子系统等应用。

Abstract: Superconducting resonator arrays can tailor the photonic environment for quantum information processing, but conventional port-based microwave spectroscopy alone does not resolve the rich spatial structure of their modes. Here, we apply laser scanning microscopy (LSM) and nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center magnetometry to a two-dimensional superconducting resonator array consisting of 33 lumped-element resonators arranged in a honeycomb lattice hosting topological edge states. We demonstrate the complementarity of the two techniques: LSM images the eigenmodes, while NV magnetometry maps the local microwave magnetic field amplitude. Using this spatial information, we experimentally validate that the realized lattice matches the design, we image the topological edge state and measure its sublattice polarization, and place an upper bound of $\approx0.3\%$ on the relative on-site frequency disorder in the lattice. Our work establishes a combined spatial characterization approach for compact superconducting resonator arrays with applications in hybrid spin--superconducting quantum systems.

</details>


### [82] [Analog quantum approximate optimization algorithm for solving the weighted MaxCut problem on three-dimensional single-atom Rydberg array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13796)
*Junxi Chen,I. I. Beterov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文理论研究了基于三维囚禁里德堡原子阵列的模拟QAOA方案，用于求解加权MaxCut问题，并结合离散量子退火与GNN热启动两种参数优化策略，在无噪声情形下几乎全部实例优于GW舍入，并在Aquila真实误差模型下评估了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权MaxCut是组合优化中的典型难题，需要高效且可实验落地的近似求解方案；里德堡原子阵列具备可编程相互作用与全局激光驱动的优势，但其模拟QAOA在加权图上的参数优化与真实噪声下的表现尚不清晰。

Method: 采用三维排布的独立囚禁里德堡原子，由全局寻址激光脉冲驱动，在里德堡共振附近施加位点相关失谐；将问题权重编码为通过选择三维原子位置实现的原子间相互作用能。以20个含12个顶点的随机加权3-正则图作为基准，比较离散化量子退火与基于图神经网络（GNN）的热启动初始化两种参数优化策略，并分析采样统计与总脉冲时间成本，最后用Aquila中性原子处理器的真实误差模型在量子轨迹上模拟优化后调度。

Result: 组合式优化策略（量子退火+ GNN热启动）形成参数组合方案，在无噪声情形下几乎全部实例上优于Goemans-Williamson舍入；文中给出两种方法的采样统计与脉冲时间成本对比，并在Aquila真实误差模型下评估了优化调度的性能。

Conclusion: 三维里德堡原子阵列可实现权重编码的模拟QAOA，混合优化策略在加权MaxCut上具有超越经典GW近似的潜力，但实际性能受采样统计与脉冲时间成本及硬件噪声制约，需在真实误差模型下进一步权衡。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate an analog implementation of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) for the weighted maximum cut problem (MaxCut) problem. The protocol uses a three-dimensional array of individually trapped Rydberg atoms driven by globally addressed laser pulses, with site-dependent detunings applied near the Rydberg resonance. As benchmarks, we consider twenty randomly weighted 3-regular graphs with 12 vertices each. Problem weights are encoded directly in the interatomic interaction energies by choosing appropriate three-dimensional atom positions. We compare two parameter-optimization strategies: discretized quantum annealing and warm-start initialization using a graph neural network (GNN). The combined optimization strategy creates a portfolio that outperforms the Goemans-Williamson (GW) rounding almost in all instances in the noiseless case. We analyze the sampling statistics and the total pulse-time cost of both approaches. Finally, we emulate the performance of the optimized schedules on quantum trajectories using a realistic error model of the Aquila neutral-atom quantum processor.

</details>


### [83] [Coherent Optical Readout of Neutral-Atom Qubits via Homodyne Detection of Resonant Fluorescence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13799)
*Minho Jang,Jaeyong Hwang,Minhyeok Kim,Sanghyo Park,Donggyu Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出用光电二极管平衡零拍探测实现中性原子量子比特的相干快速读出，理论模型与三维蒙特卡洛模拟表明可在100 μs内实现超过99%的态分辨保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复采样、电路中测量与容错中性原子量子处理需要快速、非破坏性的量子比特读出，但基于相机的荧光探测存在超出荧光收集时间的电子学延迟，成为速度瓶颈。

Method: 提出对态依赖共振荧光进行平衡零拍探测（相干读出）的方案；建立解析模型，定量刻画原子运动对时间积分正交信号与噪声的影响——简谐运动导致相位平均，光子反冲引起依赖轨迹的信号涨落；并由信号积累与运动退相干之间的竞争确定最优探测时间，最后用三维蒙特卡洛模拟验证模型。

Result: 三维蒙特卡洛模拟验证了解析模型，并在实验可实现的参数下预测100 μs内态检测保真度超过99%。

Conclusion: 结果确立了中性原子量子比特快速相干荧光读出的读出条件与物理约束，为相干读出方案的设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Fast, non-destructive qubit readout is essential for repeated sampling, mid-circuit measurement, and fault-tolerant neutral-atom quantum processing, but camera-based fluorescence detection introduces electronic latency beyond fluorescence-collection time. We propose coherent readout of the state-dependent resonant fluorescence using balanced homodyne detection with photodiodes. An analytic model quantifies the effect of atomic motion on the time-integrated quadrature signal and noise, showing that harmonic motion causes phase averaging while photon recoils produce trajectory-dependent signal fluctuations; the competition between signal accumulation and motion-induced decoherence sets an optimal detection time. Three-dimensional Monte Carlo simulations validate the model and predict state-detection fidelities exceeding 99% within 100 $\mu\mathrm{s}$ for experimentally realistic parameters. These results establish the readout conditions and physical constraints for rapid coherent fluorescence readout of neutral-atom qubits.

</details>


### [84] [Adaptive Policy-Guided Error Mitigation (APGEM): A Unified Learning-Based Framework for Noise Resilience in Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13815)
*Bisma Majid,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Mir Mohammad Yousuf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出广义自适应策略引导误差缓解（APGEM），将误差缓解技术的选择建模为在线决策，用单一上下文bandit控制器依据变分算法自身的反馈信号逐步选择最优缓解技术，在5类学习范式、80种算法-噪声条件下达到oracle效用的95.5%，在8台真实IBM设备噪声模型上达99.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际硬件上，误差缓解方法通常被手工选定一次并贯穿整个实验周期，而量子机器学习训练过程中电路与噪声会持续漂移，导致某一阶段的最优策略在另一阶段失效，固定选择方式与QML场景不匹配。

Method: 将缓解技术的选择视为在线决策问题：单个上下文bandit控制器观测任意变分算法本就会产生的信号（保真度、熵、奖励或损失、梯度行为），在每一步从零噪声外推（ZNE）、概率误差消除（PEC）、测量误差缓解（MEM）、动态解耦（DD）和电路优化中选择一种。由于仅依赖共享反馈接口，框架可无缝迁移到监督、无监督、强化及混合量子-经典学习。

Result: 在5类范式、80种算法-噪声组合上，自适应选择达到始终选择最优技术的oracle的95.5%，显著优于全部6种固定策略（Friedman χ²=92.18，p≈1.1×10⁻¹⁷；成对Wilcoxon p<4×10⁻⁶）。学习到的策略具有噪声感知性，其偏好技术随去极化与读出误差升高而转移。在8台真实IBM设备的噪声模型上，APGEM几乎精确跟踪oracle（oracle效用的99.9%），并在3至8量子比特范围内保持约1.3×10⁴次采样的恒定开销。

Conclusion: 误差缓解应作为随训练动态演化的在线决策而非一次性静态配置；共享反馈驱动的上下文bandit方案通用、跨范式有效、可泛化至真实设备噪声，且开销可控。

Abstract: Error mitigation stands between variational quantum algorithms and useful results on today's noisy hardware, yet in practice a mitigation method is usually chosen once, by hand, and applied unchanged for the life of an experiment. This is a poor fit for quantum machine learning (QML), where the circuit and the noise drift as training proceeds, so the best strategy in one regime is often wrong in another. We present a generalized form of Adaptive Policy-Guided Error Mitigation (APGEM), which treats the choice of mitigation technique as an online decision made from an algorithm's own feedback. A single contextual-bandit controller observes signals any variational algorithm already produces, such as fidelity, entropy, reward or loss, and gradient behavior, and selects among Zero-Noise Extrapolation, Probabilistic Error Cancellation, Measurement Error Mitigation, Dynamical Decoupling, and circuit optimization per step. Because it depends only on this shared feedback interface, the framework applies unchanged across supervised, unsupervised, reinforcement, and hybrid quantum-classical learning. Evaluated on five paradigms across 80 algorithm-and-noise conditions, adaptive selection reaches 95.5% of an oracle that always picks the best technique and significantly outperforms all six fixed strategies (Friedman $\chi^{2}=92.18$, $p\approx1.1\times10^{-17}$; pairwise Wilcoxon $p<4\times10^{-6}$). The learned policy is noise-aware: its favored technique shifts as depolarizing and readout error rise. On noise models from eight real IBM devices, APGEM tracks the oracle almost exactly (99.9% of oracle utility) at a flat cost of roughly $1.3\times10^{4}$ shots from three to eight qubits.

</details>


### [85] [Correlations carried and controlled by coupled non-Markovian environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13900)
*Stuart Flannigan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出"局域纯态层级"(local hierarchy of pure states)方法，用于模拟与空间相关、非马尔可夫且模式间可交换激发的环境耦合的量子系统，并以局域Rabi自旋—腔链为平台，研究环境相干输运与局域反馈如何调控远端自旋间的关联与纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境既能保留量子信息，又能在空间分离的系统间传递信息，因此描述其联合影响需同时处理"环境记忆"与"空间关联"；现有方法多单独处理非马尔可夫性或空间相关性，缺乏数值精确的统一框架。

Method: 构建局域纯态层级：将整体波函数按空间局域模式（各腔）展开为随机纯态集合，以分层随机微分方程精确刻画空间相关、非马尔可夫且模式间存在激发交换的环境；应用于局域耦合的Rabi自旋—腔对链（被消去的腔环境中的光子跳跃产生自旋间关联），并对中间腔进行监测、将测量记录反馈至对应自旋；同时用张量网络模拟XXZ与Heisenberg链。

Result: 腔环境的光子跳跃使原本隔离的自旋间产生关联与纠缠；对中间腔的监测与反馈进一步增强无直接反馈的端点自旋间关联；反馈启动后的延迟响应与反馈停止后纠缠继续增长，支持"环境相干动力学将早期反馈效应输运至端点自旋"的机制；张量网络模拟显示环境输运会改变多体关联模式。

Conclusion: 耦合环境与局域反馈可有效控制远距离自旋间的关联；所提供的数值精确随机纯态框架可用于研究相互作用非马尔可夫多体系统的相干与耗散调控。

Abstract: An environment can retain quantum information and carry it between spatially separated systems. Describing the combined influence of these processes requires a treatment of both environmental memory and spatial correlations. Here, we formulate a local hierarchy of pure states for quantum systems coupled to spatially correlated, non-Markovian environments whose modes themselves exchange excitations. We use this method to describe the reduced spin dynamics of a chain of locally coupled Rabi spin--cavity pairs motivated by cavity-QED implementations, where photon hopping in the eliminated cavity environment generates correlations and entanglement between otherwise isolated spins. Monitoring intermediate cavities and feeding their measurement records back onto the corresponding spins further enhances correlations between endpoint spins that receive no direct feedback. The delayed response when feedback starts, and continued entanglement growth after it stops, support a mechanism in which coherent environmental dynamics carry the effects of earlier feedback to the endpoint spins. Finally, tensor-network simulations of XXZ and Heisenberg chains show how environmental transport changes many-body correlation patterns. These results show how coupled environments and local feedback can control correlations between distant spins. We provide a numerically exact stochastic pure-state framework for simulating these processes, opening up avenues to explore the coherent and dissipative control of interacting non-Markovian many-body systems.

</details>


### [86] [Reservoir-induced Quantum Phase Transitions in spin ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13934)
*Giuseppe Musolino,Daniele Lamberto,Gabriele Orlando,Omar Di Stefano,Salvatore Savasta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究N个二能级原子与玻色库耦合、处于热力学极限下的开放量子系统：当系统-库相互作用无亚稳极小（无抵消项情形）时，系统可发生由库结构、而非相干内部动力学决定的量子相变；用精确量子朗之万方程（在临界附近过阻尼区仍有效）通过复激发谱定位临界点，并给出闭式反射/透射谱，显示模式软化和趋于转变时日益不对称的线型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究开放量子系统中相变的驱动机制：区分库结构（耗散/环境谱）与内部相干动力学在决定量子相变中的作用，并处理临界附近损耗率超过软化模式共振频率的过阻尼困难区域。

Method: 考虑热力学极限下N个二能级原子集合与玻色库耦合；在无抵消项（无亚稳极小）情形下，采用精确量子朗之万方程（在临界附近过阻尼区仍成立），以复激发谱定位临界点，并推导闭式反射与透射谱。

Result: 当系统-库相互作用缺乏亚稳极小时，系统仍可发生由库结构决定的量子相变；复激发谱给出临界点位置；反射/透射谱呈闭式解，随接近相变出现模式软化与逐渐增强的线型不对称性。

Conclusion: 库结构本身即可驱动开放原子系综的量子相变，无需依赖相干内部动力学或抵消项提供的亚稳极小；精确朗之万框架在过阻尼临界区依然适用，并为临界软化和不对称谱线提供可解析预测。

Abstract: In this work, we present a comprehensive analysis of an open quantum system consisting of a collection of N two-level atoms in the thermodynamic limit coupled to bosonic reservoirs. Our results demonstrate that, when the system-bath interactions lacks a metastable minimum, as in the counterterm-free case, the system can undergo a quantum phase transition set by the reservoir structure rather than by coherent internal dynamics. Using exact quantum Langevin equations, valid even in the overdamped regime near criticality, where the loss rates can exceed the resonance frequency of the softening mode, we locate the critical point via the complex excitation spectrum and obtain closed-form reflection and transmission spectra that display the mode softening and an increasingly asymmetric lineshape near the transition.

</details>


### [87] [Multiparameter quantum estimation of circulant networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13948)
*Maria Popovic,Silvia Cassina,Ilaria Gianani,Claudia Benedetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由连续时间量子行走探测的循环网络中耦合强度的多参数量子估计，推导量子Fisher信息矩阵，确定最优探测态与量子极限精度及其随未知耦合数的标度，并分析位置测量（含有限分辨率粗粒化探测）所能达到的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络可自然描述相互作用模式、传输介质与通信架构，准确刻画其耦合结构是量子技术中的关键挑战；需要系统刻画基于量子行走的网络推断中耦合估计的基本极限与实际极限。

Method: 以连续时间量子行走为探针，针对循环（circulant）网络中的多个耦合强度进行多参数量子估计：推导量子Fisher信息矩阵（QFIM），求解最优探测态与量子Cramér-Rao界；进而对行走者位置测量给出经典Cramér-Rao界，比较最优探测态与局域探测态的表现；最后用粗粒化位置测量建模有限分辨率探测。

Result: 得到QFIM的解析表达、最优探测态及量子测量允许的极限精度，并给出该精度随未知耦合数的标度关系；位置测量的经典精度界显示最优与局域探测态之间的差异；粗粒化通常降低估计精度，但某些情形下低分辨率测量反而优于更细空间分辨率的测量。

Conclusion: 研究系统刻画了量子行走网络推断中耦合估计的基本极限（量子）与实际极限（位置测量、有限分辨率），并表明分辨率并非越高越优，为网络耦合参数的实验估计方案设计提供依据。

Abstract: Networks provide a natural description of interaction patterns, transport media, and communication architectures. Accurately characterizing their coupling structure is a key challenge in quantum technologies. We investigate the multiparameter quantum estimation of coupling strengths in circulant networks probed by continuous-time quantum walks. We derive the quantum Fisher information matrix, identify the optimal probe states and determine the ultimate precision allowed by quantum measurements, together with its scaling with the number of unknown couplings. We further analyze the precision achievable through position measurements of the walker, comparing the corresponding classical Cramér-Rao bounds for optimal and localized probe states with the quantum limit. Finally, we consider finite-resolution detection modeled by coarse-grained position measurements. Although coarse graining generally reduces estimation precision, we find that lower-resolution measurements can, in some instances, outperform other measurements with finer spatial resolution. Our results provide a systematic characterization of the fundamental and practical limits of coupling estimation in quantum-walk-based network inference.

</details>


### [88] [Quantum propagation of vibrational excitation in harmonically confined trapped-ion crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13967)
*Takumi Yuri,Yu-Xuan Chen,Kenji Toyoda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在一维10离子阱晶体中实验研究局域振动激发（局域声子）的传播，观察到离子间距不均匀性导致的近邻跳跃与整体运动特征，实验与数值模拟细致吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子阱中的振动激发（声子）是量子模拟与量子计算的有用资源，局域声子可用于模拟在系统中传播的量子粒子；本文旨在实验探究一维离子晶体中局域振动激发的传播行为。

Method: 将10个离子束缚于简谐势中形成一维离子晶体，制备局域振动激发并观测其在链中的传播，同时与数值模拟结果进行对比。

Result: 离子间距的不均匀性导致特征性行为，如近邻跳跃（adjacent hopping）与整体运动（global movement）；实验结果与数值模拟在细微层面也保持一致。

Conclusion: 该平台有望推广至玻色子多体系统及更复杂的准粒子研究。

Abstract: Vibrational excitations, or phonons, in trapped ions serve as useful resources for quantum information processing, including quantum simulation and computing. Localized excitations of ions' vibrational motion (local phonons) can be used for simulating quantum particles that propagate over a system. Here, we experimentally investigate the propagation of a localized vibrational excitation in a one-dimensional trapped-ion crystal. A 10-ion crystal confined in a harmonic potential is used. It is found that inhomogeneity in the spacing between the ions leads to characteristic behaviors such as adjacent hopping and global movement. A comparison of the experimental results with numerical simulations shows agreement up to fine details. The system explored here may be extended to many-body systems of bosonic particles as well as more complex quasiparticles.

</details>


### [89] [Maximal Violation of Mermin Inequality for Commuting von Neumann Algebras and Multipartite Vacuum Entanglement in QFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13981)
*Kan He,Jinchuan Hou,Shuyuan Yang,Bingke Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Summers–Werner关于两体CHSH不等式在相对论量子场论真空中被极大破坏的判据（等价于强稳定性）推广到三个及以上相互对易的von Neumann代数，用多体Mermin不等式给出判据：三体情形下"每个正规态上的极大破坏"等价于存在共同的渐近GHZ稳定子序列；各局部因子的强稳定性是必要条件，嵌入无限GHZ张量积是充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Summers与Werner已证明CHSH型Bell不等式在相对论量子场真空态中被极大且普遍地破坏，但其判据只涉及两体情形（一个代数与其交换子）。多体情形下相应的刻画尚不清楚，因此作者研究三个及更多相互对易von Neumann代数上的对应问题，并以多体Mermin不等式替代CHSH不等式作为分析工具。

Method: 以三体Mermin泛函为主要对象，将"每个正规态上均达到极大破坏"这一性质与算子代数/模态结构性质相联系：分析共同渐近GHZ稳定子序列的存在性，考察各局部因子强稳定性的必要性，并通过构造反例检验强稳定性、type III_1性质及三体对偶性等条件是否充分；结果进一步推广到n≥3体。

Result: 对三体Mermin泛函证明：在每个正规态上极大破坏 ⇔ 存在共同的渐近GHZ稳定子序列。各局部因子的强稳定性是极大破坏的必要条件；嵌入一个无限GHZ张量积是充分条件。反例表明，即使各代数的强稳定性与type III_1性质同时成立、并辅以三体对偶性，也不足以得到极大破坏。

Conclusion: 多体Mermin不等式的极大破坏并不由局部因子的强稳定性（乃至type III_1与三体对偶性）保证，其本质判据是共同渐近GHZ稳定子序列的存在性，这构成Summers–Werner两体结果的严格多体推广；相关结论可延伸至n≥3体情形。

Abstract: Summers and Werner proved that Bell inequalities are violated maximally and generically in the vacuum of a relativistic quantum field theory in [S. J. Summers and R. Werner, Commun. Math. Phys. 110 (1987), 247-259]. Their criterion concerns two-party case, that is, there are two algebras of quantum observables: one algebra and its commutant. In this case, the maximal CHSH value is converged to in every normal state exactly when the algebra is strongly stable. In the paper, we study the corresponding question for three and more mutually commuting von Neumann algebras. We focus on the multipartite Mermin inequalities instead of CHSH ones. For the tripartite Mermin functional we show that maximal violation in every normal state is equivalent to the existence of a common sequence of asymptotic GHZ stabilizers. Strong stability of each local factor is an necessary condition for maximal violation, and a joint embedding of an infinite GHZ tensor product is sufficient. Counterexamples show that strong stability and type III_1 of the individual algebras, even together with tripartite duality, do not suffice to arrive at the maximal violation. The results are also extended to n>=3 parties.

</details>


### [90] [Pool-free, residual-triggered ansatz growth for variational imaginary-time evolution: when a fixed operator order beats adaptive screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13998)
*Yu-Hao Chen,Renata Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出无池化的动态VITE：层由哈密顿量的嵌套对易子预先确定并按对称性与粒子数分组，当相对McLachlan残差超过阈值时追加下一层；在五个非可积体系上以1.6–5.4倍更少的线路数达到10^-4相对误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADAPT-VQE、AVQITE等自适应变分算法在每一步增长都要对算子池进行筛选，ADAPT-VQE还要重新优化全部参数，开销较大。作者希望找到一种无需算子池的替代方案，降低变分虚时演化（VITE）的成本。

Method: 构造无池化动态VITE：演化层事先固定，由哈密顿量的嵌套对易子生成并按对称性与粒子数分组；不进行池筛选与全局重优化，而是以相对McLachlan残差为判据，超过阈值时追加下一层。与6–8种自适应变体（单Pauli弦池、量子比特激发池、批量ADAPT-VQE等）对比。

Result: 在混合场Ising链与环面以及拉伸H4五个非可积体系上，相对误差达10^-4所需线路数比最廉价的自适应变体少1.6–5.4倍；链上优势在8至16格点范围内保持。自适应方法的大部分成本来自筛选与重优化。纵向场扫描表明优势依赖可积性破缺（使自适应筛选变贵）；在横场Ising模型、10^-6的严格目标以及前段堆叠弱层的调度下优势消失。

Conclusion: 固定嵌套对易子层的无池化VITE可作为自适应变分算法的高效替代，其收益源于省去池筛选与重优化，但以可积性破缺、较宽松的精度目标和合理的层序为前提，适用边界需明示。

Abstract: Adaptive variational algorithms such as ADAPT-VQE and AVQITE screen an operator pool at every growth step, and ADAPT-VQE also re-optimizes all parameters. We study a pool-free alternative for variational imaginary-time evolution (VITE). The layers are fixed in advance by nested commutators of the Hamiltonian, grouped by symmetry and particle number, and the next layer is appended when the relative McLachlan residual exceeds a threshold. On five non-integrable systems (the mixed-field Ising chain and tori, and stretched H$_4$), this dynamic VITE reaches a relative error of $10^{-4}$ with 1.6-5.4 times fewer circuits than the cheapest of six to eight adaptive variants, including single-Pauli-string and qubit-excitation pools and batched ADAPT-VQE. On the chain the advantage persists from 8 to 16 sites. Screening and re-optimization account for most of the adaptive methods' cost. On the spin models, a longitudinal-field scan shows that the advantage requires broken integrability, which makes adaptive screening expensive. The advantage is lost on transverse-field Ising models, at tight targets (relative error $10^{-6}$) and where the schedule front-loads weak layers.

</details>


### [91] [Normalizing Flows for Classical Simulation of Quantum Circuits in Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14006)
*Edward Jiang,Timothy Heightman,Marcin Płodzień*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于多体系统相空间表述的量子线路表示学习框架：将N量子比特态视为2N维光滑流形上的概率分布，酉门参数化为分布间的归一化流，从而用生成模型模拟量子线路，在大规模体系下精度超过现有SOTA神经网络方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络类量子线路模拟方法在大规模体系下精度不足，需要更有效的线路表示与模拟途径。

Method: 利用相空间（Wigner/准概率）表述，把N量子比特态映射为2N维流形上的概率分布；将酉门刻画为该分布之间的归一化流（normalizing flow），从而以生成模型形式实现线路建模与仿真；在三类常见量子线路族上做评估。

Result: 在体系规模较大时，精度优于当前最先进的神经网络方法。

Conclusion: 相空间+归一化流的建模范式是量子线路模拟的有效新方向，具备良好的规模化潜力。

Abstract: We present a novel framework to learn a representation of quantum circuits based on the phase space formulation of many-body systems. Given that $N$-qubit states can be represented as probability distributions on a smooth $2N$-dimensional manifold, we show how unitary gates can be parameterized as normalizing flows between these distributions, opening a new avenue for quantum circuit simulation using generative models. We evaluate our method on three common families of quantum circuits, and show that its accuracy exceeds that of current state-of-the-art neural network methods for large system sizes.

</details>


### [92] [PINNverse meets Jordan--Wigner: learning the Hamiltonian of long spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14023)
*Gubio G De Lima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于Jordan-Wigner变换的PINNverse方法，将逆物理信息神经网络的输出规模从4^N降为N^2，配合含时Hartree-Fock闭包处理寄生ZZ耦合，在合成数据上成功反演至50量子比特自旋链的哈密顿量参数，耦合学习误差优于0.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆物理信息神经网络（PINNverse）从可观测量期望值反演量子哈密顿量参数时，网络输出维度随量子比特数N以4^N指数增长，使长链反演在计算上不可行。作者希望消除这一指数开销，使逆问题可扩展到大规模自旋链。

Method: 针对具最近邻交换相互作用的自旋链，利用Jordan-Wigner变换使动力学封闭在N×N的单体密度矩阵上，网络输出仅需N^2个量；训练数据为五种全局设置下测得的局域可观测量。寄生ZZ耦合（串扰）通过含时Hartree-Fock闭包纳入模型。网络先求解运动方程，再反演哈密顿量参数；对长链局域频率误差，进一步以网络估计为初值做直接最小二乘拟合进行修正。

Result: 在合成数据上，同一网络可先求解运动方程，随后恢复多达50个量子比特链的哈密顿量参数，耦合参数学习精度优于0.1%；长链中局域频率的系统误差经最小二乘后处理被消除。

Conclusion: Jordan-Wigner单体化表述可将PINNverse的输出复杂度由4^N降至N^2，使逆哈密顿量学习可扩展到数十量子比特规模；结合Hartree-Fock闭包处理串扰、以及网络初值加最小二乘精修的两步策略，可实现高精度的参数反演。

Abstract: Inverse physics-informed neural networks (PINNverse) learn the parameters of a quantum Hamiltonian from measured expectation values, but the network output grows as $4^N$ with the number of qubits $N$. We show that, for spin chains with nearest-neighbor exchange interactions, the Jordan-Wigner transformation removes this exponential cost: the dynamics closes on an $N\times N$ one-body density matrix, so the network needs only $N^2$ outputs and the data are local observables measured in five global settings. Parasitic $ZZ$ couplings (crosstalk) are included through a time-dependent Hartree--Fock closure. With synthetic data, the same network first solves the equation of motion and then recovers the Hamiltonian parameters of chains of up to $50$ qubits, with couplings learned to better than $0.1\%$. Errors in the local frequencies of long chains are removed by a direct least-squares fit started from the network estimate.

</details>


### [93] [CV-TF-QKD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14044)
*Stefano Pirandola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将孪生场(TF)思想推广到连续变量(CV)QKD，采用独立高斯调制相干态与不可信单光子中继，证明全记录对账与二进制相位公开两种方案在集体攻击下渐近密钥率近似相等，且单中继每臂速率按√η标度；对相位公开方案给出可组合安全性(应对一般相干攻击)，有限码长计算预测超越无中继纯损耗与匹配热损耗界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TF-QKD的"孪生场优势"（突破 repeaterless 界限、√η 标度）此前主要在离散变量框架下实现；作者希望将其扩展到高斯调制连续变量量子密码，用现成的相干态源与单光子中继获得同等增益。

Method: 构造CV版TF-QKD：两臂使用独立高斯调制相干态，经不可信单光子中继（测量/干涉）后成码。设计两条对账路线：(1) 全记录对账(full-record reconciliation)；(2) 二进制相位公开(binary phase-disclosure)协议，配合有界的制备强度检测与符号宇称互补性(sign-parity complementarity)论证，建立针对一般相干攻击的可组合安全性。进一步做含探测器低效、暗计数、相位漂移与参考脉冲开销的有限码长计算。

Result: 在固定单臂输入参照过量噪声下，两种协议的每中继每臂密钥率均按 √η 标度（η 为端到端透射率）；渐近集体攻击下两者速率近似相等。相位公开方案获得一般相干攻击下的可组合安全性。给定器件参数时，有限码长密钥率超过纯损耗与匹配热损耗的 repeaterless 界限。

Conclusion: 孪生场优势可成功移植到高斯调制CV量子密码，兼具√η 标度与超越 repeaterless 界限的性能，为CV-QKD的长距离/中继架构提供了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous-variable version of twin-field quantum key distribution, using independent Gaussian-modulated coherent states and an untrusted single-photon relay. Full-record reconciliation and a binary phase-disclosure protocol achieve nearly equal asymptotic key rates against collective attacks. At fixed weak input-referred excess noise per arm, both rates per relay use scale as $\sqrt{\eta}$, where $\eta$ is the end-to-end transmissivity. For phase disclosure, we establish composable security against general coherent attacks using a bounded test of preparation intensities and sign-parity complementarity. For specified apparatus parameters, finite-key calculations incorporating detector inefficiency, dark counts, phase fluctuations, and reference overhead predict rates exceeding the pure-loss and matched thermal-loss repeaterless bounds. These results extend the twin-field advantage to Gaussian-modulated continuous-variable quantum cryptography.

</details>


### [94] [Dynamical GKP codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14081)
*Jonathan Pelletier,Baptiste Royer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"动力学GKP码"，将动力学（测量序列）编码形式从量子比特推广到连续变量，用缩放辛格将GKP码推广为子系统GKP码，并移植祖先/探测器/良性误差机制，给出由持久稳定子格与有界推断窗口高效计算syndrome与距离的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学码（以测量序列保护量子信息）的既有形式体系仅针对量子比特，而GKP码虽是有望实现量子计算的连续变量编码方案，却缺乏对应的动力学形式体系。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 用缩放辛格（scaled symplectic lattices）将GKP码推广为子系统GKP码；2) 在此基础上移植动力学量子比特码的ancestry（祖系）、detector（探测器）与benign-error（良性误差）机制，得到测量序列的更新规则与距离；3) 建立由持久稳定子格与有界推断窗口出发、通用且高效地计算动力学GKP码syndrome与距离的方法；4) 以双模square-hexagonal Floquet GKP码作示例，并分析具有无理辛内积的测量。

Result: 双模square-hexagonal Floquet GKP码相比square与hexagonal GKP码测量权重更低，同时兼具二者逻辑门集合，更易实现完整单比特Clifford群；具有无理辛内积的测量（离散量子比特码中无对应物）在单模情形下退化为破坏性零差探测（destructive homodyne detection）。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子比特码的重要工具推广到连续变量系统，并在离散稳定子测量与连续零差探测之间建立了新的桥梁。

Abstract: Dynamical codes protect quantum information with sequences of measurements, generalizing the stabilizer formalism of static codes. However, the dynamical formalism has been developed exclusively for qubit systems. While Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes are promising candidates for quantum computing that encode qubits in the continuous phase space of oscillators, no dynamical formalism has been developed for them. In this work, we bridge this gap and introduce dynamical GKP codes, combining continuous-variable lattice encodings with time-dependent translation measurement dynamics. To obtain the update rules and the distance of a given measurement sequence, we first generalize GKP codes to subsystem GKP codes using scaled symplectic lattices. Building on this structure, we adapt the ancestry, detector, and benign-error machinery originally developed for dynamical qubit codes. From this construction, we obtain a general and efficient method to compute the syndrome and distance of a dynamical GKP code from a persistent stabilizer lattice and a bounded inference window. We illustrate the framework with the example of the two-mode square-hexagonal Floquet GKP code. This code has reduced measurement weight compared to both the square and hexagonal GKP codes, while combining their set of logical gates, resulting in easier implementation of the full single-qubit Clifford group. Finally, we show that measurements with irrational symplectic overlap, which have no analog in discrete qubit codes, reduce to destructive homodyne detection in the single-mode case. These results extend an important qubit-code tool to continuous-variable systems and identify a new bridge between discrete stabilizer measurements and continuous homodyne detection.

</details>


### [95] [Cross-Platform Benchmarking of GHZ-State Generation in Quantum Processors via Cloud Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14114)
*Sergio Hernández,Unai Aseguinolaza,David Aguirre,Marta Gili,Joseba Hernández,Guillermo Valverde,Uxue Ballarin Manso,Uxue Auzmendi,Iker Pastor,Pablo García Bringas,Ane Blázquez García,Rubén Peña,Mikel Sanz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用QuSquare套件的多体纠缠基准，通过云访问在默认编译、无误差缓解条件下评测5家厂商9款超导与离子阱量子处理器，发现可用物理比特数与通过基准的最大GHZ态尺寸之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件平台技术路线与运行特性各异，需要基准测试来识别各处理器的优势、比较性能，并为面向具体应用选择平台提供依据；同时希望反映终端用户实际体验的性能（云访问、默认编译、无误差缓解）。

Method: 采用QuSquare套件中的多体纠缠基准，对5家厂商的9款商用处理器（超导与离子阱）进行测试；通过远程云服务访问，使用各供应商默认编译且不做误差缓解。基准以直接保真度估计（DFE）估算GHZ态保真度，并搜索在统计误差范围内保真度与真正多体纠缠（GME）阈值F=1/2相容的最大GHZ态尺寸N*。

Result: 物理比特总数与通过基准的最大GHZ态尺寸差距显著：最佳超导处理器分别达到N*=27和N*=24比特，不足其寄存器规模的四分之一；而12比特离子阱处理器以全寄存器通过。中位数校准数据对基准结果预测能力很差；结果强烈依赖统计判定规则——若要求"认证的"GME，能在任一测试尺寸上通过的设备由9台降至4台。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了硬件规模与可验证纠缠能力之间的鸿沟，并表明校准指标不可靠、判定规则需明确。结果为未来跨平台量子处理器基准测试提供了基线。

Abstract: Quantum computing has rapidly evolved into a diverse ecosystem of hardware platforms that differ in their underlying technologies and operational characteristics. Consequently, benchmarking has become essential for identifying the strengths of quantum processors, comparing their performance, and enabling informed decisions when selecting the most appropriate quantum computing platform for specific applications. In this work, we benchmark nine commercially accessible quantum processors from five vendors, spanning superconducting and trapped-ion technologies, using the multipartite entanglement benchmark from the QuSquare suite. All experiments were performed through remote cloud-access services, with the default compilation of each provider and without error mitigation, reflecting the performance experienced by end users. The benchmark estimates the fidelity of Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) states by direct fidelity estimation and searches for the largest GHZ state whose fidelity is compatible, within the statistical error, with the genuine multipartite entanglement (GME) threshold $F=1/2$. Our results reveal a substantial gap between the total number of physical qubits available on a processor and the size of the largest GHZ state passing the benchmark: the best superconducting processors reach $N^*=27$ and $N^*=24$ qubits, less than a quarter of their registers, whereas the 12-qubit trapped-ion processor passes with its full register. We further show that median calibration data are poor predictors of the benchmark outcome, and that the reported sizes depend strongly on the statistical decision rule: requiring certified GME reduces the number of devices that succeed at any tested size from nine to four. These results provide a baseline for future cross-platform benchmarking of quantum processors.

</details>


### [96] [Coherent-Dissipative Crossover of Quantum Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14139)
*Li Liu,Niu Wang,Xun-Wei Xu,Jie-Qiao Liao,Hui Jing,Le-Man Kuang,Changliang Ren*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过对快速中介子耦合的两个量子振子做绝热消除，揭示相干交换与集体耗散两条同步通道，并给出统一解析描述；同步由相干耦合、耗散与库诱导涨落的联合平衡决定，对共享输出的连续监测可将库信息转化为资源，在相干-耗散交叉区最强地增强条件同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放系统中的量子同步既可源于相干相互作用，也可源于集体耗散，但二者的相互作用机制尚不清楚，缺乏统一框架描述这两类通道及其过渡行为。

Method: 建立由快速中介子耦合两个量子振子的一般理论框架；通过绝热消除得到相干交换与集体耗散两条独立同步通道，推导连接耗散主导与相干主导区的解析表达式；并分析对共享输出通道进行连续监测所引入的条件化动力学。

Result: 同步程度由相干耦合、耗散与库诱导涨落的联合平衡决定，而非仅由相干耦合决定；对共享输出的连续监测能把库信息转化为可利用资源，显著增强条件同步，且在相干-耗散交叉点附近增益最强。

Conclusion: 研究确立了相干-耗散量子同步的一般机制，并展示了开放量子系统中由测量赋能的可控同步，为统一理解相干与耗散协同调控同步提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Quantum synchronization in open systems can arise from both coherent interactions and collective dissipation, yet their interplay remains poorly understood. Here we develop a general framework for two quantum oscillators coupled through a fast mediator. Adiabatic elimination reveals two distinct synchronization channels, coherent exchange and collective dissipation, and yields an analytical description connecting dissipation and coherence-dominated regimes. We show that synchronization is governed by the joint balance of coherent coupling, dissipation, and reservoir-induced fluctuations, rather than by coherent coupling alone. Continuous monitoring of the shared output further converts reservoir information into a resource, substantially enhancing conditional synchronization, with the strongest gain near the coherent-dissipative crossover. These results establish a general mechanism for coherent-dissipative quantum synchronization and its measurement-enabled control in open quantum systems.

</details>


### [97] [Multiparticle entanglement in a time-dependent electron--ion--photon system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14142)
*Axel Stenquist,Jan Marcus Dahlström*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究光电离过程中电子-离子-光子复合体系的纠缠动力学：初始电子-离子纠缠经真正的多体纠缠态，最终趋近电子-光子纠缠，并揭示瞬态离子-光子纠缠与高维纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光电离为时域纠缠转移提供了天然场景：出射电子可与剩余离子纠缠，随后的自发辐射可能将关联转移到光子。作者希望定量刻画这一三步体系中的纠缠流动过程。

Method: 用一对一（one-versus-rest）二分划分的冯·诺依曼熵量化复合体系纠缠动力学；再用对数负度（logarithmic negativity）分辨两两纠缠，并利用其对弱占据Schmidt模式的增强敏感性探测连续谱相关的高维纠缠。

Result: 体系从初始电子-离子纠缠出发，经过一个真正的多粒子纠缠态，最终趋近电子-光子纠缠态；对数负度进一步分辨出两两纠缠，特别是存在瞬态的离子-光子纠缠；其敏感性还揭示出与光子能量连续谱相关的高维纠缠。

Conclusion: 光电离可实现时域纠缠转移，且过程中出现多体纠缠、瞬态离子-光子纠缠及高维纠缠等丰富结构，需结合熵与对数负度等互补度量才能完整刻画。

Abstract: Photoionization provides a natural setting for entanglement transfer in the time domain: an outgoing electron can become entangled with the residual ion, while subsequent spontaneous emission may transfer these correlations to an emitted photon. We quantify the entanglement dynamics of the composite electron--ion--photon system using the von Neumann entropy of the one-versus-rest bipartitions, showing that the initially entangled electron--ion system evolves through a genuinely multiparticle-entangled state before approaching an entangled electron--photon state. Logarithmic negativity further resolves the pairwise entanglement, revealing in particular transient ion--photon entanglement. Further, its enhanced sensitivity to weakly populated Schmidt modes additionally exposes high-dimensional entanglement associated with the photon-energy continuum.

</details>


### [98] [Secure Information Flow in Quantum Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14147)
*Zhicheng Zhang,Mingsheng Ying,Lirong Xia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为量子程序的信息流安全建立语义基础与验证方法：提出基于非干扰的定义和基于模拟的攻击者模型定义并证明二者等价，同时基于量子Hoare逻辑提出"自共轭"证明技术以克服自组合技术在量子场景下因类不可克隆障碍而失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息流安全问题在量子程序中变得更加微妙：量子态可含叠加与纠缠，且经典变量与量子变量的交互复杂，因此需要针对量子程序重新刻画信息流安全并提供可操作的验证手段。

Method: 1) 提出两种安全性定义：非干扰定义（经典概念的推广）与模拟定义（显式建模攻击者，具清晰操作语义），并证明其等价性；2) 基于量子Hoare逻辑构建证明框架；3) 由于标准自组合（self-composition）技术受类不可克隆定理的障碍在量子情形失效，提出"自共轭"技术，利用延迟测量原理与酉操作性质实现安全性的逻辑刻画。

Result: 证明了非干扰与模拟两种安全性刻画的等价性；给出了可用的量子Hoare逻辑验证框架，并在量子一次性密码、量子纠错、匿名纠缠生成等实例上演示了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子程序信息流安全奠定了语义基础并提供了首个（文中语境下）克服不可克隆障碍的逻辑验证方法，自共轭技术可作为量子版本自组合的替代方案，实例验证表明框架具备实用性。

Abstract: Information-flow security aims to prevent the leakage of confidential information through program execution. This problem becomes fundamentally subtler for quantum programs, because their states may involve superposition and entanglement, and their executions may involve intricate interactions between classical and quantum variables. This paper develops semantic foundations and verification methods for secure information flow in quantum programs. We formulate two notions of information-flow security. The first is a noninterference-based definition that naturally generalizes its classical counterpart. The second is a simulation-based definition that explicitly models an attacker, providing a clear operational interpretation of security. We prove that these two notions are equivalent.
 To verify the information-flow security of quantum programs, we develop a proof framework based on quantum Hoare logic. A key challenge is that the standard self-composition technique, which expresses information-flow security in Hoare logic by composing a program with a renamed copy of itself, fails in the quantum setting due to an obstruction analogous to the no-cloning theorem. To overcome this challenge, we introduce self-conjugation, a novel technique that leverages the deferred measurement principle and properties of unitary operations. We demonstrate our framework on examples drawn from the quantum one-time pad, quantum error correction, and anonymous entanglement generation.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Advantage in Assisted Work Distillation from Complementary Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14173)
*Chellasamy Jebarathinam,Debarshi Das,Ray-Kuang Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在热操作资源理论中建立微观热力学取功与非经典关联刻画之间的操作桥梁，证明互不偏基下的并发信息论关联是可量化的取功能量资源，并由此导出一类"功型"EPR导引不等式与可探测超不可导引性的动态标度功不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将量子热力学取功与量子关联（纠缠、导引）分置于不同理论框架，缺乏二者之间的定量操作联系；同时弱关联纠缠态在标准无约束场景下往往不可见，需要新的见证手段。

Method: 在热操作资源理论框架内，构建辅助取功蒸馏的推广模型：将可信接收方系统划分为相互独立的粒子存储池；以多组互不偏基上测得的信息论关联作为取功能源的显式度量；据此推导功型EPR导引不等式，并在单边半设备无关信任框架下限制不可信方维度，构造维度依赖的动态标度功不等式。

Result: 并发互不偏基关联被证明可作为取功的显式可量化能量资源；所导出的功型导引不等式违反可认证单边设备无关的量子热力学增强；动态标度功不等式能够在受限维度下见证超不可导引性，从而激活并认证标准无约束场景中完全不可见的弱关联纠缠态的热力学效用。

Conclusion: 该工作将热力学取功与量子关联判据统一于同一操作框架，表明功本身可作为导引与超不可导引性的见证量，为单边设备无关量子热力学增强及弱关联态的资源激活提供了可操作判据。

Abstract: We establish an operational bridge between microscale thermodynamic work extraction and the foundational characterization of nonclassical correlations in bipartite quantum systems. Within the resource theory of thermal operations, we analyze a generalized framework of assisted work distillation where the trusted receiver's system is divided into separate, independent particle storage pools. We demonstrate that concurrent information-theoretic correlations measured across sets of mutually unbiased bases act as an explicit, quantifiable energy resource for local work extraction. Based on this paradigm, we derive a novel class of work-based Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen steering inequalities whose violations certify a one-sided device-independent quantum thermodynamic enhancement. Furthermore, by restricting the dimension of the untrusted party under a one-sided semi-device-independent trust framework, we introduce dynamically scaling work inequalities capable of witnessing superunsteerability. This allows us to activate and certify the hidden thermodynamic utility of weakly correlated entangled states that are otherwise completely invisible in standard, unconstrained scenarios.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum-Enhanced QSAR for DYRK1A: Evaluating Complementary and Utility QPU-Derived Molecular Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14179)
*Alejandro Giraldo,Daniel Ruiz,Mariano Caruso,Hamed Mohammadbagherpoor,Vladyslav Bohun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在同一DYRK1A数据集上，分别以表格分子描述符和量子处理器测得向量作为输入、使用相同的RBF-SVM，两种模型的命中推荐集合并不一致：各自的活性分子互为遗漏，因此会把不同的化学系列送入实验评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中实验筛选能力有限，hit identification所选的化合物决定了哪些化学系列得以进入确认与类似物研究；未被测定的hit则其系列不再开发。作者因此关注一个实践性问题：在完全相同的建模流程下，不同的分子表示是否会推荐同一批化合物去实验。

Method: 对DYRK1A激酶，使用相同分子集合，分别构建两种表示：一是表格形式的分子描述符，二是在量子处理器上测得的向量；两者均用相同的RBF-SVM分类器打分，并直接比较两模型给出的推荐（命中）集合及已标注活性分子的归属。

Result: 两模型推荐的化合物不同：量子处理器向量模型纳入了表格表示所遗漏的化合物（其中包含已标注的活性分子），表格表示也纳入了量子向量模型所遗漏的活性分子。报告的核心是这一集合——哪些化合物入选、处于何种标签状态、以及已发表化学是否已涉及它们。

Conclusion: 作者不主张形式化的量子优势，也未解释两种向量为何产生不同的分离边界；这些化合物均落在两种表示共同覆盖的邻域内，差异仅在于哪个模型将其判为活性。由此得出预测分歧的代价：每个模型会把不同的化学系列送去做实验评价。

Abstract: In drug discovery, hit identification selects a limited set of compounds for experimental evaluation from a larger collection of candidates. Because laboratory screening capacity is limited, that choice determines which chemical series continue into further investigation. A hit that is not assayed does not enter confirmation or the analog series, and that chemical series is not opened. For the DYRK1A kinase, the same test molecules were scored from a tabular representation of molecular descriptors and from a vector measured on a quantum processor, with the same RBF-SVM. The question is whether the two models propose the same set for experimental evaluation: they propose different compounds. The model on the vector measured on the quantum processor includes compounds that the tabular representation leaves out, and among them are already-labeled actives. The tabular representation includes actives that the vector measured on the quantum processor leaves out. What is reported is that set: who enters, with what label, and whether published chemistry already mentions it. No formal quantum advantage is claimed, nor a mechanism for why the two vectors separate differently. Those compounds fall within the neighborhood that both representations already cover; what changes is which model marks them active. From that follows the cost of predictive disagreement: each model would send a different chemical series to experimental evaluation.

</details>


### [101] [Quantum-Informed Graph Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14180)
*Matthew Brunet,Leah Prepchuk,Rebekah Herrman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子增强图分解（QEGD）启发式算法，以QAOA为子程序通过局部顶点合并稀疏化图，在MaxCut上与GDOA及多种QAOA变体比较，精度更高、耗时显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA求解组合优化问题时精度与资源需求随问题规模和结构恶化；GDOA虽通过迭代降规模保证最优解，但需解变量数呈指数增长的整数规划，且松弛项可能导致次优解。

Method: 提出QEGD启发式：将QAOA作为子程序，通过局部顶点合并（local vertex-merging）对图进行稀疏化，再在稀疏化后的MaxCut问题上调用QAOA求解。

Result: 在随机5-正则与6-正则图上，QEGD的近似比在所有测试图中均高于GDOA；在Erdős–Rényi图上，QEGD平均近似比高于QAOA²和ParaQAOA，在稀疏图上高于DC-QAOA、在较稠密图上与之相当，而平均墙钟时间仅为DC-QAOA的0.4%–5%。

Conclusion: 以QAOA为子程序做图稀疏化的QEGD在保持较高近似比的同时大幅降低求解时间，是相较GDOA与主流QAOA变体更具实用性的方案。

Abstract: The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) is a near-term quantum algorithm for solving combinatorial optimization problems, but its accuracy and resource requirements may scale poorly with problem size or structure, reducing its utility in practice. Approaches such as the Graph Decomposition for Optimization Algorithm (GDOA) seek to address this by iteratively reducing an input problem until it reaches a size where it can be solved using QAOA while maintaining the same optimal solution. This algorithm, however, requires solving integer programs that have exponentially many variables, and can result in suboptimal solutions, as the integer programs may contain slack terms. In this work, we propose a heuristic called the Quantum Enhanced Graph Decomposition (QEGD) algorithm. Its novelty lies in its use of QAOA as a subroutine for sparsifying a graph through local vertex-merging. The algorithm, when used to sparsify MaxCut problems that are then solved using QAOA, achieves higher approximation ratios across all tested graphs than GDOA for random 5-regular and 6-regular graphs. We then compare the time-to-solution and approximation ratio of QEGD to other QAOA variants a collection of Erdös-Rényi graphs. QEGD has higher approximation ratios on average than QAOA$^2$ and ParaQAOA on all tested graphs. It also results in higher average approximation ratios than DC-QAOA on sparse Erdös-Rényi graphs and comparable average approximation ratio on denser Erdös-Rényi graphs. However, its average wall-clock time is between 0.4\% and 5\% of the average DC-QAOA wall-clock time.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum-Assisted Counterfactual Regret Minimization for Bounded-Payoff Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14182)
*Gabriel Maresch,Erik Schulze,Jnan Yalla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子辅助反事实遗憾最小化（QA-CFR）框架，利用量子振幅估计降低分级奖励不完全信息博弈中遗憾更新的计算复杂度，并保持经典算法的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分级奖励结构的不完全信息博弈中，均衡计算需在指数级策略空间上估计收益期望，计算挑战巨大。反事实遗憾最小化（CFR）虽能有效逼近纳什均衡，但其蒙特卡洛变体在从采样博弈树轨迹估计反事实值时产生大量采样开销。

Method: 将量子振幅估计（QAE）引入CFR框架，用于加速有界收益不完全信息博弈中的遗憾更新，替代蒙特卡洛采样式的反事实值估计；并从理论上分析所得可利用度（exploitability）界，讨论兼容NISQ的振幅估计变体及其资源需求结构。

Result: 论文提出QA-CFR框架并给出理论分析：证明其在降低遗憾更新计算复杂度的同时保持经典对应算法的收敛性质，导出了可利用度上界，并刻画了NISQ兼容振幅估计变体的资源需求结构。

Conclusion: 量子振幅估计为CFR类算法提供了降低采样开销的潜在路径；论文同时指出，实证验证该潜在优势所需的实验设计仍待完成，属于理论与框架层面的探索性工作。

Abstract: Imperfect-information games with a graded-reward structure present significant computational challenges for equilibrium computation, since the payoff expectations must be estimated across exponentially large strategy spaces. Counterfactual Regret Minimization over the years has proven effective for approximating Nash equilibria, but Monte Carlo variants create a large overhead of sampling costs when the counterfactual values are estimated from sampled game-tree trajectories. We propose a novel Quantum-Assisted Counterfactual Regret Minimization, a framework that uses Quantum Amplitude Estimation to reduce the computational complexity of the regret updates in bounded-payoff imperfect-information games, while preserving the convergence of its classical counterpart. We give a theoretical analysis of the resulting exploitability bound, discuss NISQ-compatible amplitude-estimation variants, the structure of their resource requirements, and outline what is needed to validate the potential advantage empirically.

</details>


### [103] [Low-energy spectral-window truncation for compact U(1) lattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14188)
*Yi Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为原始纯紧致U(1)格点哈密顿量在三维周期立方格点（每个方向N≥3，θ=0）上证明了一个非渐近的电通量截断界，覆盖整个真空相对的整个低能窗口，无需能隙隔离或窗口维数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截断电通量是格点规范理论数值模拟（如量子模拟、张量网络）的必要步骤，但既有结果缺乏对整个低能窗口的统一、非渐近的严格界，常依赖能隙隔离、非简并等物理假设或引入随窗口维数增长的因子。作者希望给出无假设、可直接用于有限截断精度估计的严格结果。

Method: 在三维周期立方格的规范不变零全局通量扇区中，证明窗口中任一归一化向量的尾部范数满足阶乘平方型估计；该界同时控制由同一初态归一化投影出发的有限时间硬壁演化。并通过该模型中一个解析的有限体积强耦合例子验证非真空带与序一 plaquette 信号变化在指定有限截断下被保持；另用极分解给出针对参考纠缠输入的线性等距编码，并给出局域电平方读出界。

Result: 得到无假设的阶乘平方尾部范数界（不含窗口维数因子），可用于精度相关的充分截断值估计与局域读出误差界；解析强耦合例证明确有非真空带被保留。进一步证明在固定正裸耦合、物理体积、窗口宽度和时长下，取 β>2 且截断按 β log N / log log N 增长，可使态与有界读出截断误差随格点加密趋于零。

Conclusion: 该工作为纯紧致U(1)格点哈密顿量的低能窗口提供了严格、非渐近且适应精度的截断保证，并以极温和（近对数）的截断增长实现格点加密下的收敛，对格点规范理论的量子模拟与张量网络计算具有直接的实用意义。

Abstract: We prove a nonasymptotic electric-flux truncation bound for the entire vacuum-relative low-energy window of the original pure compact $U(1)$ lattice Hamiltonian. On a three-dimensional periodic cubic lattice with $N\geq3$ sites per direction at $\theta=0$, in the gauge-invariant zero-global-flux sector, the factorial-squared tail-norm estimate holds for every normalized vector in the window, without physical eigenvalue-isolation, window-edge gap, or nondegeneracy assumptions and without a window-dimension factor. It controls finite-time hard-wall evolution from the normalized projection of the same initial state. An analytic finite-volume strong-coupling example in this model certifies a nonvacuum band and an order-one plaquette signal change preserved by a specified finite cutoff. The same estimate yields precision-dependent sufficient cutoffs, an alternative linear isometric encoding by polar decomposition for reference-entangled inputs, and local electric-square readout bounds. At fixed positive bare coupling, physical volume, window width, and duration, cutoffs scaling as $\beta\log N/\log\log N$ with $\beta>2$ make state and bounded-readout truncation errors vanish under lattice refinement.

</details>


### [104] [Classical phase unwrapping enables large-time-step unitary quantum Krylov subspace diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14214)
*Tushar Kumar Jain,Rahul Maitra,Nicholas J. Mayhall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出CURE-QKSD：一种经典后处理方案，在UQKSD中用较大时间步长改善Krylov基条件数，并通过相位一致性解析回缺失的$2\pi/\tau$倍数，从而以更小的Krylov维数达到化学精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UQKSD通过投影实时传播子提取分子能量，避免了QPE的深电路与变分法的Pauli项采样。但小时间步长$\tau$下Krylov向量近线性相关，重叠矩阵病态：固定总演化时间下增加Krylov向量不再提升精度，阈值化能量对截断选择愈发敏感。增大$\tau$可改善条件数并降低达到目标精度所需的Krylov维数$M$，代价是能量只能确定到模$2\pi/\tau$，即出现混叠。

Method: CURE-QKSD保持UQKSD电路与广义本征值问题不变，仅做经典后处理：利用一次（必要时两次）UQKSD运行的相位一致性（phase-consistency）景观，恢复缺失的$2\pi/\tau$整数倍，从而在混叠时间步长下解析本征值。

Result: 在6个分子（12–20量子比特）的无噪测试中，28个目标能量全部在混叠区间被恢复，最终误差不超过$1.1\times10^{-7}$ Ha。在高斯噪声$\sigma=10^{-4}$下，于大混叠时间步长、$M=20$–$65$时，基态或第一激发态的中位误差达到化学精度，而未校正的主值支能量偏差达$0.7$–$115$ Ha。

Conclusion: CURE-QKSD使混叠的大时间步长可用于UQKSD计算，允许使用更小、条件数更好的Krylov基，是面向早期容错硬件的实用改进。

Abstract: For computing molecular energies on early fault-tolerant hardware, unitary quantum Krylov subspace diagonalization (UQKSD) avoids both the deep circuits of quantum phase estimation and the Pauli-term sampling of variational methods by extracting the energies from a projected real-time propagator. At a small time step $\tau$, nearly linearly dependent Krylov vectors make the overlap matrix ill-conditioned. Our tests at fixed total evolution time show that additional Krylov vectors then add no accuracy, while the thresholded energy becomes increasingly sensitive to the choice of cutoff. A larger time step improves the conditioning and reduces the Krylov dimension $M$ needed for a target accuracy, but the energies are then known only modulo $2\pi/\tau$. We present Classical Unwrapping for Resolving Eigenvalues (CURE-QKSD), a classical post-processing method that leaves the UQKSD circuits and generalized eigenvalue problem unchanged and recovers the missing multiples of $2\pi/\tau$ from phase-consistency landscapes of one or, when needed, two UQKSD runs. In noiseless tests on six molecules with 12--20 qubits, all 28 targeted energies are recovered in their aliased regimes with final errors of at most $1.1\times10^{-7}$~Ha. Under Gaussian noise with $\sigma=10^{-4}$, the median error of the ground or first excited state reaches chemical accuracy at large aliased time steps with $M=20$--$65$, whereas uncorrected principal-branch energies are off by $0.7$--$115$~Ha. CURE-QKSD therefore makes aliased time steps usable for UQKSD calculations with smaller, better-conditioned Krylov bases.

</details>


### [105] [Reading long-range information from a separable quantum record requires entangled measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14295)
*Qingtian Miao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 无纠缠的探针记录仍可编码长程信息：当重叠度 $0<p\le p_0=1-1/\sqrt2$ 时，任何可分测量（含LOCC及所有偏转置正测量）对远距离自旋关联的判别成功率随距离趋于 $1/2$；而当 $p_*\le p\le1$ 时，纠缠效应测量可使成功率与距离无关地高于 $1/2$，$[p_*,p_0]$ 区间给出严格分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究"局域产生、完全可分的量子记录"能否承载只能由纠缠测量读取的长程关联信息，从而刻画可分测量与纠缠测量在信息提取能力上的边界。

Method: 每个探针与一对初始无关联的近邻Ising自旋局域相互作用，把键标签编码为两个重叠为 $1-p$ 的态；接收方获得一份记录副本，判断两个远距离自旋同向或反向。分析正方格子上最优可分测量（含所有LOCC）及在探针任意割下效应偏转置保持正的测量所达到的成功概率，并与联合（纠缠效应）读数对比。

Result: 对 $0<p\le p_0=1-1/\sqrt2$，随自旋间距增大，可分测量最优成功率趋于 $1/2$；该结论涵盖所有LOCC及所有偏转置正效应测量。对 $p_*=0.29181\le p\le1$，联合读数成功率恒定高于 $1/2$ 一个与距离无关的正量。区间 $[p_*,p_0]$ 上二者严格分离。

Conclusion: 局域制备、不含纠缠的记录也可保存长程信息，但唯有具有纠缠效应的测量才能将其读出；可分测量在临界重叠阈值以下无法区分远距自旋关联。

Abstract: Qubit probes record the relative orientation of neighboring, initially uncorrelated Ising spins through local interactions. Each probe encodes one bond label in two states with overlap $1-p$. The resulting probe record is fully separable. A receiver gets one copy and guesses whether two distant spins point in the same or opposite directions. On the square lattice, the best readout with separable measurement effects approaches success probability $1/2$ as the spin separation grows, for $0<p\le p_0=1-1/\sqrt2$. This includes all local operations with classical communication. The same conclusion holds for every measurement whose effects remain positive under partial transposition across every cut of the probes. For $p_*=0.29181\le p\le1$, a joint readout of the same record maintains a success probability above $1/2$ by a positive amount independent of distance. The overlap $[p_*,p_0]$ gives a strict separation between the readouts. A record that is created locally and contains no entanglement can therefore hold long-range information that only measurements with entangled effects can read.

</details>


### [106] [Electrical Stark Tuning and In Situ Field Sensing of $V_{2}$ Silicon Vacancies in a 4H-SiC a-Plane Lateral pin-Diode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14308)
*Jannik H. Schwarberg,Fedor D. Hrunski,Maximilian Hollendonner,Wolfgang Knolle,Mathias Rommel,Fabian J. Magerl,Jan F. Dick,Jörg Schulze*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在a面4H-SiC衬底上用CMOS兼容工艺制备横向pin二极管，实现V2硅空位的电控与可寻址光学激发，零偏下光学线宽<80 MHz；并利用斯塔克效应进行低温局域电场原位测量与ZPL精确调谐，调谐后谱稳定性超过4小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统c面4H-SiC限制了谐振光激发的几何构型与实验方案；同时量子网络需要不同发射体之间光谱不可区分，而个体发射体存在谱位差异。

Method: 采用可规模化CMOS工艺在a面4H-SiC晶圆上制备横向pin二极管，对本征区内的V2（立方格位）硅空位实现电学控制与经晶圆表面的谐振光寻址；通过零偏下光学跃迁线宽表征光学质量；借助斯塔克效应（正向/反向偏压）进行低温局域电场原位测量并与宏观TCAD仿真对比；利用反向偏压斯塔克效应对单个发射体ZPL进行精确调谐以补偿谱位差。

Result: 尽管经历完整半导体工艺，集成色心仍保持良好光学质量，零偏线宽低于80 MHz；集成量子传感器可原位测得低温局域电场，并与TCAD仿真对比揭示高度局域化的场聚集；斯塔克调谐可补偿不同发射体的谱位差异，在瞬态电荷涨落弛豫后谱稳定性超过4小时。

Conclusion: a面4H-SiC是面向未来干涉实验与量子光子集成电路的稳健平台。

Abstract: Silicon carbide has emerged as a highly promising platform for scalable quantum technologies, with the silicon vacancy serving as a robust spin qubit and quantum sensor. However, conventional c-plane 4H-SiC restricts the experimental scheme and resonant optical excitation to the sample's geometry. The usage of a-plane substrates enables more versatile resonant optical access to the color center through the wafer surface. Here, we demonstrate the electrical control and optical addressability of $V_{2}$ (cubic lattice site) silicon vacancies within the intrinsic region of lateral pin-diodes fabricated on a-plane 4H-SiC wafers using a scalable complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) process. Despite extensive semiconductor processing, the integrated color centers exhibit good optical quality, evidenced by narrow optical transition linewidths below 80 MHz at zero bias. These embedded quantum sensors are utilized for in situ measurement of local electric fields at cryogenic temperatures via the Stark effect. Comparisons with macroscopic technology computer-aided design simulations reveal the value of these integrated quantum sensors to help understand cryogenic device behavior, for example in predicting highly localized field crowding. Furthermore, to address the spectral indistinguishability required for quantum networks, we precisely tune the zero-phonon line (ZPL) of individual emitters via the reverse-bias Stark effect. This successfully compensates for the spectral variations of distinct emitters. Following the relaxation of transient charge fluctuations, the Stark-tuned emitters exhibit spectral stability over measurement periods exceeding four hours. Our results establish a-plane 4H-SiC as a robust platform for future interference experiments and quantum photonic integrated circuits.

</details>


### [107] [Error Resilience and Unitary 2-Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14314)
*Zheng-Hang Sun,Fabian Ballar Trigueros,Xhek Turkeshi,Piotr Sierant*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究编码-纠错-解码电路中编码器偏离单位2设计时对纠错相的保护作用，证明恢复保真度与二阶酉框架势之间存在普适不等式，并指出恢复保真度可作为多体系统偏离2设计行为的诊断量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于取自单位2设计集合的编码器，此类电路存在误差阈值以下的"纠错相"，恢复保真度趋于1；但实际物理系统往往不满足Haar二阶矩统计，偏离会如何影响该纠错相尚不清楚。

Method: 推导将恢复保真度与二阶酉框架势（衡量集合接近单位2设计的程度）相联系的普适不等式；以掺入可调数量非高斯门的matchgate电路为典型例子进行解析分析；并考察哈密顿量型编码器（如实验驱动的全连接自旋模型）。

Result: 解析证明未掺杂的matchgate电路在热力学极限下不存在稳定的纠错相；随掺杂增加，编码器集合趋近近似单位2设计，纠错机制得以恢复；全连接自旋模型等哈密顿量编码器遵循同一机制。

Conclusion: 编码器偏离单位2设计的程度定量决定纠错相的稳定性，恢复保真度可作为量子多体系统中偏离单位2设计行为的诊断指标。

Abstract: Encoding-error-decoding circuits provide a setting for studying protection of quantum information against errors. In these circuits, an encoding unitary is followed by an error layer, decoding by the inverse unitary, and ancilla postselection. For encoders drawn from unitary 2-design ensembles, these circuits exhibit an error-protecting phase below a threshold error strength in which the fidelity between the input and recovered logical states approaches unity. Here, we show how departures from Haar second-moment statistics of the encoder can destabilize the error-protecting phase and derive a universal inequality linking recovery fidelity to the second unitary frame potential, which quantifies the ensemble's proximity to a unitary 2-design. This connection is exemplified by matchgate circuits doped with a tunable number of non-Gaussian gates. We analytically show that undoped circuits lack a stable error-protecting phase in the thermodynamic limit and that increasing the doping restores the error protection mechanism as the encoder ensemble approaches an approximate unitary 2-design. Further examples of Hamiltonian encoders, including an experimentally motivated all-to-all spin model, follow the same mechanism. Our results identify recovery fidelity as a diagnostic of departures from unitary 2-design behavior in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [108] [Dynamical Casimir emission from Floquet-driven Landau polaritons: Gauge invariance forbids a superradiant phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14339)
*S. Hughes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立周期性驱动Landau极化子的精确理论：计入磁通调制产生的Faraday电场后，问题经Kohn定理严格约化为质心模与腔模耦合，其Hamiltonian在每一时刻均为平方和；由Krein定理可证驱动频率高于可计算的Krein频率时系统全阶稳定，低于它仅在共振舌内出现从真空产生粒子对的动态Casimir效应。高频驱动下电子感受到的是平均场加有质动力势阱（Fock–Darwin问题），而非增强耦合。作者进一步证明规范定理：任何能带结构（含Kane非抛物性）下均不存在Floquet光子凝聚，并导出广义TRK求和规则，指出所谓Floquet SRPT源于在固定Landau能级基下对耦合取平均、所得有效模型违反TRK规则。结论：磁调制不产生SRPT，而给出Faraday驱动的极化子–磁等离激元对（低阈值）与两色光子对（5–10 dB双模压缩）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前文献预测：单独调制磁场可增强周期平均耦合，从而诱导Floquet超辐射相变（SRPT）。但线性系统（如太赫兹腔中二维电子气形成的Landau极化子）由玻色Hopfield模型描述，规范不变性禁止SRPT。作者旨在厘清这一矛盾：检验Floquet SRPT是否真实存在，并给出磁调制下Landau极化子的严格处理与规范约束。

Method: (1) 建立周期驱动Landau极化子的精确理论，显式计入调制磁通感生的电场（Faraday定律）；(2) 借助Kohn定理将问题严格约化为电子质心模与腔模耦合，其Hamiltonian在任意时刻为平方和；(3) 应用Krein定理并给出自包含证明，给出稳定性判据与Krein频率；(4) 分析共振舌内的动态Casimir效应；(5) 证明适用于任意能带（含Kane非抛物性）的规范定理，并导出广义Thomas–Reiche–Kuhn求和规则；(6) 指出原预测的根源：在固定Landau能级基下对耦合取平均，所得有效模型违反TRK求和规则，且忽略Faraday场。

Result: 驱动频率高于Krein频率时系统全阶稳定；低于它时除共振舌外稳定，舌内出现动态Casimir效应（从真空产生粒子对）。高频极限下电子感知平均场加有质动力势阱（Fock–Darwin问题），并无耦合增强。规范定理排除任意能带下的Floquet光子凝聚。物理上取代SRPT的是：低阈值的Faraday驱动极化子–磁等离激元对，以及具有5–10 dB双模压缩的两色光子对。

Conclusion: 磁调制不产生Floquet超辐射相变，而是产生动态Casimir辐射。正确的规则是对最小耦合Hamiltonian本身进行调制与（时间）平均，而非对其投影的参数取平均——此规则适用于任何线性、最小耦合系统。文中给出的精确约化、Krein稳定性判据与广义TRK规范约束，为分析驱动腔量子电动力学系统提供了严格框架。

Abstract: Ultrastrong light--matter coupling of linear systems, such as Landau polaritons formed by a two-dimensional electron gas in a terahertz cavity, is described by the bosonic Hopfield model, and gauge invariance forbids a superradiant phase transition (SRPT). Modulating the magnetic field alone has been predicted to enhance the cycle-averaged coupling and induce a Floquet SRPT. We develop an exact theory of periodically driven Landau polaritons. Including the electric field induced by the modulated flux (Faraday's law), the problem reduces exactly, by Kohn's theorem, to the electronic center-of-mass mode coupled to the cavity, whose Hamiltonian is a sum of squares at every instant. A theorem of Krein then guarantees stability to all orders above a computable drive frequency, the Krein frequency, for which we give a self-contained proof. Below it, the system is stable except within resonance tongues where the drive creates pairs from the vacuum, the dynamical Casimir effect. At high drive frequency the electrons experience the mean field plus a ponderomotive trap (the Fock--Darwin problem), not an enhanced coupling. A gauge theorem excludes a Floquet photon condensate for any band structure, including Kane nonparabolicity, for which we derive a generalized Thomas--Reiche--Kuhn (TRK) sum rule. The predicted transition arises from averaging the coupling in a fixed Landau-level basis, which yields an effective model that violates the TRK sum rule; under driving, gauge invariance also requires the induced Faraday field. The rule to modulate and average the minimal-coupling Hamiltonian, not the parameters of its projection, applies to any linear, minimally coupled system. Instead of an SRPT, magnetic modulation produces dynamical Casimir emission: Faraday-driven polariton--magnetoplasmon pairs at low threshold, and two-color photon pairs with up to 5--10~dB of two-mode squeezing.

</details>


### [109] [High-Speed Control of a Fluxonium Qubit via Picosecond Pulses from Josephson Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14358)
*M. V. Bastrakova,D. S. Pashin,V. A. Vozhakov,N. V. Klenov,I. I. Soloviev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于约瑟夫森数字电路产生的皮秒级单磁通量子（SFQ）脉冲，提出两种互补协议实现fluxonium比特的高保真单比特控制，多能级模拟显示保真度超过99.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原本为transmon设计的SFQ控制协议在低频fluxonium上效率低下：磁耦合与电荷耦合的不对称性导致要么泄漏过大、要么门时间过长，因此需要专门的控制策略。

Method: 提出两种互补协议：(1) 通过磁通通道施加高频SFQ脉冲包，方案简单、解析透明且鲁棒；(2) 将相移的磁通与电荷脉冲序列组合，增强共振驱动以缩短门时长。采用多能级（multilevel）数值模拟评估性能。

Result: 单比特门保真度均超过99.99%；简化协议在比特参数与磁通偏置适度变化下仍保持鲁棒，组合协议在保持高保真的同时显著加速门操作。

Conclusion: 结果表明基于SFQ的低温控制是实现fluxonium比特快速、精确且可扩展操控的可行路径。

Abstract: We develop a control strategy for fluxonium qubits based on picosecond single-flux-quantum (SFQ) pulses generated by Josephson digital circuits. A major challenge is that SFQ protocols originally designed for transmon control become inefficient for low-frequency fluxonium. In this regime, the asymmetry of flux and charge couplings leads either to excessive leakage or excessively long gate times. We show that this limitation can be overcome by two complementary protocols. The first protocol employs high-frequency SFQ pulse packets applied through the flux channel, offering a simple, analytically transparent, and robust approach for high-fidelity single-qubit control. The second protocol combines phase-shifted flux and charge pulse sequences to enhance the resonant drive and reduce the gate duration. Multilevel simulations demonstrate single-qubit gate fidelities above $99.99\%$; the simpler protocol remains robust under moderate variations of qubit parameters and flux bias, while the combined protocol preserves high fidelity while substantially accelerating the gate. These results establish SFQ-based cryogenic control as a realistic route to fast, accurate, and scalable manipulation of fluxonium qubits.

</details>


### [110] [Invariance can prevent overfitting and shortcut learning in Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14368)
*Giuseppe D'Ambruoso,Giovanni Gramegna,Domenico Pomarico,Giuseppe Magnifico,Cosmo Lupo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在严格受控条件下比较等变与非等变量子神经网络，发现对称性（不变性）本身即可带来更好的泛化、抗捷径学习能力和噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往等变量子电路常与非等变架构在门类型、结构或规模上也存在差异，导致"对称性带来的具体收益"难以单独分离，因此需要控制变量来考察不变性的作用。

Method: 针对标签在二面体群 $D_4$ 下不变图像分类任务，构建四个八量子比特模型，其编码方式、电路结构、可训练参数数目相同且具有等价的动力学李代数；在同一基准下比较不变与非不变实现，指标为泛化误差、对捷径学习的鲁棒性以及对真实噪声模型的抵抗力。任务为四个二分类任务，包括 MNIST 及星系、红细胞两个科学数据集。

Result: 在四个二分类任务上，仅用几十张图像训练时，不变模型泛化优于非不变模型；不变性使模型免疫方向捷径——当训练中引入类别相关的角落水印后，非不变模型会学到水印，水印移动后准确率常降至随机水平；不变模型在真实噪声模型下准确率增益仍然保持。

Conclusion: 在架构、参数与李代数严格对齐的受控实验中，等变性（不变性）本身即能提升小样本泛化、抑制捷径学习并增强噪声鲁棒性，表明对称性归纳偏置是量子模型设计中的独立有效因素。

Abstract: Many image classification tasks have labels that are unchanged by rotations and reflections. Equivariant quantum neural networks incorporate such symmetries by design, but the specific benefit of symmetry is difficult to isolate. Equivariant quantum circuits are usually compared with architectures that also differ in gates, structure or size. Here, we investigate the role of invariance in a controlled setting, classifying images whose labels are invariant under the dihedral group $D_4$. We compare four eight-qubit models with identical encoding, circuit structure and number of trainable parameters, and equivalent dynamical Lie algebras, and benchmark invariant implementations against non-invariant ones in terms of generalization error, robustness to shortcut learning and resilience to a realistic noise model. On four binary tasks, including MNIST and two scientific data sets of galaxies and red blood cells, the invariant models trained on a few tens of images show better generalization than non-invariant ones. Invariance also makes the models immune to orientation shortcuts: when trained with a class-dependent corner watermark, the non-invariant models learn the watermark and often drop to chance level when it is moved. We show that the gain in accuracy of invariant models persists also under a realistic noise model.

</details>


### [111] [Operational limits of reference renewal for retained quantum-service results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14376)
*Roberto Fernández-Barrios,Iker Pastor-López,Amaia Pikatza-Huerga,Pablo García Bringas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过"引用更新(reference renewal)"实验重新考察预设协议下已保留的物理量子服务结果,12条转换各保留6个结果,其中10条完成更新、8条达到预设终点、5条仅凭保留结果即成功;精确单侧p=0.0546875,不足以支持恢复假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在预设协议下,已保留的物理量子服务结果能否通过引用更新被"重新认定"为有效结果,即验证恢复假设(recovery hypothesis)是否成立。

Method: 采用预设协议开展引用更新实验:12条转换(transition)各保留6个结果,统计完成更新数、达标数及仅依赖保留结果的成功数;计算精确单侧检验p值;并借助离线重建从保存的计数中恢复判定,采用"回溯绑定+oracle"校验及oracle提供校准分数进行对照分析。

Result: 10条转换完成更新,8条达到预设终点,5条仅用保留结果即成功;精确单侧p=0.0546875,未达显著性;不完整引用、绑定失败与顺序等待暴露出操作层面的限制;离线重建可从保留计数恢复判定;回溯"绑定+oracle"校验无需更新即接受全部60条可用转换结果;当oracle同时提供校准分数时,更新未体现增量式的数值正确性效用。

Conclusion: 引用更新未能确立恢复假设,其数值正确性效用相对"回溯绑定+oracle"校验无增量价值;保留结果的判定可通过离线重建恢复,实际瓶颈在于引用完整性、绑定与顺序等待等操作限制。

Abstract: We implement reference renewal to reconsider retained physical quantum-service results under a prespecified protocol. Twelve transitions retained six results each; ten completed renewal, eight met the prespecified endpoint, and five succeeded using retained results alone. The exact one-sided p=0.0546875 does not establish the recovery hypothesis. Incomplete references, binding failures and sequential waits expose operational limits. Offline reconstruction recovers decisions from preserved counts. Retrospective binding-plus-oracle checking accepts all 60 usable-transition results without renewal. With the exact oracle also supplying calibration scores, the study does not demonstrate incremental numerical-correctness utility of renewal.

</details>


### [112] [Implementing rapid reset of superconducting qubits with on-chip dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14391)
*Feiyu Li,Yang Liu,Zhen Yang,Zhenxing Liu,Jiayu Ding,Yang Gao,Xuehui Liang,Weijie Sun,Yanjun Ma,Chuhong Yang,Guangming Xue,He Wang,Fei Yan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作实验演示了一种可扩展的超导比特复位与泄漏消除方案：由谐振腔耦合片上电阻负载构成专用耗散单元，在提供Purcell保护的同时将比特与较热的外部电路隔离。|g⟩-|e⟩复位时间常数中位数约48 ns，有效浴温30 mK；400 ns复位脉冲下残余激发态布居为0.29%（|g⟩初始化）和0.67%（|e⟩初始化），|f⟩态泄漏布居降至0.51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速高保真比特复位是量子态初始化与量子纠错的前提。传统超导比特复位依赖连接外部电路的耗散元件，使比特暴露于高温环境的热噪声；因此需要一种兼具Purcell保护、与外部热环境隔离且易于扩展集成的复位方案。

Method: 在片上构建由谐振腔耦合集成电阻负载组成的专用耗散单元，并将其用于多个transmon比特的复位与泄漏消除；该架构在提供Purcell保护的同时将比特与较热的外部电路隔离，从而实现受控的耗散工程。

Result: |g⟩-|e⟩跃迁复位时间常数中位数约48 ns，有效浴温30 mK；400 ns复位脉冲后残余激发态布居分别为0.29%（|g⟩初始化）与0.67%（|e⟩初始化）；|f⟩态泄漏残余布居降至0.51%。

Conclusion: 该方案简单、可复现、易于集成，为超导量子处理器的耗散工程提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Rapid and high-fidelity qubit reset is essential for quantum state initialization and quantum error correction. Conventional reset protocols for superconducting qubits often rely on dissipative elements connected to external circuitry, exposing qubits to thermal noise from higher-temperature environments. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a scalable approach to qubit reset and leakage removal over multiple transmon qubits using dedicated dissipation units comprising resonators coupled to on-chip resistive loads. This architecture provides Purcell protection while isolating the qubits from warmer external circuitry. We achieve a median reset time constant of approximately 48 ns for the $|g\rangle-|e\rangle$ transition, with an effective bath temperature of 30 mK. A 400-ns reset pulse yields residual excited-state populations of $0.29\%$ and $0.67\%$ for qubits initialized in $|g\rangle$ and $|e\rangle$, respectively. We further demonstrate efficient leakage removal from the $|f\rangle$ state, reducing its residual population to $0.51\%$. These results establish a simple, reproducible, and easy-to-integrate approach to dissipation engineering in superconducting quantum processors.

</details>


### [113] [Simulating closed- and open-system dynamics of Tavis--Cummings lattices on fault-tolerant quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14398)
*Soumya Sarkar,Rakshit Gharat,Nam Nguyen,Gavin Brennen*
