Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文系统分析了含原子分辨的Tavis–Cummings晶格在容错量子计算下的逻辑资源开销，涵盖闭系统实时演化与含损耗、弛豫、退相干的开放系统模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tavis–Cummings晶格承载极化激元多体动力学，其二维实时关联函数经典计算代价高，尤其在各发射体弛豫或退相干速率不等而破坏置换对称性时更为困难；需要明确容错量子模拟的资源基准。

Method: 基于阶梯算符分块编码构造光子跃迁与局域光–物质交换电路并组合成晶格哈密顿量；通过在位更新源与目标格点的光子占据数，跃迁电路省去双寄存器方案所需的辅助光子寄存器；闭系统用广义量子信号处理实现实时演化，开放系统（光子损耗、发射体弛豫、退相干）用采样一阶膨胀。

Result: 在16至256个腔、均匀系数的三个实例中，在共享态制备、精度、控制与工作空间约定一致的情况下，受控行走比配对双寄存器实现少用4.75%–8.11%的Toffoli门；并给出估计延迟腔谱函数及瞬态光子统计（如g^(2)(0)）所需的运行次数与时间步界。

Conclusion: 在显式计入态制备与测量成本后，所得估计为评估容错光–物质晶格模拟代价提供了参考基准。

Abstract: Tavis--Cummings lattices couple cavities containing several emitters and host polaritonic many-body dynamics whose real-time correlations are costly to compute classically in two dimensions, especially when unequal emitter relaxation or dephasing rates break permutation symmetry. We analyze logical resources for fault-tolerant closed- and open-system simulation of an atom-resolved lattice with $L$ cavities, $N$ emitters per cavity, and photon cutoff $n\_{\max}$, including target observable estimation. Building on ladder-operator block encoding, we construct circuits for photon hopping and local light--matter exchange and combine them to encode the lattice Hamiltonian. By updating source and destination photon occupations in place, the hopping circuit eliminates the auxiliary $L\lceil\log_2(n\_{\max}+1)\rceil$-qubit photon register used by two-register implementations. Across three uniform-coefficient examples with $16$ to $256$ cavities, the controlled walk uses $4.75$--$8.11%$ fewer Toffoli gates than the paired two-register implementation under matched shared preparation, precision, control, and workspace conventions. For closed systems, we implement real-time evolution with generalized quantum signal processing and bound the cost of estimating retarded cavity spectral functions. For open systems with photon loss, emitter relaxation, and dephasing, we use a sampled first-order dilation and bound the runs and timesteps needed to estimate transient photon statistics such as $g^{(2)}(0)$. With state-preparation and measurement costs explicit, these estimates provide a reference for assessing fault-tolerant light--matter lattice simulation costs.

</details>


### [114] [Multidimensional Quantum Block Turbo Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14406)
*Khaled Jebari,Luiz Anet Neto,Ramesh Pyndiah,Jean-Louis de Bougrenet de la Tocnaye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将经典Turbo迭代译码思想拓展至多维量子分组Turbo码，提出针对多层乘积码的Turbo译码理论，给出算法并在去极化信道上仿真三维情形性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有量子串行Turbo码与量子分组Turbo码，但缺少面向多层乘积码的多维量子分组Turbo码统一理论，故需补足该框架。

Method: 构建多维量子分组Turbo码理论，设计面向多层乘积码的Turbo迭代译码算法，并以三维情形为例在去极化信道上进行数值仿真。

Result: 通过三维情形仿真验证了所提多维Turbo译码算法在去极化信道上的译码性能。

Conclusion: 多维量子分组Turbo码为多层乘积码提供了可行的迭代译码方案，是量子Turbo编码框架的自然推广。

Abstract: The theory of turbo iterative decoding was brought into the quantum coding framework through the development of quantum serial turbo codes and quantum block turbo codes. In this paper, we present a theory for multidimensional quantum block turbo codes, a turbo decoding technique for multi layer product codes. We describe its algorithm and simulate the performance for the 3D case on a depolarizing channel.

</details>


### [115] [The energetic cost of loading classical data in quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14422)
*Joao F. Doriguello,Alessandro Luongo,Harshit Verma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将量子数据加载视为真实硬件过程，在超导处理器上量化QRAM查询的动态能量代价，给出了12量子比特地址桶旅架构QRAM的保真度与功耗指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习、化学与密码分析类量子算法普遍假设对经典数据的相干访问代价可忽略，缺乏对数据加载真实物理成本（能量、功率）的定量刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用metric-noise-resource框架，将查询保真度与功率同门持续时间、相对双比特驱动强度以及多级制冷机的衰减分布相关联，并计入热光子与门误差；分析线性深度多路复用器与抗噪桶旅（bucket-brigade）两种架构，后者适配原生超导门集，并使用完全分解的门调度改进保真度分析。

Result: 对含12量子比特地址寄存器的代表性桶旅QRAM查询，查询保真度达0.969；单次查询消耗动态能量3.51×10⁻⁴ J，对应平均动态功率4.38 W、峰值64.4 W；在固定目标保真度条件下，工作点与衰减分布可使平均功率变化达两倍以上。

Conclusion: QRAM数据加载具有不可忽略的动态能耗，其功率对工作点与制冷衰减剖面高度敏感，因此在评估量子算法资源时须将数据加载的硬件能耗纳入考量。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms for machine learning, chemistry, and cryptanalysis often assume coherent access to classical data at negligible physical cost. We instead treat data loading as a hardware process and quantify the dynamic energetic cost of a quantum random access memory (QRAM) query on a superconducting processor. Using the metric-noise-resource framework, we relate query fidelity and power to gate duration, relative two-qubit drive strength, and the distribution of attenuation across a multi-stage refrigerator, while accounting for thermal photons and gate errors. We analyze the linear-depth multiplexer and the noise-resilient bucket-brigade architectures, adapting the latter to a native superconducting gate set and refining its fidelity analysis using the fully decomposed gate schedule. For a representative bucket-brigade QRAM query with a $12$-qubit address register we obtain a query fidelity of $0.969$. The query consumes $3.51\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{J}$ of dynamic energy, corresponding to an average dynamic power of $4.38\,\mathrm{W}$ and a peak of $64.4\,\mathrm{W}$. Our optimization shows that, at fixed target fidelity, the operating point and attenuation profile can change the average power by a factor of two or more.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum Thermal Transistor via Nonlinear Environmental Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14458)
*Laiba Hayat,M. Tahir Naseem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究谐振子与发射极、集电极、基极三个热库耦合时热流的热放大机制，指出仅允许单量子交换时不存在热放大，而发射极处的双量子交换可触发并预测热放大；当发射极与集电极均由双量子交换主导时，调节基极温度可在奇偶振子扇区之间重新分配布居，使发射极与集电极热流变化远大于基极热流，实现大热放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热放大可实现对热流的灵敏调控，是量子器件中热管理与热信号处理的基础；但以往热放大机制（尤其是系统-热库非线性耦合的作用）缺乏清晰判据与可控理论描述，因此需要明确何种耦合条件能产生热放大并给出可实现方案。

Method: 构建谐振子与三个热库（发射极、集电极、基极）耦合模型；采用受控弱非线性展开并结合数值定态解，分析单量子与双量子交换对热放大的影响；通过改变基极温度考察奇偶振子扇区的布居重分布；提出用纵向耦合辅助二能级系统与热库谱滤波的实验实现方案，并给出消除辅助系统以获得所需振子动力学的条件。

Result: 单量子交换限制下复现了热放大的缺失；弱非线性展开（经数值定态解验证）表明发射极双量子交换可产生放大并给出其起始判据；当发射极与集电极均由双量子交换主导时，基极温度改变引起奇偶扇区布居重分配，发射极与集电极热流变化远大于基极电流，从而实现大热放大。

Conclusion: 非线性系统-热库耦合可作为调控热流的资源，为热放大与热流控制提供新途径，并给出了基于辅助二能级系统与谱滤波的可行实现条件。

Abstract: Thermal amplification enables sensitive control of heat currents, providing a basis for heat management and thermal signal processing in quantum devices. For a harmonic oscillator coupled to emitter, collector, and base thermal reservoirs, we recover the absence of thermal amplification when system-reservoir interactions permit only single-quantum exchange. A controlled weak-nonlinearity expansion, supported by numerical stationary solutions, shows that two-quantum exchange at the emitter enables amplification and predicts its onset. When two-quantum exchange dominates at the emitter and collector, varying the base temperature redistributes populations between even and odd oscillator sectors. This changes the emitter and collector heat currents far more than the base current, enabling large thermal amplification. We propose a realization using a longitudinally coupled auxiliary two-level system and bath spectral filtering, specifying conditions for its elimination to yield the required oscillator dynamics. These results establish nonlinear system-reservoir coupling as a resource for controlling heat flow and enabling thermal amplification.

</details>


### [117] [Finite-Key Readiness and Authentication-Key Replenishment in QKD-Assisted UAV Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14461)
*Dang H. Le,Ban Q. Tran,Susan Mengel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对双地面网关、采用一次性诱骗态BB84有限密钥的固定翼UAV密钥服务，作者用计数级蒙特卡洛（经半解析模型复现）把密钥获取是否成功耦合到受保护的上传-计算-下载流程与悬停能耗，量化"等钥匙"的时延代价。26 dB损耗下新鲜密钥使双任务95百分位完成时间由10.37 s升至28.65 s；块大小的截止期限制被标量速率模型漏掉；脉冲预算窗口下界由认证密钥补充决定、上界由截止期决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要新鲜量子密钥的服务必须先等待密钥释放，且可能被拒绝，之后才能开始受保护计算；既有工作多以标量密钥率评估QKD，未刻画这种"先等钥匙"的端到端代价及其对任务截止期、脉冲预算和认证密钥消耗的约束。

Method: 设定可信静止UAV为两地面网关服务，每网关从一次参数预定的有限密钥诱骗态BB84尝试中取密钥；建立计数级蒙特卡洛模型并由半解析模型交叉验证，将密钥接受/拒绝对接受保护上传、计算、下载全过程，计入失败尝试与悬停能耗；与标量速率模型对比；分析脉冲预算窗口（下界=认证密钥补充，上界=截止期），并做高成本压力基准（哈希全计入）与留出试验的置信下界评估。

Result: 26 dB有效光损耗、认证哈希近乎零成本时，95百分位完成时间由10.37 s增至28.65 s，悬停能量同比增加；标量速率模型未捕捉到块尺寸的截止期上限；固定源/信道/接受模型下，认证"扣费"移动窗口下界而哈希成本不移动。35 s截止期时非空窗口的最大可容忍损耗在1%失配下为27 dB、2%下为22 dB，3%下无可行设置；在假定获取概率下，所选配置在26 dB及18–20 dB（2%失配）留出试验上满足截止期，同时下置信界达96.9–97.3%；全计哈希的压力基准将窗口收窄，使上述损耗限分别降至26 dB与20 dB。

Conclusion: 新鲜密钥的开销必须按端到端任务截止期而非平均密钥率评估：误差率或损耗的轻微上升即可使截止期达标概率远低于目标并耗尽认证密钥，而此时协议安全参数并未改变，说明运维层面需显式预留认证密钥预算并做丢失敏感性设计。

Abstract: A service that needs fresh quantum keys must wait for key release, and may be denied it, before protected computation starts. We quantify this cost for a trusted stationary UAV serving two ground gateways, each obtaining its keys from one finite-key decoy-state BB84 attempt with parameters fixed in advance. A count-level Monte Carlo model, reproduced by a semi-analytic model, links key acceptance to protected upload, computation, and download, including failed attempts and hover energy. At 26~dB effective optical loss, with authentication hashing at its proxy-benchmarked, near-zero cost, fresh keys raise the 95th-percentile completion time of both tasks from 10.37 to 28.65~s; hover energy grows in proportion. The scalar rate models evaluated here miss the deadline limit on the block size. The pulse budget lies in a window bounded below by authentication-key replenishment and above by the deadline; for a fixed source, channel, and acceptance model, the authentication debit shifts the floor, whereas hashing cost does not. At a 35-s deadline the largest tested loss with a non-empty window is 27~dB at 1\% misalignment and 22~dB at 2\%; at 3\% no tested setting has one. Conditional on the assumed acquisition probability, the selected configurations meet this deadline with simultaneous lower confidence bounds of 96.9--97.3\% on held-out trials, at 26~dB and at 18--20~dB with 2\% misalignment. A high-cost stress reference that charges hashing in full narrows the window and lowers these loss limits to 26 and 20~dB at the reference processing capacity. A small rise in error rate or loss pushes the deadline probability well below its target and drains authentication keys, while the protocol's security parameter is unchanged.

</details>


### [118] [Nonperturbative string dynamics in a (2+1)D lattice gauge theory on a superconducting quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14484)
*Sanziana Stelea,Anthony Gandon,Francesco Tacchino,Jad C. Halimeh,Ivano Tavernelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用63个超导量子比特（含12个辅助比特）量子模拟(2+1)维Z2格点规范理论，研究带动力学物质的电场弦演化，覆盖强禁闭与非微扰退禁闭区域，并借助规范冗余实现误差检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电场弦是规范理论中禁闭的核心机制。在二维空间中，弦的演化涉及电荷运动、对产生与横向形变等竞争过程。非微扰区域超出经典方法能力，而纠缠增长限制了张量网络模拟可及的时间尺度，因此需要量子模拟手段。

Method: 构建含动力学物质与显式磁面片相互作用的(2+1)维Z2格点规范理论量子模拟；使用63个超导量子比特（12个辅助比特）；演化端点电荷为静态或动力学的初始局域弦；进行空间分辨测量，并保留物质与规范自由度分离以直接访问局域高斯定律约束，结合辅助比特校验与噪声重标定，运行最多15步二阶Trotter线路。

Result: 在退禁闭区观察到电荷与通量的快速扩散；在对产生共振附近，弦内部物质占据增强；强电场下扩散受抑制；弦路径占据率揭示由面片操作连接的构型间的横向重排；高斯定律约束结合辅助校验与噪声重标定，实现对最长15步Trotter演化动力学的可靠测量。

Conclusion: 研究确立量子模拟是探索二维非微扰弦动力学的实用途径，且规范冗余可作为实验资源用于误差检测。

Abstract: Electric flux strings underpin confinement in gauge theories. In two spatial dimensions, their evolution involves competing processes of charge motion, pair creation, and transverse deformation. Accessing these dynamics beyond perturbative limits challenges classical approaches, as entanglement growth restricts the times accessible to tensor-network simulations. Here we use quantum simulation to investigate a (2+1)D Z2 lattice gauge theory with dynamical matter and an explicit magnetic plaquette interaction, spanning strongly confined and nonperturbative deconfined regimes. Using 63 superconducting qubits, including 12 ancillas, we evolve initially localized strings with static or dynamical endpoint charges. Spatially resolved measurements reveal rapid charge and flux spreading in the deconfined regime, enhanced matter occupation in the string interior near the pair-creation resonance, and suppressed spreading at strong electric fields. String-path occupations further probe transverse rearrangements between configurations connected by plaquette operations. Retaining separate matter and gauge degrees of freedom makes local Gauss-law constraints directly accessible for error detection. Combining these constraints with ancilla checks and noise rescaling, we measure dynamics in circuits containing up to 15 second-order Trotter steps. Our results establish quantum simulation as a practical approach to exploring nonperturbative string dynamics in two spatial dimensions, with gauge redundancy serving as an experimental resource.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [And yet it moves - or does it? Spurious drifts at equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14089)
*Pierre Ronceray*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 当扩散系数依赖系统状态时，从轨迹测得的名义漂移中含有与力无关的虚假成分；该虚假漂移可由轨迹矩单独推断，从而实现热力学一致的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在状态依赖扩散（乘性噪声）体系中，常规从轨迹估计的漂移往往是条件平均而非真实概率流，含有一项与力无关的虚假部分，会导致平衡态粒子看似沉降、相互排斥甚至旋转（且时间反演对称）。若与不一致推断的扩散张量配对，会把被动粒子误判为主动粒子。因此需要一种能识别并剔除该虚假漂移的推断框架。

Method: 纯理论分析：将漂移界定为条件平均而非概率流，明确其只有在与一致推断的扩散张量配对时才有物理意义；推导虚假漂移的解析形式，并证明其可仅由轨迹矩（位移的条件矩）单独推断，进而构建热力学一致的漂移—扩散联合推断方案。

Result: 给出从轨迹矩直接估计虚假漂移的显式方法；说明该虚假漂移在平衡态下表现为沉降、排斥、旋转等时间反演对称的假象；指出漂移与扩散张量不匹配是产生"被动力学看似主动"误判的根源。

Conclusion: 轨迹漂移估计必须与扩散张量联合、自洽地进行；通过轨迹矩校正虚假漂移后，可获得热力学一致的力场与扩散张量推断，避免把被动体系误判为主动体系。

Abstract: When diffusion depends on the state of a system, the drift measured from its trajectories contains a spurious part unrelated to forces. At equilibrium, particles can then appear to sediment, repel each other, even rotate - the same way whether the movie is played forward or backward. Drifts are conditional averages, not currents: they carry meaning only together with a consistently inferred diffusion tensor. Paired with the wrong one, they make passive particles look active. I show how to infer spurious drifts from trajectory moments alone, enabling thermodynamically consistent inference.

</details>

### [120] [Fully GPU-accelerated semistochastic Epstein-Nesbet perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13522)
*Lizhong Fu,Honghui Shang,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出全GPU加速的分布式半随机二阶Epstein-Nesbet微扰理论（EN-PT2）实现，通过核函数融合与固定规模随机相位CountSketch数组，大幅降低内存与通信开销，实现相对64核CPU的Arrow代码8.21–37.53倍加速，并在双节点16块GPU上取得93.5%的强扩展并行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EN-PT2是改进selected CI能量的关键工具，但其外部行列式空间巨大，在GPU集群上带来显著的内存与通信瓶颈，亟需高效的大规模并行实现。

Method: 实现分布式半随机EN-PT2的全GPU版本：将激发生成、筛选与PT2权重计算融合于单次核函数遍历；采用固定规模的随机相位CountSketch数组限制内存占用，并以有界集合求和替代大规模外部行列式交换。

Result: 单块A100在相同目标统计标准误下比64核CPU上的Arrow快8.21–37.53倍；固定负载强扩展测试在跨两节点的16块GPU上达到93.5%并行效率；成功应用于(60e,187o)活性空间的铁氧配合物、每态2亿行列式的铁卟啉参考态以及神经网络量子态。

Conclusion: 该工作证明GPU加速的半随机EN-PT2可高效地对大规模参考波函数施加微扰修正，适用于强关联体系与神经网络量子态等前沿场景。

Abstract: Second-order Epstein-Nesbet perturbation theory (EN-PT2) is an important tool for improving selected configuration interaction (SCI) energies, but the large external determinant space creates substantial memory and communication costs on GPU clusters. We develop a fully GPU-accelerated implementation of distributed semistochastic EN-PT2, fusing excitation generation, screening, and PT2 weighting in a single kernel traversal. Fixed-size random-phase CountSketch arrays limit memory use and replace large external determinant exchange with bounded collective summation. The implementation on one A100 completes EN-PT2 calculations 8.21-37.53 times faster than Arrow on 64 CPU cores at the same requested statistical standard error, and fixed-workload strong-scaling tests achieve 93.5% parallel efficiency on 16 GPUs across two nodes. Applications to an iron-oxo complex in a (60e, 187o) active space, iron-porphyrin references containing 200 million determinants per state, and neural-network quantum states establish the method's efficiency for perturbative corrections to large reference wave functions in the GPU era.

</details>

### [121] [Chemotactic recruitment offsets congestion in honeybee trophallaxis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13314)
*William Magrogan,Orit Peleg,Zachary P. Kilpatrick*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该文用随机框架耦合趋化搜索与供体聚集处的随机顺序服务，刻画蜜蜂口对口传递食物的效率，发现效率在中等"放弃率"处最大化，并揭示服务与信号反馈可自组织地将招募匹配到局部服务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜜蜂通过口对口传递（trophallaxis）分配食物，效率取决于搜寻者找到供体并被服务；化学信号可吸引搜寻者，但聚集的蜂群也可能造成拥堵。作者希望量化这一招募—拥堵权衡，并解释无中心协调下群体如何把搜寻者分配到不同服务能力的供体。

Method: 建立随机（stochastic）建模框架：搜寻过程为依赖化学信号的"速度跳跃"（velocity-jump）搜索，供体聚集处的服务为随机顺序（random-order）排队；以群体喂饱既定比例成员所需时间作为传递效率度量，并解析/数值分析放弃率、信号强度与双供体服务率差异的影响。

Result: 定向招募源于已进食蜜蜂在供体附近逗留并增强局部化学线索，但逗留同时降低服务速率，故效率在中等放弃率处最优；明确了"被迁移的线索"（displaced cues）仍具效用性的条件；对服务率不等的两个供体，较快者更早产生饱食蜂、信号更强，从而把招募引向吞吐量更高者、远离易拥堵者。

Conclusion: 服务与信号之间的反馈可在无集中协调下自组织招募，使搜寻者分布匹配局部服务容量，为群体分布式资源分配提供机制性解释。

Abstract: Honeybee colonies distribute food by trophallaxis, a sequence of local interactions in which nectar donors transfer resources to hungry nestmates. Efficient transfer requires a searcher to find a donor and then be served. Chemical signals may draw searchers to a donor, though the crowd they form may block the way. We develop a stochastic framework coupling velocity-jump search that depends on chemical signaling to random-order service at donor aggregations, and measure trophallactic efficiency by the time the colony needs to feed a prescribed fraction of its members. Directed recruitment emerges because fed bees linger near donors and strengthen local chemical cues, but lingering also slows service. Trophallactic efficiency is therefore maximized at an intermediate abandonment rate that balances recruitment against congestion. We determine when displaced cues remain useful and how competing donors partition searchers. With two donors of unequal service rate, the faster one produces fed bees sooner and so advertises more strongly, drawing recruitment toward the donor that can sustain more throughput and away from the one that would congest. Together, these results show how feedback between service and signaling can self-organize recruitment to match local service capacity without centralized coordination.

</details>

### [122] [Energetic costs of reaching the physical limits of sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14145)
*Albert Alonso,Julius B. Kirkegaard,Robert G. Endres*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了梯度感知不确定性与吸收分子数之间的闭式关系，并将其与熵产生联系起来；核心结论是梯度感知受计数噪声而非能量成本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞通过吸收配体分子感知化学梯度，这一非平衡过程必然产生熵。但细胞需要吸收多少分子、耗散多少能量才能逼近感知的物理极限，此前缺乏定量答案。浓度感知仅依赖总计数，而梯度感知还需解析吸收通量的角向不对称性，二者极限的关系尚不清晰。

Method: 理论建模：推导闭式解析表达式，将梯度感知不确定性与吸收分子数关联，并进一步映射到熵产生。引入由细胞吸收强度决定的、有界的偶极对比因子（dipole-contrast factor），以刻画角向不对称性的解析能力。在强信号区分析趋化对齐与扩散限制摄取之间的关系，并重新分析 Dictyostelium（盘基网柄菌）趋化实验数据，进行参数定位。

Result: 强信号区中细胞可在仅吸收扩散限制摄取量的一小部分时接近趋化对齐峰值，所需摄取事件数由细胞尺度上的相对浓度变化决定。偶极对比因子将梯度感知的不确定性限制在有界范围内。对Dictyostelium数据的再分析显示，该细胞处于上述极限的0.25%，代谢负荷很小。

Conclusion: 梯度感知的物理极限由计数噪声主导，而非能量耗散成本；细胞以极低代谢代价即可接近感知极限，说明感知精度受分子计数统计涨落约束。结果为理解趋化感知的能量—精度权衡提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Cells sense chemical gradients by absorbing ligand molecules, a nonequilibrium process that necessarily produces entropy. But how many molecules must a cell absorb, and therefore energy dissipate, to approach the physical limits of sensing? We derive closed-form expressions relating gradient sensing uncertainty to the number of absorbed molecules, which we link to entropy production. Unlike concentration sensing, which depends only on the total count, gradient sensing must resolve the angular asymmetry of the absorbed flux, adding a bounded dipole-contrast factor set by how strongly the cell absorbs. In the strong-signal regime, cells approach peak chemotactic alignment while absorbing a small fraction of the diffusion-limited uptake, with the required number of uptake events set by the relative concentration change across the cell. Re-analysis of Dictyostelium chemotaxis places the cell at 0.25% of that limit with a minor metabolic load, suggesting that sensing is limited by counting noise rather than by energetic cost.

</details>

### [123] [Two atoms with strong light-induced dipolar interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14082)
*Hanzhen Lin,Yoo Kyung Lee,Nabeel Rasheed,Vitaly Fedoseev,Cicely Motamedi,Wolfgang Ketterle*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 研究两个原子间距小于光波长时的光散射，观测到偶极-偶极强相互作用导致的多重新现象：存在一个窄密度区间使部分散射光保持相干并获得约2倍的集体增强；通过动量分布揭示双原子波函数的共振弹出与耗尽动力学；紧束缚下总荧光强度依赖散射长度（源于窄Franck-Condon区），并测得探测光强对散射长度的调制，提供移动Feshbach共振的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当原子间距小于光波长时，激光诱导的电偶极矩之间相互作用强烈，传统独立原子散射图像失效，需从微观层面理解光缔合与宇称投影过程中究竟发生了什么。

Method: 将两个被紧束缚（光镊）的原子置于亚波长间距，测量其散射光；利用动量分布探测双原子波函数动力学；分析荧光强度对散射长度的依赖；并改变探测激光强度以测量其对散射长度的调制。

Result: 在狭窄的密度区间内，部分散射光保持相干，相对于独立原子呈现约2倍的集体增强因子；动量分布显示出共振弹出与耗尽；总荧光强度强烈依赖散射长度，源于狭窄的共振Franck-Condon区域；探测光强可改变散射长度。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了对光缔合与宇称投影微观过程的直接洞察，并建立了利用探测光强移动Feshbach共振的新方法。

Abstract: We study how two atoms scatter light when they are closer than an optical wavelength and observe multiple new phenomena due to the strong interactions between laser-induced electric dipole moments. We identify a narrow range of densities where a fraction of the scattered light is coherent and shows a collective enhancement factor of two compared to independent atoms. The dynamics of the two-atom wavefunction can be observed through momentum distributions which reveal resonant ejection and depletion. Due to the tight confinement, the total fluorescence intensity strongly depends on the scattering length, since it originates from a narrow resonant (Franck-Condon) region. Furthermore, we measure how the probe laser intensity modifies the scattering length, which establishes a new way to shift Feshbach resonances. Our work provides microscopic insight into what occurs during photoassociation and parity projection in optical tweezers.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

### [124] [A New Computational Model for the Spin-Free Sum-of-Squares Energy Bounds of Electronic Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14319)
*Yuming Bao,Yancheng Wu,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将无自旋SOS能量界的半定规划精确重写为单个对称矩阵（0≤X≤I）上的凸二次问题，用GPU加速Coulomb收缩与缓存式全校正Frank–Wolfe（DRSM）求解，实现万轨道级大规模计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无自旋SOS能量界的半定规划随轨道维度增大而规模爆炸，直接求解不可行；需要在不损失精确性的前提下降低变量维度并利用算子结构，使大规模电子哈密顿量的SOS松弛优化成为可能。

Method: 推导既有无自旋SOS模型的精确重构：转化为约束于[0,I]的单个对称矩阵上的凸二次规划，并给出可行迭代到原SOS证书的显式恢复映射，将Frank–Wolfe间隙等同于恢复后原对偶目标之差；二次作用通过GPU加速的Coulomb收缩实现；采用缓存算子乘积、在小规模校正二次规划中优化凸组合权重的全校正Frank–Wolfe（DRSM）。

Result: 实验覆盖65个FCIDUMP模型、分子几何与规则氢环，含9例原坐标恢复验证，并与FISTA及边界点SDP求解器对比；8块H100上C$_{720}$（10,080空间轨道）冷启动5.02小时达到重算Frank–Wolfe间隙8.82×10⁻⁹ hartree；全空间氢环计算达10,000轨道，循环约化进一步节省计算。

Conclusion: 精确重构、数据结构利用与Coulomb作用复用相结合，使所选大规模SOS松弛的优化在万轨道量级上变得实用可行。

Abstract: Optimizing spin-free sum-of-squares (SOS) energy bounds for electronic Hamiltonians leads to semidefinite programs whose expanded size grows rapidly with the orbital dimension. We derive an exact reformulation of an established spin-free SOS model as a convex quadratic problem over a single symmetric matrix constrained between zero and the identity. Explicit recovery maps connect feasible quadratic-program iterates to certificates in the original SOS formulation, and identify the Frank--Wolfe gap with the separation between recovered primal and dual objectives. We implement the quadratic action through GPU-accelerated Coulomb contractions and solve the reformulated problem using a cached implementation of fully corrective Frank--Wolfe, referred to here as the dimension-reduced simplicial method (DRSM) that caches operator products and optimizes convex-combination weights in a small corrective quadratic program. Numerical experiments cover 65 FCIDUMP models, molecular geometries, and regular hydrogen rings, including original-coordinate recovery checks on nine instances and comparisons with FISTA and a boundary-point SDP solver. On eight H100 GPUs, a cold-start calculation for C$_{720}$ with 10,080 spatial orbitals reaches a freshly recomputed Frank--Wolfe gap of $8.82\times10^{-9}$ hartree in 5.02 hours. Full-space hydrogen-ring calculations reach 10,000 orbitals, with cyclic reduction providing further computational savings. These results show how exact reformulation, data structure exploitation and reuse of Coulomb actions make optimization of the selected large-scale SOS relaxation practical.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Structure of correlations for the single-$j$ model using quantum information theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14460)
*Javier Faba,Vicente Martín,Luis Robledo*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 通过研究具有阿贝尔与非阿贝尔破缺对称性的单-$j$核模型，揭示两类对称性破缺对平均场态内关联的不同作用，并说明对称性恢复如何重新分配"虚假"关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界普遍认为量子相变中平均场基态自发破缺对称性可"在不丧失平均场结构的前提下吸收最大关联"，但关联如何被纳入平均场态、以及对称性恢复趋近精确基态时关联结构如何变化仍不清楚。

Method: 以单-$j$模型为对象（一个同时含阿贝尔与非阿贝尔破缺对称性的真实费米子核模型），分别考察两类对称性破缺对平均场态内关联的影响，并分析对称性恢复后关联在成对轨道与更复杂子系统间的重新分布。

Result: 两类对称性对平均场态内关联的作用差别显著；对称性恢复会将已有的"虚假"关联从成对轨道重新分配到更复杂的子系统。

Conclusion: 结果使"对称性破缺作为吸收最大关联机制"这一概念变得不清晰，表明该图像并非普遍成立。

Abstract: We say that a system undergoes a quantum phase transition when the mean-field ground state of a given Hamiltonian spontaneously breaks its symmetries depending on the Hamiltonian parameters. It is a widely accepted view that this phenomenon enables the mean-field state to absorb the maximum amount of correlations without losing its mean-field structure. However, it is still uncertain how those correlations are incorporated within the mean-field state and how the correlation structure changes when we get closer to the exact ground state by restoring the symmetries. In this work, we answer these questions by studying the single-$j$ model, which is a realistic fermionic nuclear model with abelian and non-abelian broken symmetries. We find that both types of symmetries have very different effects for the correlations within the mean-field state. This makes unclear the concept of the symmetry breaking process as a mechanism to absorb the maximum amount of correlations. Moreover, the symmetry restoration redistributes existing `fake' correlations between paired orbitals to more complex subsystems.

</details>

### [126] [Memory-gated transient anomalous transport of quasiparticles out of equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14261)
*Pranay Venkatesh,Tianchu Li,Srijan Bhattacharyya,Andrés Montoya-Castillo*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文以色散Holstein小极化子模型为代表，提出"带符号的浴记忆时间"统一描述瞬态反常输运：记忆时间的符号与大小决定输运偏离扩散的方向与强度，符号改变时输运在亚扩散与超扩散之间切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反常输运在凝聚态、材料科学与生物物理中普遍存在，但在远离平衡条件下，输运何时、以多大程度、朝哪个方向偏离扩散仍是未解的基本问题；现有研究缺乏对瞬态偏离的统一判据。

Method: 采用色散Holstein模型描述小极化子，作为一大类耗散系统的代表；通过调节耗散环境与载流子态密度之间的共振/非共振条件，分析浴记忆时间的符号变化，并在微观层面追踪极化子形成与相干极化子运动之间的竞争。

Result: 带符号的浴记忆时间同时决定瞬态偏离的方向和强度；当记忆时间为零时，扩散无需渐近延迟即刻出现；将环境从与载流子态密度共振调至非共振，记忆时间变号，输运由亚扩散转为超扩散。

Conclusion: 研究建立了基于记忆的瞬态反常输运统一描述，表明可通过环境调控控制非平衡准粒子流，为耗散体系中输运的预测与操控提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Anomalous transport is ubiquitous across condensed matter, materials science, and biophysics. Yet, in far-from-equilibrium settings, what determines when, how much, and in which direction transport departs from diffusion remains a fundamental open question. By adopting the dispersive Holstein model of small polarons as representative of a wide class of dissipative systems, we show that a signed bath memory time governs the direction and the strength of the transient departure, and that when it vanishes, diffusion emerges without an asymptotic delay. Tuning the dissipative environment from resonant to off-resonant with the carrier density of states, transport turns from subdiffusion to superdiffusion as the memory time changes sign. Microscopically, we trace this memory effect to a competition between polaron formation and coherent polaron motion. These findings establish a unified memory-based description of transient anomalous transport, offering a path to control nonequilibrium quasiparticle flow through environmental tuning.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Thermodynamic Formalism for Non-Invariant Probabilities: Entropy, Divergence, Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13904)
*Artur O. Lopes,Elismar R. Oliveira*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文引入"ni-熵" h_ni，将Kolmogorov-Sinai熵从σ-不变测度推广到全空间M上的Borel概率，并据此建立ni-压力、ni-KL散度及若干信息论 variational 原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶Ruelle算子 L_J^* 作为动力系统通道推广随机矩阵作用，但一般不保持σ-不变性，使得经典KS熵只在 M_σ 上有定义；需要在非不变测度上定义熵与信息论量以研究该通道的输出性质。

Method: 以基数为1的柱集上的期望值函数构造 ni-熵 h_ni(μ)，证明其凹性与上半连续性且限制在 M_σ 上等于KS熵；由其构造势函数的 ni-压力 P(A)；定义不要求绝对连续的 ni-KL散度 D_ni(ρ||ν)；研究最小输出熵、Donsker-Varadhan变分原理、h_ni的变分公式、散度界、信息投影及其与 P 的关系。

Result: h_ni 为 M 上的凹、上半连续泛函，是 Shannon 熵之外不同于且推广 KS 熵的量；在此基础上给出 ni-压力的 variational 刻画与 Donsker-Varadhan 型原理，得到散度的上下界及信息投影结论。

Conclusion: 在非不变测度框架下可自洽地建立熵—压力—散度的信息论体系，用以刻画对偶Ruelle算子通道的输出熵与投影行为，为符号动力系统中的通道容量类问题提供工具。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{M}$ and $\mathcal{M}_\sigma$ denote, respectively, the sets of Borel and $\sigma$-invariant probabilities on the uncountable set $\Omega=\{1,2,...,d\}^\mathbb{N}$. Given a Hölder Jacobian $J$, the dual Ruelle operator $\mu \in \mathcal{M} \mapsto \mathcal{L}_{J}^* (\mu)$ will play the role of a dynamical channel; generalizes the action of a stochastic matrix on probability vectors. Since $\mathcal{L}_{J}^*$ generally breaks $\sigma$-invariance, we introduce the \emph{ni-entropy} $h_{ni}(\mu)$ for non-invariant measures, employing the concept of the expected value function on cylinders of size one. $h_{ni}(\mu)$ differs from Shannon entropy but extends the Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy from $\mathcal{M}_\sigma$ to $\mathcal{M}$; $h_{ni}$ is concave and u.s.c. This leads us to the concept of ni-pressure $\mathcal{P}(A)$ of a potential $A:\Omega \to \mathbb{R}$. $\mu\in \mathcal{M}$ is not a density with respect to a dominating measure. We define an {\it ni-Kullback-Leibler divergence} $D_{ni}(\rho || \nu)$ not assuming absolute continuity. We investigate concepts of Information Theory such as Minimal Output Entropy, Donsker-Varadhan variational principle, a variational formula for $h_{ni}$, bounds for divergence, information projections, and their connections to the ni-pressure $\mathcal{P}$.

</details>

### [128] [Goldstone-Mediated Chiral Superconductivity in Self-Doped Electron Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12946)
*Shuchen Zhang,Zhihuan Dong,Zhaoyu Han,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究菱方石墨烯中电子晶体相边界附近自掺杂晶体的Goldstone声子，发现其可过屏蔽库仑斥力，从而驱动手性拓扑超导并产生未配对Majorana边缘模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验在菱方石墨烯中观察到超导性出现在电子晶体相附近，提示存在非常规配对机制；作者希望探究由电子声子（晶体集体模式）介导配对的可能性，特别是平凡与拓扑电子晶体相边界处自掺杂形成的小型Dirac费米海情形。

Method: 聚焦平凡与拓扑电子晶体相界附近，自掺杂产生Dirac型小费米海；用自洽方法计算自掺杂晶体（SDC）的Goldstone声子谱，进而导出费米面上巡游载流子间有效的电子-声子介导相互作用；在λ-jellium模型与简化的菱方石墨烯模型中检验结论，并提出非线性I-V信号作为实验判据。

Result: Goldstone声子可过屏蔽库仑斥力，导致手性拓扑超导态，具有未配对的Majorana边缘模；该结论在λ-jellium模型和简化菱方石墨烯模型中均稳健成立，并给出特征性非线性I-V信号作为可检验预测。

Conclusion: 研究提出一条非常规超导的新途径：电子关联与拓扑共同起关键作用，Goldstone声子介导的过屏蔽可超越库仑斥力形成手性拓扑超导，并可通过非线性输运信号验证。

Abstract: Motivated by the observation of superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene in the proximity of electronic crystalline phases, we study the possibility of pairing mediated by electronic phonons. We focus on the vicinity of the phase boundary between trivial and topological electron crystals, where self-doping generically gives rise to a small Fermi sea of carriers with Dirac dispersion. We compute the Goldstone phonon spectra of the self-doped crystal (SDC) using a self-consistent approach, and subsequently derive the effective electronic-phonon-mediated interaction between itinerant carriers at the Fermi surface. Remarkably, we find that the Coulomb repulsion can be overscreened by the Goldstone phonons, leading to a chiral topological superconductor with an unpaired Majorana edge mode. Our conclusions are robust across the $\lambda$-jellium model and a simplified model of rhombohedral graphene, and we propose characteristic non-linear I-V signals as a test of our prediction. More generally, our results outline an intriguing route for unconventional superconductivity where both electronic correlations and topology play key roles.

</details>


### [129] [Mimicking the zero-field Josephson diode effect with remanent magnetization and self-field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13157)
*Nathan Satchell,Arjun Sapkota,Pukar Sedai,Kurt Lorenzen,Juan Gomez*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 所谓"本征约瑟夫森二极管效应"的零场非互易信号，实际上可由偏置电流在电极中产生的自磁场与势垒剩磁这两个普通因素共同解释；可通过移动电流引线位置（改变自磁场方向）的直流测试予以区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带磁化势垒的约瑟夫森结在零场下呈现临界电流方向不对称，通常被归因于时间反演对称性破缺导致的本征约瑟夫森二极管效应。作者质疑这一归因，指出存在更普通的替代机制。

Method: 在十字型 Nb/Pd89Ni11/Nb 结中，分别研究偏置电流在电极中产生的自磁场与势垒面内剩磁对干涉图样的影响；并通过改变电流引线的接入臂（从而反转自磁场方向）进行对照实验，同时改变电流极性与外加场方向。

Result: 自磁场使两种电流极性下的干涉图样向相反方向平移；面内剩磁进一步平移图样，使不对称性出现在零场，表现为非易失二极管，不对称度 10–12%，极性由磁化方向决定。仅靠反转电流或磁场无法区分该"仿冒"效应与本征效应。

Conclusion: 移动电流引线至电极另一臂可反转自磁场二极管（有场与零场均如此），而本征二极管不受影响。这提供了一种简单的直流判据，用于电流垂直于膜面（CPP）几何下甄别约瑟夫森二极管。

Abstract: A Josephson junction whose critical current is larger in one direction than in the other is a diode. With a magnetized barrier, a junction can be a diode at zero applied field, and this is commonly attributed to an intrinsic Josephson diode effect, in which the magnetic barrier breaks time-reversal symmetry. Here we show that the same signatures arise from two ordinary ingredients: the magnetic field of the bias current in the electrodes and the remanent magnetization of the barrier. In cross-type Nb/Pd$_{89}$Ni$_{11}$/Nb junctions, the self-field displaces the interference patterns of the two current polarities in opposite directions. An in-plane remanent magnetization translates the pattern and brings the resulting asymmetry to zero field, where the junction mimics a nonvolatile diode with an asymmetry of 10 to 12% and a polarity set by the magnetization direction. Reversing the current or the field cannot distinguish this mimic from an intrinsic effect, but the wiring can: moving the current lead to the other arm of the electrode across the field reverses the self-field diode, in applied field and at zero field alike, whereas an intrinsic diode would be unchanged. This gives a simple dc test for Josephson diodes in the current-perpendicular-to-plane geometry.

</details>

### [130] [Stagewise and interpretable emergence of inside-outside computation in Transformer-based language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14221)
*Jack T. Parley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过合成上下文无关文法研究Transformer学习句法结构的动力学，解析出可解释的分阶段学习曲线，并揭示网络逐步形成"中间编码潜变量树、后半段精化解码"的编解码器结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"事后解剖"研究已表明训练完成的Transformer近似inside-outside等经典解析算法，但对其如何被学习到几乎缺乏理论刻画，作者希望打开这一理论缺口。

Method: 构建带有可变解析树拓扑的合成上下文无关文法，使各损失阶段可解析计算并与隐藏潜变量树变量对应；再通过表征探测（probing）观察深度方向与训练过程中的inside-outside计算涌现。

Result: 各损失阶段可解析计算且明确对应潜变量树表征，学习曲线完全可解释；训练早期仅用少数初始层的局部bigram/trigram信息，随后逐渐发展为编码器-解码器结构——最抽象的潜变量信息编码于网络中部，后半段网络逐层精化最终词元预测。

Conclusion: 该组织方式与真实LLM中的发现相呼应，且因数据统计可完全解析控制，为句法结构学习的理论进展提供了可控实验平台。

Abstract: Variability in syntactic structure and parsing ambiguity are fundamental aspects of natural language which modern LLMs have to learn to deal with proficiently. Previous "post-mortem" studies have shown that fully trained Transformers indeed approximate canonical parsing algorithms such as inside-outside, but little is established theoretically about how this is actually learned. Here, we make a first step towards addressing this by studying the learning dynamics in synthetic context-free grammars with variable parse tree topology. The stagewise learning curves are fully interpretable, in the sense that all of the loss stages a) can be computed analytically, and b) can be clearly associated to the representation of a hidden latent tree variable, which allows to lift ambiguity and utilize information from wider contexts. By probing representations, we visualize the emergence of inside-outside computation across depth and during training: while during early stages local (bigram/trigram) information is used and involves only a few initial layers, gradually the Transformer develops an encoder-decoder structure where the most abstract latent information is encoded in the middle, and the latter half of the network is used to progressively refine the final token prediction. This organization of computation is reminiscent of findings in real-world LLMs, and importantly applies here to a synthetic model which allows full analytical control of the data statistics, thus opening the door to theoretical progress.

</details>

### [131] [Lorentzian entropy, subgaussian matrix concentration, and sharp functional inequalities for all matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00626)
*Eren Ercan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了齐次Lorentzian律的完全熵收缩，由此得到亚高斯矩阵集中不等式与每个拟阵上的尖锐函数不等式，并据此解决若干极值函数与量子边际的刻画问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望把"从r个粒子中均匀保留ℓ个"这一自然采样操作在矩阵熵层面量化，并借此统一处理矩阵浓度、拟阵基图的函数不等式、熵极值分类以及全字段量子边际区域等问题。

Method: 证明齐次Lorentzian律的完全熵收缩：保留ℓ/r粒子使矩阵熵按最优普适因子ℓ/r收缩（任意有限维矩阵、正特征域、含重复标签）；建立保留矩阵方差与任意Hermitian偏移的矩阵Laplace比较；对单位边拟阵基图证明小集与Gross不等式、谱刻画与极值特征函数分类；对幂熵与多重仿射律做极值分类并判定精确量子边际与矩阵Hölder区域。

Result: 得到有界交换差Hermitian可观测量的亚高斯界、基和的Chernoff与Bernstein界；单位边拟阵基图的谱落在{0,2}∪[3,∞)，最优完全熵衰减率为4，解决Alaniz Pintos一阶猜想与Feder–Mihail游走等号问题；度≥2时矩阵熵极值唯一由其双粒子边际延拓，等号仅依赖归一化对律；1&lt;p&lt;2的幂熵极值被分类，给出仿射等号的四阶判据，保留层Gibbs重构具维数无关稳定性；Lorentzian生成多项式情形下确定各字段同时量子边际与矩阵Hölder区域，连通尖锐律在秩三以内为行列式型，更高秩存在稳定的非行列式例子。

Conclusion: 完全熵收缩是贯穿全文的核心工具，把拟阵的组合结构与矩阵熵、浓度和函数不等式联系起来；作者据此给出若干尖锐常数与等号刻画，并说明"Lorentzian性+熵收缩"可作为研究量子边际与极值问题的统一框架。

Abstract: We prove complete entropy contraction for homogeneous Lorentzian laws and derive subgaussian matrix concentration and sharp functional inequalities for every matroid. Uniform retention of $\ell$ out of $r$ particles contracts matrix entropy by the optimal universal factor $\ell/r$, in every finite matrix dimension and positive field, including repeated labels. The matrix Laplace comparison retains matrix variance and arbitrary Hermitian offsets. It gives subgaussian bounds for arbitrary Hermitian observables with bounded exchange differences, and Chernoff and Bernstein bounds for basis sums.
 Every unit-edge matroid basis graph satisfies the cube's small-set and Gross inequalities, with equality classified and optimal universal complete entropy-decay rate four. Its spectrum lies in $\{0,2\}\cup[3,\infty)$, with binary direct-factor contrasts spanning the eigenspace at two. We classify extremal down-up eigenfunctions, resolving Alaniz Pintos's degree-one conjecture and the equality problem for the Feder--Mihail walk.
 For degree at least two, every matrix entropy extremizer extends uniquely from its two-particle marginal. Equality depends only on the normalized pair law, with noncommutativity confined to mutually exclusive labels. We classify power-entropy extremizers for $1<p<2$ and give a fourth-order criterion for affine equality. Retained-level Gibbs reconstruction has sharp dimension-independent stability. For multiaffine laws whose generating polynomial and complement are Lorentzian, we determine the exact simultaneous all-field quantum marginal and matrix Hölder regions and classify the sharp half-marginal fields. Connected sharp laws are determinantal through rank three; stable nondeterminantal examples exist in every higher rank.

</details>

### [132] [Parametric Proof-Carrying Compilation for Quantum Error-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12985)
*John Zhuoyang Ye,Jens Palsberg*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出PC-QEC，一个同时以码距和校验子提取方案为参数的量子纠错验证编译框架。它覆盖Standard、Flag、Shor、Knill四种方案，对每个已证明码族中所有合法码距给出电路级距离保证。核心是通过码代数证明与容错Hoare证明组合成与码距无关的有限符号证书，并在Lean 4中机械化元理论与精确电路级距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错的电路级容错保证通常依赖逐码距、逐方案的人工或半自动分析，缺乏可组合、可验证且随码距可扩展的形式化框架；同时不同校验子提取方案（Standard/Flag/Shor/Knill）的容错特性差异大，缺少统一覆盖四类方案的编译级保证。

Method: PC-QEC采用"证明携带编译"：以码距与校验子提取方案为参数，在声明式故障与读出契约下建模电路级容错。故障模型允许每个操作量子比特操作数上的单线Pauli故障，统一捕获数据错误、读出错误及传播为多比特hook错误的辅助比特故障。对每个码族与方案，将码代数证明与容错感知的Hoare证明组合为有限符号证书，其规模与码距无关；元理论与Surface、HGP、Toric码族的精确电路级距离在Lean 4中机械化验证。

Result: 在5个码族×4种方案上给出族级保证。以旋转Surface码为例，单个Standard Scheme证书即可证明所有奇数d≥3的码距下界d，平均检查耗时214秒。Lean 4机械化覆盖元理论及典型Surface、超图乘积（HGP）与Toric码族的精确电路级距离。

Conclusion: PC-QEC证明可在单一有限证书中统一覆盖多方案、多码族的电路级距离保证，并具备与码距无关的可扩展性，为量子纠错编译器的形式化可信保证提供了可行范式。

Abstract: We present PC-QEC, a proof-carrying compilation framework for quantum error correction parameterized by both code distance and syndrome-extraction scheme. It covers all four schemes---Standard, Flag, Shor and Knill---with circuit-level distance guarantees for every legal code distance in each proved family under the declared fault and readout contracts. The circuit-level model permits single-wire Pauli faults at every operation's qubit operands, capturing data errors, readout errors and ancilla faults that propagate into multi-qubit hook errors. For each family and scheme, code-algebra proofs and fault-aware Hoare proofs compose into a finite symbolic certificate whose size is independent of code distance. We demonstrate family-wide guarantees for five code families across all four schemes. For rotated Surface code, one Standard Scheme certificate proves a distance lower bound $d$ for every odd $d\ge3$ and checks in 214\,s on average. Lean 4 mechanizes the metatheory and exact circuit-level distances for canonical Surface, hypergraph-product (HGP) and Toric code families.

</details>

### [133] [Everything in its right place: attention is a radio head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13146)
*Lluís Arola-Fernández,Aissam Khadraoui,Alex Arenas*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 提出"无线电头"（radio head）：把注意力重解为接收器与上下文振荡器的同步问题，用Kuramoto模型闭式求解，得到具有"锁定/静默"两相的注意力层，理论与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力是纯数字式计算：指数选择、除法归一化、位置编码定序，三个部件各自独立且缺乏物理直觉。作者希望为注意力的每个部件找到统一解释，并赋予归一化和"弃权"行为以机制基础。

Method: 将每个过去token建模为振荡器：状态承载内容、频率随其年龄漂移；当前token同时按内容（对准天线/查询）和位置（调节接收器频率）选择它们。对接收器求解Kuramoto同步问题，得到闭式注意力层，其行为由上下文决定的临界耦合分相：超临界时接收器锁定、权重归一如常；亚临界时保持静默、权重和小于1。

Result: 训练后的网络会同时利用两相来完成选择、弃权与计数；在理论预测之处出现失败；并能在远超训练长度的情况下继续"读取"上下文。

Conclusion: 注意力的各部件（内容选择、归一化、位置）统一在一个"无线电头"装置中：调谐到听什么、何时听，无相关内容时保持静默。为注意力提供了可解释的动力学解释与外推能力。

Abstract: Attention, the core of language models, is a digital calculation over tokens: an exponential selects them, a division normalizes and a positional code sets the order. We show that attention can instead emerge from the synchronization of a receiver with its context tokens. Each past token is an oscillator whose state carries its content and whose frequency drifts with its age. The present token selects them by content, aligning an antenna with its query, and by position, setting the frequency of a receiver with its dial. Solving Kuramoto's problem for this receiver yields an attention layer in closed form with two phases: above a critical coupling set by the context, the receiver locks and its weights normalize; below it, it stays silent and its weights sum to less than one. Trained networks use both phases to select, abstain and count, fail where the theory predicts and keep reading far beyond the training length. Each part of attention finds its place in one device, a radio head: it tunes in to what to hear and when, and stays silent when nothing relevant is on air.

</details>

### [134] [Universal Multichannel Coherence-Mediated Thermal Transport in Carbon Allotropes: Experimental Evidence and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14165)
*A. Jeżowski,A. I. Krivchikov,O. A. Korolyuk,V. V. Sagan,Y. V. Horbatenko,S. V. Cherednychenko,A. V. Dolbin,N. A. Vinnikov,O. Bezkrovnyi,D. Szewczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作在4.5–300 K测量了GO、trGO、GO-水合富勒烯复合物、膨胀石墨及EG-MWCNT复合物的热导率，发现均呈玻璃型温度依赖；传统"Peierls+单一热激活相干通道"双通道模型系统性失效，据此提出最多含三个Arrhenius型相干通道的多通道唯象模型，并揭示激活能层级近似满足E2≈4E1、E3≈(12–25)E1的普适规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构无序碳材料的热输运横跨宽温区，基于单一相干贡献的传统模型难以准确描述其热导行为，亟需建立能够统一刻画结构层级与热输运关系的唯象框架。

Method: 对密度、孔隙率、石墨化程度和微结构差异显著的多种无序碳材料（GO、trGO、GO-HyFn、EG、EG-MWCNT）在4.5–300 K区间进行热导率测量；检验含Peierls项与单一热激活相干项的双通道模型；进而构建以最多三个具有不同激活能的Arrhenius型通道作为相干贡献的广义多通道唯象模型，并与已发表的其他无序碳材料数据比对。

Result: 所有样品尽管在密度、孔隙率、石墨有序度和微结构上差异显著，均表现出玻璃型热导率温度依赖；传统双通道模型系统性无法复现实验曲线，而依结构状态需两或三个相干通道即可准确拟合。激活能呈现可重复的层级关系（E2≈4E1，E3≈(12–25)E1）；通道振幅随材料密度增大而增强，激活能却近乎不变，指示存在普适的振动能量尺度。该框架亦可一致描述文献中其他无序碳材料的数据。

Conclusion: 研究为多通道相干介导的热输运提供了实验证据，并建立起连接结构层级与无序碳固体热输运的统一唯象桥梁；激活能的普适层级暗示存在与密度无关的本质振动能量标度。

Abstract: Understanding thermal transport in structurally disordered carbon materials remains a fundamental challenge because conventional models based on a single coherence contribution cannot accurately describe heat conduction over a broad temperature range. In this work, the thermal conductivity of graphene oxide (GO), thermally reduced graphene oxide (trGO), GO-hydrated fullerene composites (GO-HyFn), expanded graphite (EG), and EG-multiwalled carbon nanotube (MWCNT) composites was measured between 4.5 and 300 K. Despite substantial differences in density, porosity, graphitic ordering, and microstructure, all materials exhibit glass-like temperature dependence of thermal conductivity.
 The experimental results demonstrate that the conventional two-channel model, comprising a Peierls contribution and a single thermally activated coherence term, systematically fails to reproduce the observed temperature dependence. To resolve this discrepancy, we propose a generalized multichannel phenomenological model in which the coherence contribution consists of up to three Arrhenius-type channels with distinct activation energies. Depending on the structural state, two or three coherence channels are required to accurately describe the thermal conductivity.
 A key finding is the reproducible hierarchy of activation energies across diverse carbon allotropes, following approximately (E2 ~ 4E1 and E3 ~ (12-25)E1). While the channel amplitudes increase with material density, the activation energies remain nearly invariant, indicating universal vibrational energy scales. The proposed framework also consistently describes published data for other disordered carbon materials. These results provide experimental evidence for multichannel coherence-mediated heat transport and establish a unified phenomenological bridge between structural hierarchy with thermal transport in disordered carbon solids.

</details>

### [135] [Tripartite nonlocal magic from reflected and negativity spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13071)
*Sriram Akella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 三体纯态在局域幺正意义下等价于稳定子态，当且仅当其边缘谱、反射谱与负性谱（模符号）均为平坦谱；由此用"反平坦度"量化三体非局域magic，并可经随机测量实验测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子态与magic的判别此前多集中于单体/两体情形，缺乏可计算且可实验测量的三体非局域magic判据；本文旨在给出三体纯态稳定子性的谱刻画及其量化诊断工具。

Method: 证明三体纯态局域幺正等价于稳定子态当且仅当边缘谱、反射谱、负性谱（计符号）同时平坦；以"反平坦度"（谱偏离平坦的程度）作为非局域magic度量；结合随机测量协议实现实验可测；在非阿贝尔量子double模型与随机张量网络中检验该判据的呈现条件。

Result: 非阿贝尔量子double中，该非局域magic仅当三分割的三重点位于plaquette上时出现；随机张量网络中，其出现当且仅当两个边界区域的纠缠楔相连。

Conclusion: 谱平坦性构成三体稳定子性的完整判据，"反平坦度"提供了可计算、可实验测量的三体非局域magic诊断量，并在拓扑序与全息纠缠结构中呈现清晰的几何对应。

Abstract: A tripartite pure state is local-unitary equivalent to a stabilizer state if and only if its marginal, reflected, and negativity spectra (up to signs) are flat. These conditions are quantified using anti-flatness, giving a diagnostic of tripartite nonlocal magic that is computable and experimentally measurable using randomized measurements. In non-abelian quantum doubles, it appears only when the triple points of the tripartition lie on plaquettes. In random tensor networks, it appears exactly when the entanglement wedge of two boundary regions connects.

</details>


### [136] [More physics at the entangling surface: boundary conditions and entanglement in 2d TQFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13078)
*Jackson R. Fliss,Alan Rios Fukelman,Stathis Vitouladitis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究二维拓扑量子场论（TQFT）中的纠缠：指出纠缠度量依赖于实现希尔伯特空间因子化的切割映射，其必须是等距的；纯粹的路径积分切割映射一般并非等距，需用合适的边缘算符修饰以构造等距切割映射，从而对任意态、任意二分割和任意边界条件计算纠缠，并由此得到一系列物理结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TQFT 中纠缠的定义存在概念性模糊：纠缠度量对实现希尔伯特空间因子化的切割映射敏感，而最自然的、仅基于受边界条件约束的路径积分的切割映射并不保持范数。此前缺乏对二维 TQFT 纠缠定义的系统处理，尤其缺乏在任意态、任意二分割和任意边界条件下定义良好纠缠的方案。

Method: 提出以等距性（保持所有态范数）作为良好定义纠缠的判据；证明基于边界条件路径积分的朴素切割映射对一般 TQFT 非等距；对任意二维 TQFT，通过在路径积分上"穿戴"（dress）适当的边缘算符来构造等距切割映射，并据此计算任意态、任意二分割、任意边界条件下的纠缠量；此外利用单一态的多区间 Rényi 熵重构任意亏格配分函数，并研究含（可能非可逆）对称性理论中对称基态与真空的纠缠量与纠缠不对称性。

Result: 得到尽管所有边界条件均有能隙，仍出现类似 Affleck-Ludwig 的非整数"基态简并度"；可由单一态的多区间 Rényi 熵重构 TQFT 在任意亏格的配分函数；对含（可能非可逆）对称性的理论，证明对称基态的纠缠熵可在无需了解真空的情况下区分具有相同对称性的不同能隙相，而真空的纠缠不对称性可熵性地探测对称性破缺。工作以有限群规范理论、Haagerup 能隙相及伴随 QCD₂ 的红外相为例进行了说明。

Conclusion: 纠缠在二维 TQFT 中可被系统、良定义地刻画，关键在于等距切割映射的构造；该框架统一地给出纠缠熵、非整数基态简并、由纠缠重构配分函数以及纠缠对能隙相与对称性破缺的判别能力，并广泛适用于有限群规范理论、Haagerup 相与伴随 QCD₂ 红外相等非平凡理论。

Abstract: We present a systematic treatment of entanglement in two-dimensional topological quantum field theories (TQFTs). Our approach clarifies the sensitivity of entanglement measures to the cutting maps that implement Hilbert space factorization. Such maps embed the TQFT Hilbert space into a description with potentially more degrees of freedom, and for entanglement to be well defined they must be isometric, i.e., they must preserve the norms of all states. We show that the naïve cutting map based solely upon path integration subject to boundary conditions is not isometric for generic TQFTs. For any 2d TQFT, we give a prescription to construct isometric cutting maps by dressing path integration with suitable edge operators. This allows us to compute entanglement for any state, any bipartition, and any boundary condition. As a consequence, we find a non-integer "ground-state degeneracy" à la Affleck-Ludwig, despite all boundary conditions being gapped. We further show how to reconstruct the partition function of the TQFT at arbitrary genus from the multi-interval Rényi entropies of a single state. For theories with (possibly non-invertible) symmetries, we show that the entanglement entropy of the symmetric ground state can distinguish gapped phases with the same symmetry, without any further knowledge of their vacua, while the entanglement asymmetry of the vacua entropically detects symmetry breaking. We illustrate our formalism with a range of applications, from finite gauge theories to Haagerup gapped phases and infrared phases of adjoint QCD$_2$.

</details>


### [137] [Holographic Drag Force and the Entanglement Membrane: Geometric Reconstruction and Information Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13088)
*Dmitry S. Ageev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示运动重夸克的耗散阻力与纠缠膜张力之间存在精确、可逆的几何对应：阻力非线性展开的各阶系数依次编码膜的蝴蝶速度、近视界几何与标量 pole-skipping 谱数据；反之，完整的信息速度曲线可反演内部约化几何，配合随机动量展宽可重建外部度规（至整体标度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息对偶的核心问题是边界可观测如何编码并重建引力对偶的几何。阻力（耗散）与纠缠膜（量子信息传播）通常被视为强耦合动力学中两个独立方面，本文旨在探明二者是否共享同一几何结构，并借此统一理解不同边界观测量所携带的几何信息。

Method: 在全息框架下并列构造有限速度运动夸克的拖曳阻力与纠缠膜的膜张力，证明二者保持相同的底层几何结构并建立可逆变换；将阻力的有限速度非线性展开（逐阶系数）与蝴蝶速度、近视界局部几何、标量 pole-skipping 谱数据逐级对应；再借助随机动量展宽引入额外径向信息，用于度规重建。

Result: 阻力与膜张力之间存在可逆的精确映射；非线性领头修正确定蝴蝶速度，高阶项逐次探测视界附近膜的局部几何；该层级与标量 pole-skipping 建立全阶关系；五次阻力系数决定信息速度对初始纠缠的领头依赖；完整信息速度曲线反演物理内部膜分支上的约化体几何，与阻力的外部重建互补；随机动量展宽提供重建完整外部度规（至整体标度）所需的附加径向信息。

Conclusion: 耗散、纠缠动力学与谱性质以互补方式提供对全息几何的访问，构成理解不同边界观测量所编码几何信息的统一框架，并为量子信息恢复等问题提供新约束与重建途径。

Abstract: A central question in holography is how different boundary observables encode and reconstruct the geometry of the gravitational dual. In this work, we uncover an exact geometric correspondence between two seemingly distinct aspects of strongly coupled dynamics: the dissipative drag force experienced by a moving heavy particle(quark) and the entanglement membrane governing the propagation of quantum information. We show that both constructions retain the same underlying structure of the geometry, leading to an invertible transformation between the finite-velocity drag force and the geometric membrane tension. This correspondence reveals that nonlinear momentum loss encodes detailed information about entanglement dynamics: the leading nonlinear correction determines the butterfly velocity, while higher-order terms successively probe the local geometry of the membrane near the horizon. We further connect this hierarchy to scalar pole-skipping, establishing an all-order relation between nonlinear dissipation and near-horizon spectral data. The shared geometric structure also has implications for quantum information recovery, with the quintic drag coefficient determining the leading dependence of information velocity on initial entanglement. Conversely, the complete information-velocity curve provides a reconstruction of the reduced bulk geometry along the physical interior membrane branch, complementing the exterior reconstruction from drag. We show that stochastic momentum broadening supplies the additional radial information needed to reconstruct the complete exterior metric up to an overall scale. Our results thus reveal how dissipation, entanglement dynamics, and spectral properties provide complementary access to holographic geometry, establishing a unified framework for understanding the geometric information encoded in distinct boundary observables.

</details>


### [138] [Categorical RMT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13093)
*Chuanxin Cui,Kristan Jensen,Sridip Pal,Moshe Rozali*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出"范畴随机矩阵理论"(categorical RMT)，将随机矩阵理论推广到具有普通或非可逆对称性的环上一维局域量子系统：由对称缺陷定义的扭曲Hilbert空间及管代数的不可约表示划分对称扇区，对称性固定精确简并与扇区间的等同关系，其余部分由普通RMT描述，不相关块视为统计独立。在混沌Fibonacci任意子链与S₃链中验证了该框架，并推广至1+1维CFT固定自旋的primary态及全息CFT中由SymTFT或Chern–Simons理论修饰的环面虫洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通随机矩阵理论只能逐个对称扇区地描述混沌系统的能级统计，无法处理带非可逆对称性的系统：不同扭曲的Hilbert空间之间可共享同一扇区（因而共享部分谱），却不存在任何可逆对称性将它们联系起来，超出普通RMT的描述范围。

Method: 对环上的局域量子系统，用对称缺陷定义扭曲Hilbert空间，以管代数的不可约表示作为对称扇区；假设对称性决定扇区间的精确简并与等同，其余能级由普通RMT描述，不相关块统计独立。在混沌Fibonacci任意子链中检验，并在混沌S₃链中做一致性验证；进一步推广到1+1维CFT固定自旋的primary态（模协变性要求额外关联），以及连续对称性下用Sugawara构造与谱流分离中性谱。

Result: 在混沌Fibonacci任意子链中证实了非可逆对称性导致的跨扭曲扇区谱共享；框架在混沌S₃链中得到检验。CFT情形下，中性谱即使在全玻色理论中也可呈辛型统计；全息CFT中，由SymTFT或Chern–Simons理论修饰的环面虫洞实现了所预言的ramp及其模完备化。

Conclusion: 范畴RMT为含普通与非可逆对称性的局域量子系统提供了统一的能级统计框架，超越了普通RMT的逐扇区描述，并在格点模型、CFT及全息虫洞计算中得到一致支持。

Abstract: Random matrix theory (RMT) describes the level statistics of chaotic systems one symmetry sector at a time. We propose its analogue for local quantum systems on a circle with ordinary or non-invertible symmetries. Symmetry defects define twisted Hilbert spaces, and the symmetry sectors are irreducible representations of the tube algebra, which acts within and between these spaces. Under our proposal, the symmetry fixes exact degeneracies and identifications among sectors, and ordinary RMT describes the rest, with unrelated blocks treated as statistically independent. We call this categorical RMT. For a non-invertible symmetry it goes beyond ordinary RMT, since Hilbert spaces with different twists can share a sector, and so part of their spectrum, though no invertible symmetry relates them.
 We confirm this in a chaotic Fibonacci anyon chain, and check the framework in chaotic $S_3$ chains. We extend categorical RMT to primary states at fixed spin in 1+1-dimensional CFTs, where modular covariance requires additional correlations. For continuous symmetries, the Sugawara construction and spectral flow isolate a neutral spectrum, which can have symplectic statistics even in a bosonic theory. In holographic CFTs, torus wormholes decorated by the SymTFT or by Chern--Simons theory realize the predicted ramp and its modular completion.

</details>


### [139] [BPS Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13097)
*Yiming Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 将大N矩阵bootstrap技术用于约束对偶于经典几何的重BPS态中简单算子的期望值，在弱耦合N=4 SU(N) SYM中研究1/2、1/4、1/8-BPS扇区及1/16-BPS的BMN截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重BPS态对偶于经典几何（无视界几何或黑洞），其期望值受BPS条件约束；对无视界几何，其模空间应给出bootstrap中的大片允许区域，而对黑洞应收敛出"岛"。希望通过矩阵bootstrap系统区分两类几何，并检验bootstrap的有效性。

Method: 在大N、弱耦合下运用大N矩阵bootstrap，约束标度维为O(N^2)的BPS态中简单算子的期望值；分别在1/2、1/4、1/8-BPS扇区以及1/16-BPS扇区的BMN截断中进行数值分析，并与大电荷极限下的本征值描述比较。

Result: 1/2、1/4、1/8-BPS扇区的bootstrap给出大片允许区域，与大电荷极限下这些态的本征值描述一致；1/16-BPS的BMN截断给出初步结果，其中同时包含对偶于无视界几何与黑洞的态。

Conclusion: 矩阵bootstrap可作为刻画重BPS态及其几何对偶的有效工具，但要在bootstrap中单独隔离出黑洞态仍是一个挑战，尚待进一步研究。

Abstract: We apply the large $N$ matrix bootstrap technique to constrain expectation values of simple operators in heavy BPS states that are dual to classical geometries. For states dual to horizonless geometries, their explicit moduli give rise to large allowable regions in the bootstrap, while for black holes, a converging bootstrap should find islands. We explore this method in $\mathcal{N}=4$ $SU(N)$ super Yang-Mills theory at large $N$, working at weak coupling and focusing on BPS states with scaling dimensions of order $N^2$. For the $1/2$, $1/4$, and $1/8$-BPS sectors, the bootstrap finds large regions, in agreement with an eigenvalue description of such states in the limit of large charges. We also present preliminary results for a BMN truncation of the $1/16$-BPS sector, which contains states dual to both horizonless geometries and black holes. Isolating the black hole states in the bootstrap remains a challenge.

</details>


### [140] [Landau Theory of Black-Brane Phase Transitions from Kasner Singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14075)
*Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用黑洞内部几何构造连续黑膜相变的Landau理论：正常相终止于Reissner–Nordström柯西视界，标量化相终止于Kasner奇点；以内区倒Kasner标量速度为序参量，其Landau势系数完全由终端奇点确定，并由Iyer–Wald第一定律与边界Landau理论联系起来，成功预言临界标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续黑膜/标量化相变通常用边界量或数值方法研究，缺乏从黑洞内部几何出发的解析理解；作者希望建立一套基于内区几何的Landau理论，用终端奇点信息直接刻画相变并解释临界行为。

Method: 构造基于黑洞内部几何的Landau理论：比较正常相（RN柯西视界）与标量化相（Kasner奇点）两个终端；引入守恒径向荷，从任一端点确定巨势以直接比较两相；临界点上解析证明守恒径向荷的二次修正为零；以内区倒Kasner标量速度为序参量构造Landau势，其系数由终端奇点决定；再借助Iyer–Wald第一定律把该势与常规边界Landau理论对应并固定四次项系数；最后对两个不同标量自相互作用模型进行检验。

Result: 解析证明临界点处守恒径向荷的二次修正消失；所得Landau理论对两个不同标量自相互作用模型均预言了Kasner序参量的临界标度以及两相巨势差，并与完全backreacted数值解一致。

Conclusion: 黑膜连续相变可由终端奇点几何完全确定，内区倒Kasner速度是有效序参量；通过Iyer–Wald第一定律与边界Landau理论自洽衔接，表明内部几何为全息相变提供了等价且解析可控的描述。

Abstract: We develop a Landau theory of a continuous black-brane phase transition using the geometry of the black-hole interior. The normal phase has a Reissner--Nordström Cauchy horizon, whereas the scalarized phase ends at a Kasner singularity. A conserved radial charge determines the grand potential from either endpoint and permits a direct phase comparison. At the critical point, we show analytically that the quadratic correction to the conserved radial charge vanishes. Using the inverse Kasner scalar velocity as an interior order parameter, we then construct a Landau potential whose coefficients are determined entirely from the terminal singularity. The Iyer--Wald first law relates this potential to the conventional boundary Landau theory and fixes its quartic coefficient. For two models with different scalar self-interactions, the resulting theory predicts the critical scaling of the Kasner order parameter and the grand-potential difference between the phases, in agreement with fully backreacted numerical solutions.

</details>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Inverse Scattering & Isospectrality in (Analog) Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13871)
*Christian F. Schmidt,Stefan Floerchinger*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将宇宙学粒子产生映射为一维量子散射：标度因子对应零能共振、宇宙奇点对应束缚态。发现标度因子反演（膨胀↔收缩）的等谱性（粒子数相同、相位差π）。给定振幅与相位，正则渐近静态宇宙的历史重建唯一；解析与数值验证恢复logistic膨胀历史精度优于0.02%，并量化了动量分辨与测量误差下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学粒子产生（动力学时空激发场量子）是CMB原初扰动的根源，并已在超冷原子模拟器中被实现。本文提出其逆问题：能否从激发谱重构产生它的宇宙学历史，且重构是否唯一？

Method: 建立粒子产生与一维量子散射的映射：膨胀历史充当散射势景观，产生谱对应反射波与透射波。在此框架下讨论标度因子的零能共振地位（散射理论的例外情形）、奇点与束缚态的对应，以及由散射连续谱支配的正则宇宙；并利用解析可解散射景观与数值方法求解逆问题。

Result: 揭示散射理论与宇宙学的丰富互作：正则宇宙完全由散射连续谱支配。发现等谱性——膨胀与收缩历史给出相同粒子数，仅相差π相位。同时给定粒子数（或振幅）与相位时，正则渐近静态宇宙的重构唯一；数值上以优于0.02%的精度恢复常见logistic膨胀历史，并给出对动量空间分辨率与测量不确定性的稳健性定量结果。

Conclusion: 工作建立了可观测功率谱与其所产生宇宙历史之间的直接联系，为早期宇宙情景的分类及暴胀替代方案的构建提供了新视角。

Abstract: Cosmological particle production, the excitation of field quanta by a dynamical spacetime, underlies the primordial perturbations observed in the cosmic microwave background and has been realized in analog quantum simulators with ultra-cold atoms. We ask the inverse question: given the excitation spectrum, can the cosmological history that produced it be reconstructed, and is that reconstruction unique? We approach this problem through a mapping of particle production to one-dimensional quantum scattering, in which the expansion history plays the role of a scattering landscape and the produced spectrum the role of reflected and transmitted waves. This mapping reveals a rich interplay between scattering theory and cosmology. The scale factor enters as a zero-energy resonance, an exceptional case of scattering theory, while cosmic singularities correspond to bound states, so that regular cosmologies are governed entirely by the scattering continuum. We find an interesting isospectrality: histories related by an inversion of the scale factor (expansion <-> contraction) yield identical particle numbers, differing only by a pi-shift in phase. Given both the particle number (or alternatively the amplitude) and phase of the cosmological power spectrum, the reconstruction is unique for regular, asymptotically static cosmologies. We demonstrate the reconstruction both analytically, for exactly solvable scattering landscapes, and numerically, recovering common logistic expansion histories to better than 0.02%. We further quantify its robustness to the momentum-space resolution and measurement uncertainties characteristic of analog quantum simulation. This work establishes a direct link between the observable power spectrum and the cosmological history that produced it, offering a new perspective on the classification of early-universe scenarios and the construction of alternatives to inflation.

</details>

### [142] [An Idealized Model of Buoyant Gas Spreading and Silt Release beneath Rough Cave Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12895)
*Sandy Hardian Susanto Herho,Dasapta Erwin Irawan,Rusmawan Suwarman,Deny Juanda Puradimaja*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立理想化模型刻画洞潜呼出气泡在自仿射粗糙顶板下形成水池、溢出、合并并剥离泥沙的过程，结果与独立凹陷填充算法精确一致、守恒气体体积并重现侵入渗流指数，给出由顶板起伏与倾角决定的水池尺度及能见度衰减规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放回路潜水员呼出的气体在水下洞穴顶部聚集、沿岩面迁移并剥离松散淤泥，形成洞潜界称为"percolation"的落泥现象，显著降低能见度与安全性；但该过程缺乏定量描述与尺度规律。

Method: 将自仿射粗糙顶板下的浮力气体视为准静态水池序列：注满至溢出点—释放气体爬升至下一高点—水位相会处合并；移动接触线剥离淤泥，剥离泥沙按粒径谱沉降。数值实现与独立凹陷填充算法对比验证，并与毛细极限下的侵入渗流对照。

Result: 计算与独立凹陷填充算法完全一致、气体体积守恒至舍入误差，并重现侵入渗流的已知标度指数；顶板起伏与倾角之比定义组织气体聚集的水池尺度；单源气体覆盖面积随供气体积呈亚线性增长（符合自仿射几何预测）；水池尺度处的截断是"软"的，陡顶板上可存在大于该尺度的大水池。参考通道中，队伍领头潜水员释放的泥沙远多于其后者，且整队释放量远小于各成员单独行动之和；该顺序在所有剥离效率与顶板几何下成立。队伍通过后眼高处朝出口方向的能见度仍持续下降一小时以上。

Conclusion: 模型揭示了气体聚集尺度、覆盖面积标度与泥沙释放的非可加性，可用于情景间比较；但因绝对泥沙质量与视距依赖洞穴中未测量的参数，结果宜作为标度关系与相对比较而非绝对预测。

Abstract: Gas exhaled by open-circuit divers collects under the ceilings of submerged caves, migrates along the rock, and dislodges loose silt that then falls through the passage, a process that cave divers call percolation. This study develops an idealized model of the process. Buoyant gas under a self-affine rough ceiling is treated as a quasi-static sequence of pools that fill to their spill points, release gas that climbs to the next high point, and merge where their levels meet. Moving contact lines strip silt from the rock, and the released silt settles as a spectrum of grain sizes that reduces the distance at which a diver can see. The computation agrees exactly with an independent depression-filling algorithm, conserves gas volume to round-off, and reproduces the known exponents of invasion percolation in its capillary limit. A balance between the relief of the ceiling and its dip defines a pool scale that organizes where gas collects, and the area covered by gas from a single source grows more slowly than the volume supplied, as predicted by the self-affine geometry. The cutoff at the pool scale is soft, so large pools persist on steeper ceilings than the scale alone would suggest. In a reference passage, the lead diver of a team releases far more silt than the diver behind, and a team releases far less than the sum of its members acting alone, because the lead diver fills the pools and strips the paths that later divers reuse. This ordering holds across every detachment efficiency and ceiling geometry tested. Visibility toward the exit at eye level continues to decline for an hour or more after a team has passed, as fine silt descends from a nearly opaque layer beneath the ceiling. Absolute silt masses and sighting ranges depend on quantities that have not been measured in caves, and the results are best read as scaling relations and comparisons between scenarios.

</details>

### [143] [Quantum-walk transport oracles and amplitude estimation of a scalar range statistic in proton therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13475)
*Kwangzoo Chung*

Main category: physics.med-ph

TL;DR: 本文为质子输运构建离散时间量子行走 oracle，并据此用 MLQAE 估计"穿透超越远端深度阈值的概率"这一标量统计量，量子加速成立但资源成本尚不足以在临床规模上实现壁钟时间优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAE 理论上可将蒙特卡洛粒子输运的采样成本二次降低，但阻碍其进入放疗物理的有两点：缺乏对带电粒子输运的高效酉编码，以及 QAE 单标量输出与逐体素剂量图结构之间的不匹配。作者同时处理这两个问题。

Method: 构造质子离散时间量子行走输运 oracle：用能量依赖的 coin 编码 stopping power 的连续慢化近似，能量寄存器由该 coin 驱动，使随机能量损失与射程歧离由线路动力学自然涌现；oracle 酉，每步一个新鲜 coin 比特，门数随能级数线性增长。在此 oracle 上把远端深度穿透概率表述为可直接标记的振幅，并用带 Grover 迭代与有限次测量的最大似然振幅估计（MLQAE）进行估计。另做三方面资源分析：输出结构、摊销、含纠错的壁钟时间。

Result: oracle 给出 Bragg 型深度沉积剖面，与相同规则的经典 MC 偏差在 0.27% 以内；Grover 放大在 k≤32 时与 sin²((2k+1)θ) 相符至机器精度。相同预算下 MLQAE 误差随 N^{-0.93} 下降，采样为 N^{-0.49}，最大预算处误差小 4.8 倍。资源分析显示：全剂量图在任何硬件下都结构性地不利；标量统计量在临床尺度也未达到壁钟时间交叉点——oracle 每次调用约 1.0×10^6 逻辑门（合成旋转后 3.5×10^7），主要来自多路复用 coin，为允许预算的 160 至 5400 倍。

Conclusion: 量子行走 oracle 能高保真复现经典输运并实现理论上的二次采样加速，但当前门成本使临床规模下的实际时间收益无法兑现；作者量化了所需约简倍数并定位了主导成本项（多路复用 coin）作为后续优化目标。

Abstract: Quantum amplitude estimation (QAE) promises a quadratic reduction in the sampling cost of Monte Carlo (MC) particle transport, but two obstacles have kept it from radiotherapy physics: the lack of an efficient unitary encoding of charged-particle transport, and the mismatch between QAE's single-scalar output and the per-voxel structure of dose calculation. We address both. We construct a discrete-time quantum-walk transport oracle for protons in which an energy register is driven by an energy-dependent coin encoding a continuous-slowing-down surrogate of the stopping power, so that stochastic energy loss and range straggling follow from the circuit dynamics. The oracle is unitary, with one fresh coin qubit per step, and its gate count grows linearly in the number of energy levels. On it we formulate a scalar range statistic, the probability that a proton penetrates beyond a distal depth threshold, as a directly marked amplitude, and estimate it by maximum-likelihood amplitude estimation (MLQAE) with Grover iterations and finite shots. The oracle reproduces a Bragg-shaped depth-deposit profile agreeing with a classical MC of identical rules to within 0.27%, and Grover amplification matches $\sin^2((2k+1)\theta)$ to machine precision up to $k=32$. At matched budget the MLQAE error falls as $N^{-0.93}$ against $N^{-0.49}$ for sampling, 4.8 times smaller at the largest budget. A three-gate resource analysis (output structure, amortization, error-corrected wall-clock) finds full dose maps structurally unfavourable irrespective of hardware, and the scalar statistic short of a wall-clock crossover at clinical scale: the oracle's derived cost of $1.0 \times 10^6$ logical gates per call ($3.5 \times 10^7$ with rotation synthesis), dominated by the multiplexed coin, is 160 to 5400 times the permitted budget. We report the reduction that would be required and identify the term responsible for it.

</details>

### [144] [Assessing the expressive power of a gauge-invariant PEPS ansatz across a topological phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13852)
*Etsuko Itou,Tsuyoshi Okubo*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 将GGFPEPS的计算重构为Grassmann张量网络，用HOTRG/CTMRG确定性收缩，证明仅8个复变分参数即可精确描述纯Z2规范理论（能量、拓扑纠缠熵、临界耦合），且转捩点附近态超出plaquette乘积族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前GGFPEPS拟设仅用变分蒙特卡洛(VMC)研究，其统计误差难以精确确定变分参数，因而无法准确考察拟设的表达能力。

Method: 把GGFPEPS计算重写为Grassmann张量网络，实现全网络确定性收缩；引入高阶张量重整化群(HOTRG)计算至L=34的大体积，并用角转移矩阵重整化群(CTMRG)处理无限大体积。

Result: 仅含8个复变分参数的拟设能准确描述纯Z2规范理论，包括拓扑相变区域，能量与拓扑纠缠熵均吻合；由磁化率峰值得到的临界耦合与连续时间量子蒙特卡洛结果定量一致；在转捩点附近，该拟设给出的是plaquette乘积族之外的态。

Conclusion: Grassmann张量网络+HOTRG/CTMRG为GGFPEPS提供了无统计误差的精确评估手段，证实该拟设以极少参数即可准确刻画(2+1)维Z2规范理论及其拓扑相变，并揭示其表达能力超越简单插值的plaquette乘积族。

Abstract: We investigate the expressive power of the gauged Gaussian fermionic projected entangled pair state (GGFPEPS) ansatz, which is a manifestly gauge-invariant tensor network ansatz for $(2+1)$-dimensional lattice gauge theories. In the GGFPEPS ansatz, the gauge field on each link is represented by coupling its configuration to the fermion number of virtual fermions placed at both ends of the link. This ansatz has been studied by variational Monte Carlo (VMC) methods in previous work~\cite{Emonts:2022yom}. However, the statistical error of VMC makes a precise determination of the variational parameters difficult, so that the expressive power of the ansatz could not be examined accurately. In this work, we reformulate the computation as a Grassmann tensor network (TN), in which the whole network is contracted deterministically, and introduce the higher-order tensor renormalization group (HOTRG), which allows us to perform calculations in large volumes up to $L=34$. We also evaluate the infinite volume system using the corner transfer matrix renormalization group (CTMRG). We find that the ansatz with only eight complex variational parameters accurately describes the pure $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory including the region of the topological phase transition, not only in the energy but also in the topological entanglement entropy. The critical coupling obtained from the peaks of their susceptibilities agrees quantitatively with the one predicted by continuous-time quantum Monte Carlo simulations~\cite{wu2012phase}. Furthermore, near the transition point, we show that the ansatz represents a state outside the plaquette product family, which is a one-parameter family of states assumed in the construction of the ansatz as a simple interpolation between the strong and weak coupling limits.

</details>


### [145] [Lattice Hamiltonians for Chiral Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13886)
*Dorota M. Grabowska*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 提出一种定义在板（slab）边界上的手征规范理论格点哈密顿量：采用规范固定形式，边界场即物理模，Gauss定律精确成立；体规范场由边界场与整数拓扑荷标号决定，规范反常条件转化为边界能否独立满足Gauss定律的问题，为量子线路构造提供出发点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点上直接构造手征规范理论长期受制于费米子倍增与规范反常问题。作者希望通过"体-边界"（slab/domain-wall）结构，把手征费米子置于边界、让体充当介电介质存储拓扑荷，从而在规范固定框架下规避反常障碍并得到可用于量子模拟的严格定义哈密顿量。

Method: 构造 slab 几何下的格点哈密顿量；采用规范固定，使规范变量仅为边界场的物理模，Gauss定律精确满足；体规范场由边界场加一个整数标号确定，该标号对应边界产生、体以局域瞬子形式存储的拓扑荷；体作为介电介质处理，将两个规范反常条件重述为边界能否自行满足Gauss定律；对背景规范场分析不同体电荷单元扇区在Pauli-Villars减除后的简并性及镜像费米子解耦；并论证边界理论不存在真空角。

Result: 在背景规范场下，相差一个体电荷单位的各扇区经Pauli-Villars减除后是简并的，镜像费米子解耦；边界理论无真空角；哈密顿量每一项均可由边界规范变量、整数标号与费米子模表示。

Conclusion: 该工作给出一个规范固定、Gauss定律精确成立的手征规范理论格点哈密顿量表述，将反常一致性条件归结为边界Gauss定律的可满足性，并因所有项均由有限显式变量构成而成为量子线路构造的合适起点。

Abstract: We present a lattice Hamiltonian for chiral gauge theories on the boundary of a slab. The formulation is gauge-fixed, so the gauge variables are the physical modes of the boundary field, and Gauss's law holds exactly. The bulk gauge field is determined by the boundary field and an integer label, which is the topological charge that the boundary generates and the bulk stores as a localized instanton. The bulk enters as a dielectric, and both gauge anomaly conditions become the question of whether the boundary satisfies Gauss's law on its own. For background gauge fields, we show that sectors that differ by a unit of bulk charge are degenerate after Pauli-Villars subtraction and the mirror fermions decouple. The boundary theory has no vacuum angle. Every term in the Hamiltonian is defined in terms of the boundary gauge variables, the integer label and the fermion modes, a starting point for quantum circuit construction.

</details>
